Do AI Agents Know When a Task Is Simple? Toward Complexity-Aware Reasoning and Execution
Authors: Junjie Yin, Xinyu Feng
First: 2026-07-14T17:59:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:59:31+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 8 figures, 8 tables. Code and benchmark: https://github.com/eejyin/Do-AI-Agents-Know-When-a-Task-Is-Simple-Toward-Complexity-Aware-Reasoning-and-Execution
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly automate multi-step engineering and informatics workflows, yet they rarely ask how much effort a task actually requires. They often follow a maximum-context-first strategy--re-reading files and dependencies they have already seen--turning a one-line edit into a small code-base audit. We argue the missing capability is task-aware execution-scope estimation: judging a task's difficulty, the information it truly needs, and the shortest reliable path before committing budget. We formalize minimum-sufficient execution and the Agent Cognitive Redundancy Ratio (ACRR), and propose E3 (Estimate, Execute, Expand): the agent estimates an initial operating point, executes a minimum viable path, and expands scope only when verification fails. On MSE-Bench--a deterministic benchmark of 121 edits in a capability-controlled simulator--E3 matches the strongest baseline's 100% success while cutting cost by 85%, tokens by 91%, and inspected files by 92%, and further beats a strong adaptive retrieval baseline by 16%; the gains survive held-out instruction wording and essentially every cost weighting. A companion real-model harness (LLM-Case) corroborates the effect on a live gpt-4o agent editing a real open-source library, with every candidate patch graded by actually running the project's real pytest suite against a measured oracle: the over-reading is milder but real, and E3 is the leanest and fastest policy at comparable task success--its one shortfall a provider rate-limit, not a wrong edit. We frame this as a controlled probe of execution redundancy, not a measurement of any deployed agent, and position task-aware execution as a step toward engineering-grounded AI (EGAI)--agents whose effort is anchored in the engineering reality of the task. We release the framework and benchmark.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AI代理知道任务何时简单吗？迈向具备复杂性感知的推理与执行
大型语言模型（LLM）代理越来越多地自动化多步骤的工程与信息学工作流，但它们很少评估任务实际所需的努力。它们通常采用最大上下文优先策略——重读已见过的文件和依赖项，将一行编辑演变为小型代码库审计。我们认为缺失的能力是任务感知的执行范围估算：判断任务难度、其真正所需信息及最短可靠路径，再决定是否投入资源。我们形式化了最小充分执行与代理认知冗余比（ACRR），并提出E3（估算、执行、扩展）：代理先估算初始操作点，执行最小可行路径，仅当验证失败时才扩展范围。在MSE-Bench——一个包含121次编辑、在能力受控模拟器中的确定性基准测试中，E3在成功率与最强基线持平（100%）的同时，成本降低85%，token减少91%，检查文件减少92%，并进一步超越强自适应检索基线16%；这些收益在未见指令措辞和几乎所有成本权重下均保持稳定。配套真实模型框架（LLM-Case）在真实gpt-4o代理编辑开源库时也验证了该效果，每个候选补丁均通过实际运行项目真实pytest套件并对比测量的oracle进行评分：过度阅读现象虽较轻但真实存在，而E3在任务成功率相当的情况下是最轻量且最快的策略——其唯一短板是服务提供商的速率限制，而非错误编辑。我们将此视为对执行冗余的受控探测，而非对任何部署代理的测量，并将任务感知执行定位为迈向工程锚定AI（EGAI）——其努力与任务工程现实紧密关联——的重要一步。我们开源了框架与基准。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the issue that large language model agents often over-engage with tasks by re-reading redundant files, even for simple edits, due to a lack of task-aware execution scope estimation. The authors propose E3 (Estimate, Execute, Expand), a method that first estimates task difficulty, executes a minimal viable path, and expands scope only if verification fails. Evaluated on MSE-Bench—a deterministic simulator with 121 edits—E3 achieved 100% success matching the best baseline while reducing computational cost by 85%, tokens by 91%, and inspected files by 92%, and outperformed an adaptive retrieval baseline by 16%. Real-world validation using LLM-Case on a gpt-4o agent editing an open-source library confirmed the reduction in over-reading, with E3 being the most efficient and fastest policy at comparable success rates, though limited by provider rate limits. The work frames this as a probe into execution redundancy and positions task-aware execution as a step toward engineering-grounded AI.
为解决大语言模型代理在简单任务中过度处理（如重复阅读已知文件）的问题，本文提出E3方法（估计、执行、扩展），旨在实现任务感知的执行范围评估。E3先估计最小必要上下文，执行最小可行路径，仅在验证失败时才扩展范围。在包含121次编辑的确定性模拟基准MSE-Bench上，E3达成100%成功率，同时成本降低85%、token减少91%、检查文件减少92%，并优于强自适应检索基线16%。结果在不同指令表述和成本权重下稳定，真实世界测试（gpt-4o编辑开源库）也证实E3效率最高，仅受限于API速率限制而非错误率。该研究将任务感知执行视为工程化AI的关键一步。
DenseReward: Dense Reward Learning via Failure Synthesis for Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Yu Fang, Wanxi Dong, Jiaqi Liu, Yue Yang, Mingxiao Huo, Yao Mu, Huaxiu Yao, Li Erran Li, Daniel Szafir, Mingyu Ding
First: 2026-07-14T17:59:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:59:29+00:00
Comments: Website: https://dense-reward.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reinforcement learning holds great promise for improving robot policies beyond the limits of imitation learning. However, its practical adoption remains bottlenecked by the lack of reliable vision-language reward models that provide dense and informative feedback. Two key challenges remain: acquiring diverse failure data at scale and obtaining fine-grained reward signals beyond sparse trajectory-level success labels. Collecting failure trajectories typically requires laborious human effort, while pseudo-failures constructed by relabeling successful demonstrations fail to capture the diverse physical failure modes that arise during robot execution. Meanwhile, existing reward models often predict sparse binary or trajectory-level rewards, which provide limited guidance for efficient policy optimization. We introduce DenseReward, a dense robotic reward model that addresses both challenges. To train DenseReward, we develop an automated failure data generation pipeline that synthesizes physically realistic failure trajectories in simulation without human labeling, covering diverse failure modes such as collisions, missed grasps, object drops, and recovery behaviors. DenseReward predicts dense frame-level reward scores from visual observations and language instructions, enabling fine-grained estimation of task progress throughout an episode. Experiments show that DenseReward outperforms general-purpose VLMs and existing robotic reward models in dense reward prediction across both simulated and real-world manipulation. We further demonstrate that DenseReward provides effective reward guidance for downstream model predictive control and reinforcement learning. We release the dataset, trained reward models, and evaluation suite to support the development of failure-aware dense reward modeling for robot learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DenseReward：通过失败合成实现密集奖励学习的机器人操作方法
强化学习在超越模仿学习局限以改进机器人策略方面具有巨大潜力。然而，其实际应用仍受限于缺乏可靠的视觉-语言奖励模型，该模型需提供密集且信息丰富的反馈。两大关键挑战仍待解决：大规模获取多样化的失败数据，以及获取超越稀疏轨迹级成功标签的细粒度奖励信号。收集失败轨迹通常需要大量人工劳动，而通过重标记成功演示构建的伪失败轨迹无法捕捉机器人执行过程中出现的多样化物理失败模式。同时，现有奖励模型往往仅预测稀疏的二元或轨迹级奖励，对高效策略优化指导有限。我们提出DenseReward，一种密集型机器人奖励模型，旨在解决上述两个挑战。为训练DenseReward，我们开发了一种自动化失败数据生成管道，可在仿真环境中无需人工标注合成物理上真实的失败轨迹，涵盖碰撞、抓取失败、物体掉落及恢复行为等多种失败模式。DenseReward可从视觉观测与语言指令中预测密集的帧级奖励分数，从而在任务执行过程中实现任务进度的细粒度估计。实验表明，DenseReward在仿真与真实世界操作中均优于通用视觉-语言模型及现有机器人奖励模型的密集奖励预测能力。我们进一步证明，DenseReward可为下游模型预测控制与强化学习提供有效的奖励引导。我们开源了数据集、训练好的奖励模型及评估套件，以支持机器人学习中面向失败的密集奖励建模发展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for dense, informative rewards to enhance robotic manipulation via reinforcement learning, DenseReward addresses two key challenges: acquiring diverse failure data and generating fine-grained reward signals. It introduces an automated pipeline that synthesizes physically realistic failure trajectories in simulation without human labeling, covering modes like collisions and drops. The model then predicts dense frame-level rewards from visual and language inputs, enabling precise task progress estimation. Experiments show it outperforms general VLMs and existing robotic reward models in prediction accuracy across simulation and real-world tasks, and effectively guides downstream control and RL policies.
为提升机器人操作中的强化学习效果，DenseReward旨在解决两大核心问题：获取多样化的失败数据和获得细粒度的奖励信号。该方法通过自动化流程在仿真环境中合成物理上真实的失败轨迹，无需人工标注，涵盖碰撞、抓取失败、物体掉落等模式。模型能从视觉输入和语言指令中预测帧级密集奖励分数，实现任务进度的精细评估。实验表明，其在仿真与真实世界中的奖励预测精度优于通用视觉语言模型和现有机器人奖励模型，并能有效指导下游的模型预测控制与强化学习策略。
The Seriality Gap in Video Diffusion Models
Authors: Jorge Diaz Chao, Konpat Preechakul, Yuxi Liu, Yutong Bai
First: 2026-07-14T17:59:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:59:22+00:00
Comments: Jorge Diaz Chao and Konpat Preechakul contributed equally. 24 pages, 12 figures, and 5 tables. Project page: https://seriality-gap.jdiazchao.com
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When one ball strikes another, then another, video models should predict the consequences of each bounce. In controlled experiments on multi-ball hard-sphere dynamics, we find that the performance of standard bidirectional video diffusion degrades as the causal chain lengthens, even when provided more denoising steps. In a length-matched single-ball control, where ball-ball interactions are absent, the degradation largely disappears, isolating dependent-event structure rather than video length as the cause. Across intervention studies, methods that increase effective serial computation improve performance disproportionately, including autoregressive/blockwise generation and architectural depth. We identify this pattern as the seriality gap: a mismatch between tasks requiring growing serial computation and video diffusion models whose denoising loop does not provide scalable serial compute. We then prove that, for deterministic video prediction, denoising steps do not add serial computation beyond the backbone, indicating a structural obstacle for video diffusion on serial reasoning and simulation tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视频扩散模型中的串行性差距
当一个球撞击另一个球，再撞另一个球时，视频模型应预测每次弹跳的后果。在多球硬球动力学的受控实验中，我们发现标准双向视频扩散模型的性能随着因果链的增长而下降，即使提供了更多的去噪步骤。在长度匹配的单球对照实验中（无球球相互作用），性能下降基本消失，表明问题源于依赖事件结构而非视频长度。在干预研究中，增强有效串行计算的方法显著提升性能，包括自回归/分块生成和架构深度。我们将此模式称为“串行性差距”：任务需要增长的串行计算，而视频扩散模型的去噪循环无法提供可扩展的串行计算。我们进一步证明，对于确定性视频预测，去噪步骤不会在骨干网络之外增加串行计算，表明视频扩散模型在串行推理和仿真任务上存在结构性障碍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that video diffusion models fail to maintain performance as causal chains grow longer in multi-ball dynamics, this study investigates whether the issue stems from video length or the absence of serial computation. The authors compare multi-ball systems with single-ball controls and find that performance degradation correlates with event dependencies, not duration. They experiment with methods like autoregressive generation and deeper architectures, which disproportionately improve results, revealing a 'seriality gap' — a structural mismatch between tasks needing serial computation and diffusion models that lack scalable serial processing. Theoretical analysis confirms that denoising steps in video diffusion do not add serial computation beyond the backbone, indicating a fundamental limitation for serial reasoning tasks.
该研究旨在解释视频扩散模型在多球碰撞等长因果链任务中性能下降的原因。通过对比多球与单球控制实验，作者发现性能衰减源于去噪循环缺乏可扩展的串行计算能力，而非视频长度本身。实验表明，增强串行计算的方法（如自回归和分块生成）显著提升性能，揭示了“串行性缺口”现象。理论分析进一步证明，去噪步骤无法为模型提供额外串行计算，指出了视频扩散模型在序列推理任务中的结构性瓶颈。
TerraZero: Procedural Driving Simulation for Zero-Demonstration Self-Play at Scale
Authors: Zhouchonghao Wu, Akshay Rangesh, Weixin Li, Wei-Jer Chang, Zachary Lee, Tim Wang, Wei Zhan
First: 2026-07-14T17:59:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:59:02+00:00
Comments: Technical Report from Applied Intuition Research
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training robust autonomous driving agents requires a simulator that is fast enough for reinforcement learning at scale, realistic enough to ground behavior in real-world map structure, and diverse enough to cover the safety-critical long tail that logged data rarely contains. We present TerraZero, a procedural driving simulator and self-play training stack. A configurable C engine runs simulation on the CPU and policy inference on the GPU over a zero-copy path, sustaining 1.3M agent-steps per second on a single server-grade GPU, far faster than existing object-level simulators, while keeping fidelity lighter single-agent systems omit: heterogeneous agents, multiple dynamics models, and full traffic-rule enforcement. TerraZero treats logged data only as a source of real-world map geometry, populating each map with randomized rule-based road users and signal controllers and randomizing agent dynamics, rewards, and sizes per episode, so a map yields an unbounded set of scenarios. Every reported policy trains from scratch by reinforcement learning alone on a compute-efficient self-play recipe across GPUs, with zero human demonstrations and no fallback planner at inference. Policies generalize zero-shot across cities and datasets, including emergent left-hand-traffic driving without explicit supervision. As an ego policy, TerraZero is the first fully learned policy to top the InterPlan long-tail benchmark, ahead of larger learned planners; on routine-driving val14 it ranks among the best approaches and is the safest, posting the best collision and time-to-collision scores. On Waymo Open Sim Agents realism the same recipe outperforms other demonstration-free methods and is competitive with the strongest reference-anchored self-play method. One stack serves both roles: driving policies across dynamics for cars and trucks, and sim agents that jointly control vehicles, pedestrians, and cyclists.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TerraZero：面向大规模零演示自博弈的程序化驾驶仿真
训练鲁棒的自动驾驶代理需要一个既足够快速以支持大规模强化学习、又足够真实以反映现实世界地图结构、且足够多样以覆盖日志数据罕见的安全关键长尾场景的仿真器。我们提出了TerraZero，一个程序化驾驶仿真器与自博弈训练框架。一个可配置的C引擎在CPU上运行仿真，在GPU上进行策略推理，通过零拷贝路径实现，单台服务器级GPU可维持每秒130万代理步，远超现有对象级仿真器，同时保持轻量级单代理系统（省略异构代理、多种动力学模型和完整交通规则执行）。TerraZero仅将日志数据视为现实世界地图几何结构的来源，为每张地图填充随机规则生成的道路使用者与信号控制器，并在每轮中随机化代理动力学、奖励和尺寸，使单张地图可生成无限场景。所有报告的策略均通过强化学习从零开始训练，采用计算高效的自博弈配方在多GPU上训练，无需人类演示，推理时无回退规划器。策略可零样本泛化至不同城市与数据集，包括无需显式监督的左舵驾驶。作为主策略，TerraZero是首个在InterPlan长尾基准上超越其他大型学习规划器的完全学习策略；在常规驾驶验证集val14上，其表现居于最佳方法之列，且安全性最高，碰撞与碰撞时间得分最优。在Waymo Open Sim Agents真实度评估中，相同配方优于其他无演示方法，且与最强的参考锚定自博弈方法竞争。一个框架同时支持两种角色：车辆与卡车的动力学驾驶策略，以及联合控制车辆、行人与骑行者的仿真代理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, realistic, and diverse simulation to train robust autonomous driving agents without human demonstrations, TerraZero introduces a procedural simulator and self-play training stack that runs on a single GPU at 1.3M agent-steps per second. It uses a configurable C engine to simulate heterogeneous agents, enforce traffic rules, and randomize dynamics, rewards, and agent sizes per episode, generating an unbounded set of scenarios from logged map geometry. Policies are trained from scratch via reinforcement learning alone, achieving zero-shot generalization across cities and datasets, including left-hand traffic. TerraZero outperforms other demonstration-free methods on the InterPlan long-tail benchmark and ranks among the safest and best-performing on Waymo Open Sim Agents, while also serving as both driving policy and traffic agent controller.
为训练无需人类演示的鲁棒自动驾驶代理，TerraZero提出一个可配置的程序化模拟器与自玩训练栈，单GPU每秒可处理130万代理步，远超现有模拟器。它通过随机化动态、奖励和代理尺寸，结合真实地图几何生成无限场景多样性，支持异构代理与交通规则执行。代理仅通过自玩强化学习训练，零人类演示，零备用规划器，可零样本泛化至不同城市与交通规则，包括左舵驾驶，并在InterPlan和Waymo Open Sim Agents等基准上超越其他无演示方法，安全指标最优，性能居于前列。
PalmClaw: A Native On-Device Agent Framework for Mobile Phones
Authors: Hongru Cai, Yongqi Li, Ran Wei, Wenjie Li
First: 2026-07-14T17:58:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:58:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large Language Model (LLM) agents have moved beyond generating responses to executing multi-step tasks by calling tools, observing the results, and iteratively deciding the next action. Most agent systems run on desktops or servers, which support tool use and task automation. Mobile devices are also important agent environments because they are widely accessible and contain users' data, sensors, and daily-use applications. Existing mobile agents mainly operate smartphones through graphical user interface (GUI) actions such as tapping, swiping, and typing, which often form long, interface-dependent sequences, cannot directly access device capabilities, and make execution boundaries difficult to define. We present \textbf{PalmClaw}, an open-source agent framework that runs natively on mobile phones and manages the sessions, memory, skills, tools, and agent loop directly on the device. PalmClaw exposes device capabilities as device tools with explicit arguments, structured results, and clearly defined execution boundaries. This design enables agents to use mobile capabilities directly while keeping each action explicit and controlled. Experiments show an 11.5\% relative improvement in task success and a 94.9\% reduction in completion time over the strongest baseline, with lower setup burden and traces illustrating how execution boundaries are applied. Code is available at https://github.com/ModalityDance/PalmClaw.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PalmClaw：一种面向手机的原生设备端代理框架
大型语言模型（LLM）代理已从生成响应扩展到通过调用工具、观察结果并迭代决定下一步操作来执行多步骤任务。大多数代理系统运行在桌面或服务器上，支持工具使用和任务自动化。移动设备也是重要的代理环境，因其广泛可访问性及包含用户数据、传感器和日常应用。现有移动代理主要通过图形用户界面（GUI）操作（如点击、滑动和输入）操作智能手机，常形成冗长且依赖界面的序列，无法直接访问设备能力，执行边界难以定义。我们提出\textbf{PalmClaw}，一个开源的原生移动设备代理框架，直接在设备上管理会话、内存、技能、工具和代理循环。PalmClaw将设备能力作为带明确参数、结构化结果和清晰执行边界的设备工具暴露出来。该设计使代理能直接使用移动设备能力，同时保持每个操作明确可控。实验表明，相比最强基线，任务成功率提升11.5%，完成时间缩短94.9%，且设置负担更低，执行边界应用示例亦有展示。代码见https://github.com/ModalityDance/PalmClaw。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for mobile-native agent systems that can directly leverage device capabilities without relying on GUI interactions, PalmClaw introduces a framework that runs natively on smartphones, managing agent sessions, memory, skills, and tool execution locally. It exposes device functions as structured tools with explicit parameters and defined execution boundaries, enabling agents to perform tasks more efficiently and with clearer control. Experimental results demonstrate an 11.5% relative improvement in task success and a 94.9% reduction in completion time compared to the strongest baseline, while also reducing setup complexity and providing clear execution traces.
为实现移动设备上的原生智能体执行，PalmClaw提出一个直接运行于智能手机上的框架，管理会话、记忆、技能与工具循环，无需依赖图形界面操作。它将设备能力封装为结构化工具，明确参数与执行边界，支持直接调用传感器、应用与系统功能，同时保持操作清晰可控。实验表明，相比最强基线，任务成功率提升11.5%，完成时间缩短94.9%，且部署负担更低，执行边界清晰可追溯。
A Shortcut to Statistically Steady-State Turbulence with Flow Matching
Authors: Gianluca Galletti, Gerald Gutenbrunner, William Hornsby, Lorenzo Zanisi, Naomi Carey, Stanislas Pamela, Johannes Brandstetter, Fabian Paischer
First: 2026-07-14T17:58:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:58:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many nonlinear physical systems exhibit an initial transient phase in which perturbations grow before nonlinear interactions lead to a statistically steady state. While this saturated regime is of primary interest, direct numerical simulations must resolve the full transient dynamics before reaching it, incurring significant computational cost. In Computational Fluid Dynamics, reduced-order approaches such as Large Eddy Simulation mitigate computational cost by modeling small-scale dynamics, enabling tractable approximations of turbulent flows. In contrast, for systems such as gyrokinetics, comparably effective closures for the full dynamics are not generally available, and high-fidelity simulations remain necessary. Existing surrogate modeling approaches for these systems are autoregressive, hence they suffer from accumulating error. We instead propose to bypass explicit time evolution by directly modeling the distribution of saturated states under an ergodicity assumption, stating that ensemble averages over samples are equivalent to time averages of a single long simulation. We introduce GyroFlow, a latent generative model that directly estimates steady-state statistics of gyrokinetic turbulence in 5D phase space, without resolving the transient phase. GyroFlow generates saturated snapshots from noise, conditioned on dimensionless operating parameters and outperforms autoregressive, reduced-order, and other generative approaches, while providing substantial speedup. To evaluate generation quality we propose FGyD, a distributional metric computed in the latent space of a pretrained gyrokinetic model, and show that it correlates with downstream flux accuracy and solver convergence. Finally, GyroFlow can be used to warm-start the numerical code used to produce the data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过流匹配实现统计稳态湍流的捷径
许多非线性物理系统在初始瞬态阶段表现出扰动增长，随后非线性相互作用导致统计稳态。虽然该饱和态是主要研究对象，但直接数值模拟需解析完整瞬态动力学才能达到该态，计算成本高昂。在计算流体力学中，大涡模拟等降阶方法通过建模小尺度动力学降低计算成本，可实现湍流的可行近似。相比之下，对于如回旋动能系统等，尚无同样有效的全动力学闭合模型，高保真模拟仍属必要。现有针对此类系统的代理建模方法多为自回归型，因而存在误差累积问题。我们提出绕过显式时间演化，直接在遍历性假设下建模饱和态分布，即样本集合平均等价于单次长时模拟的时间平均。我们引入GyroFlow，一种潜在生成模型，直接在5D相空间中估计回旋动能湍流的稳态统计量，无需解析瞬态阶段。GyroFlow从噪声生成饱和快照，条件于无量纲操作参数，性能优于自回归、降阶及其他生成方法，同时提供显著加速。为评估生成质量，我们提出FGyD，一种在预训练回旋动能模型潜在空间中计算的分布度量，证明其与下游通量精度及求解器收敛性相关。最后，GyroFlow可用于预热生成数据的数值代码。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of simulating turbulence due to initial transients, this work proposes GyroFlow, a latent generative model that directly models steady-state statistics of gyrokinetic turbulence without resolving transients, under an ergodicity assumption. Instead of autoregressive methods that accumulate error, GyroFlow generates saturated turbulence snapshots from noise conditioned on operating parameters, achieving superior performance over existing reduced-order and generative approaches while offering substantial speedup. Experimental evaluation using FGyD, a latent-space distribution metric, shows strong correlation with downstream flux accuracy and solver convergence, and GyroFlow can also be used to warm-start numerical solvers.
为降低湍流模拟中因初始瞬态导致的计算开销，本文提出GyroFlow模型，该模型基于遍历性假设，直接建模陀螺动力学湍流的稳态统计分布，无需显式时间演化。GyroFlow从噪声中生成受无量纲参数条件约束的稳态快照，性能优于自回归和降阶方法，同时实现显著加速。实验采用FGyD分布度量（基于预训练模型的潜在空间）评估生成质量，结果显示其与下游通量精度和求解器收敛性高度相关，验证了模型的有效性及其作为数值模拟预热器的应用价值。
FlowWAM: Optical Flow as a Unified Action Representation for World Action Models
Authors: Yixiang Chen, Peiyan Li, Yuan Xu, Qisen Ma, Jiabing Yang, Kai Wang, Jianhua Yang, Dong An, He Guan, Gaoteng Liu, Jianlou Si, Jun Huang, Jing Liu, Nianfeng Liu, Yan Huang, Liang Wang
First: 2026-07-14T17:57:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:57:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
World Action Models (WAMs) are able to leverage pretrained video generators for both world modeling and action prediction. However, directly leveraging such video generators for control raises a new challenge: how to represent actions in a suitable form that aligns with pretrained video generators while carrying enough motion cues for accurate control. Existing numerical actions fail to satisfy the former, and prior visual action representations overlook the temporal motion structure across frames. We address this issue with FlowWAM, a dual-stream diffusion framework that adopts optical flow as a unified, video-native action representation. Flow videos share the same format as RGB videos and encode rich per-pixel displacement. By jointly modeling them within a shared pretrained video generator, FlowWAM can naturally implement two modes of WAMs. In policy mode, FlowWAM generates flow for action prediction, while in world-model mode, it uses target flow sequences to guide future video generation. Moreover, since flow can be easily extracted from raw videos without action labels, FlowWAM can leverage large-scale action-unlabeled video datasets for pretraining. We empirically find that our flow-based action representation delivers gains across both modes. On RoboTwin manipulation, FlowWAM raises the success rate to 92.94% on the Clean setting and 92.14% on Random, outperforming both VLA and WAM baselines. On WorldArena world modeling, it achieves the best overall EWMScore (63.71) with an 18.4% relative improvement in trajectory accuracy. More results can be found on our project website: https://flow-wam.github.io .
中文标题/摘要
标题：FlowWAM：将光流作为世界动作模型的统一动作表示
世界动作模型（WAMs）能够利用预训练视频生成器进行世界建模和动作预测。然而，直接利用此类视频生成器进行控制面临新挑战：如何以适合预训练视频生成器的形式表示动作，同时保留足够的运动线索以实现精确控制。现有数值动作无法满足前者，而先前的视觉动作表示则忽略了帧间的时间运动结构。我们提出FlowWAM，一种双流扩散框架，采用光流作为统一的、原生视频格式的动作表示。光流视频与RGB视频格式相同，编码丰富的逐像素位移。通过在共享的预训练视频生成器内联合建模，FlowWAM可自然实现WAM的两种模式：在策略模式下，FlowWAM生成光流用于动作预测；在世界模型模式下，它利用目标光流序列引导未来视频生成。此外，由于光流可从原始视频中无需动作标签轻松提取，FlowWAM可利用大规模无动作标注视频数据集进行预训练。实验证明，我们的基于光流的动作表示在两种模式下均取得性能提升。在RoboTwin操作任务中，FlowWAM在Clean设置下成功率提升至92.94%，在Random设置下为92.14%，优于VLA和WAM基线。在WorldArena世界建模任务中，其整体EWMScore达到63.71，轨迹精度相对提升18.4%。更多结果请访问项目网站：https://flow-wam.github.io。

Motivated by the need for a unified action representation that aligns with pretrained video generators while preserving motion cues, FlowWAM introduces a dual-stream diffusion framework using optical flow as its core action representation. This approach leverages the video-native format of flow videos to jointly model actions and world states within a shared pretrained generator, enabling both policy and world-modeling modes. Experiments show that FlowWAM significantly improves performance: on RoboTwin manipulation, it achieves 92.94% success rate on Clean and 92.14% on Random, outperforming VLA and WAM baselines; on WorldArena, it attains the highest EWMScore (63.71) with an 18.4% relative gain in trajectory accuracy.
为解决现有动作表示与预训练视频生成器不兼容、缺乏时序运动结构的问题，FlowWAM提出一种双流扩散框架，以光流作为统一的动作表示。该方法利用光流的视频原生格式和逐像素位移特性，在共享视频生成器内联合建模动作预测与世界建模。实验表明，FlowWAM在RoboTwin任务中成功率达92.94%（Clean）和92.14%（Random），优于VLA和WAM基线；在WorldArena中取得最高EWMScore（63.71），轨迹精度提升18.4%，验证了其在两种模式下的优越性。
Audio-Native Speech Recognition with a Frozen Discrete-Diffusion Language Model
Authors: Harsha Vardhan Khurdula, Abhinav Kumar Singh, Yoeven D Khemlani, Vineet Agarwal
First: 2026-07-14T17:53:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:53:22+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 2 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automatic speech recognition is dominated by autoregressive decoders that emit one token at a time. We ask whether a discrete diffusion language model can transcribe speech instead, refining a whole transcript in parallel over a small number of denoising steps. We train an audio-native interface for DiffusionGemma, a 26B mixture-of-experts model that generates text by uniform, random-token discrete diffusion rather than the absorbing-mask scheme common to recent diffusion language models. A frozen Whisper encoder supplies acoustic features, a lightweight projector maps them into the model embedding space, and low-rank adapters let the frozen backbone attend to the new modality. About 42M parameters are trained, which is 0.16 percent of the backbone. We find that the natural training objectives fail to ground the audio because their gradient reaches the projector only through attention that has already dismissed it. A connectionist temporal classification loss applied through the frozen output head breaks this deadlock. The resulting model reaches 6.6 percent word error rate on LibriSpeech test-clean, transcribes in roughly eight parallel steps regardless of utterance length, and uses a single adapter trained on six languages, which we evaluate here on English, Hindi, and Mandarin.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用冻结的离散扩散语言模型实现音频原生语音识别
自动语音识别主要由逐个生成令牌的自回归解码器主导。我们探讨离散扩散语言模型是否能替代该方法，通过少量去噪步骤并行生成完整转录文本。我们训练了DiffusionGemma模型的音频原生接口，该模型为260亿参数的专家混合模型，通过均匀随机令牌的离散扩散生成文本，而非近期扩散语言模型常用的吸收掩码方案。冻结的Whisper编码器提供声学特征，轻量级投影器将其映射至模型嵌入空间，低秩适配器使冻结主干网络关注新模态。 约4200万参数被训练，占主干网络的0.16%。我们发现，自然训练目标因梯度需通过已忽略音频的注意力机制才能到达投影器，导致音频无法有效锚定。通过冻结输出头应用连接主义时间分类损失，打破了这一僵局。该模型在LibriSpeech测试集clean上达到6.6%的词错误率，无论语句长度如何，均在约八步并行去噪中完成转录，且仅使用一个在六种语言上训练的适配器，本文评估其在英语、印地语和中文上的表现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of autoregressive decoders in speech recognition, the authors explore whether a discrete diffusion language model can directly transcribe audio in parallel. They adapt DiffusionGemma—a 26B MoE model using random-token diffusion—to handle audio via a frozen Whisper encoder, a lightweight projector, and low-rank adapters.
