The model achieves 6.6% word error rate on LibriSpeech test-clean, processes utterances in ~8 parallel steps regardless of length, and generalizes across English, Hindi, and Mandarin using a single adapter.
为解决传统自回归解码器在语音识别中的效率瓶颈，作者探索冻结的离散扩散语言模型能否并行转录音频。他们将使用随机令牌扩散的26B MoE模型DiffusionGemma与冻结的Whisper编码器结合，通过轻量投影器和低秩适配器实现跨模态注意力。仅训练4200万参数，通过在冻结输出头应用连接主义时间分类损失解决梯度隔离问题。最终模型在LibriSpeech test-clean上达到6.6%词错误率，无论语音长度均在约8步并行处理中完成转录，并通过单一适配器支持英语、印地语和中文。
Inclusive Federated Learning Through Compliance-Weighted Noise Allocation in Healthcare AI
Authors: Santhosh Parampottupadam, Melih Coşğun, Sarthak Pati, Maximilian Zenk, Saikat Roy, Dimitrios Bounias, Benjamin Hamm, Sinem Sav, Ralf Floca, Klaus Maier-Hein
First: 2025-05-28T08:36:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:52:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Background: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative training of clinical AI models without centralizing patient data, but adoption is limited by privacy concerns, heterogeneous institutional compliance, and resource disparities; standard differential privacy (DP) applies uniform noise to all clients, penalizing well-compliant or under-resourced institutions. Objective: We introduce a compliance-aware FL framework that adapts DP to institutional compliance, letting lower-compliance sites participate without uniformly penalizing others. Methods: A compliance scoring tool aligned with HIPAA, GDPR, NIST, ISO, and HL7/FHIR maps each client score to a per-step Gaussian noise scale for server-side DP-SGD on a small aggregator dataset. The formal $(ε,δ)$ bound applies to the aggregator dataset under a semi-honest aggregator; client-level DP needs secure aggregation (future work). We evaluate five FL strategies on PneumoniaMNIST and BreastMNIST (16 clients, 50 rounds, five seeds); the cumulative aggregator-dataset $ε$ is about 1434 (Breast) and 513 (Pneumonia) at $δ=10^{-5}$. Results: Including 12 lower-compliance clients (Experiment 1) versus a compliant-only baseline (Experiment 4) changed BreastMNIST accuracy by +4.5 (FedAvg), +6.8 (FedMedian), +5.2 (FedProx), +1.6 (FedYogi), and -4.1 (FedAdam) percentage points (pooled +2.8 pp; not significant at n=5; up to +17 pp per configuration); compliance-weighted allocation matched uniform server-side DP at equal mean noise (+0.1 pp), carrying no utility penalty, and first-round noise cost 1.3 pp (Breast) and 2.5 pp (Pneumonia, FedAvg). Conclusions: Compliance-weighted server-side DP lets lower-compliance institutions join FL without degrading performance, giving auditable per-site noise control at no utility cost; formal guarantees apply to the aggregator dataset, with client-level DP requiring secure aggregation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过合规加权噪声分配实现医疗AI中的包容性联邦学习
背景：联邦学习（FL）可在不集中患者数据的情况下协作训练临床AI模型，但其应用受限于隐私担忧、机构合规异质性及资源差异；标准差分隐私（DP）对所有客户端施加统一噪声，惩罚合规度高或资源匮乏的机构。目标：我们提出一种感知合规性的FL框架，根据机构合规性调整DP，使低合规机构可参与而不均匀惩罚其他机构。方法：一个与HIPAA、GDPR、NIST、ISO及HL7/FHIR对齐的合规评分工具，将每个客户端得分映射为服务器端DP-SGD每步的高斯噪声尺度，应用于小型聚合数据集。在半诚实聚合器假设下，对聚合数据集的正式$(ε,δ)$界限成立；客户端级DP需安全聚合（未来工作）。我们在PneumoniaMNIST和BreastMNIST（16个客户端，50轮，五种子）上评估五种FL策略；在$δ=10^{-5}$下，聚合数据集的累积$ε$约为Breast为1434、Pneumonia为513。结果：包含12个低合规客户端（实验1）与仅合规机构基线（实验4）相比，BreastMNIST准确率分别提升+4.5（FedAvg）、+6.8（FedMedian）、+5.2（FedProx）、+1.6（FedYogi）、-4.1（FedAdam）个百分点（综合+2.8 pp；n=5时无显著性；每配置最高+17 pp）；合规加权分配在均值噪声相等时与统一服务器端DP匹配（+0.1 pp），无性能损失，首轮噪声成本为Breast 1.3 pp、Pneumonia 2.5 pp（FedAvg）。结论：合规加权服务器端DP使低合规机构可加入FL而不损害性能，提供可审计的每站点噪声控制且无性能代价；正式保障适用于聚合数据集，客户端级DP需安全聚合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by privacy and compliance barriers in healthcare federated learning, this study proposes a compliance-weighted noise allocation method that adapts differential privacy to institutional adherence levels, allowing low-compliance sites to participate without uniform penalty. The method assigns per-step Gaussian noise scales to clients based on a HIPAA/GDPR-aligned compliance score, applied server-side to a small aggregator dataset with formal (ε,δ) guarantees under semi-honest aggregation. Experiments on two medical datasets showed that including lower-compliance clients improved model accuracy by up to 17 percentage points in some algorithms, with compliance-weighted noise matching uniform DP in utility while incurring only minor first-round cost.
为解决医疗联邦学习中机构合规性差异导致的参与障碍，本研究提出一种基于合规度的噪声分配方法，将差分隐私的噪声强度与机构合规评分挂钩。通过与HIPAA、GDPR等标准对齐的评分系统，每个客户端的合规分数决定其在服务器端DP-SGD中每轮的高斯噪声尺度，噪声应用于小型聚合数据集。在PneumoniaMNIST和BreastMNIST数据集上对五种联邦学习算法进行评估，结果表明引入低合规机构后，模型平均准确率提升2.8个百分点，且与统一噪声分配相比无显著性能损失，同时保持对聚合数据集的正式$(ε,δ)$隐私保证。
DermDepth: Toward Monocular Metric Scale 3D Reconstruction Models for Dermatology
Authors: Héctor Carrión, Narges Norouzi
Venue: MICCAI 2026
First: 2026-07-14T17:51:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:51:37+00:00
Comments: Accepted at MICCAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Dermatological practice routinely involves measuring and tracking lesion size, morphology and texture, as critical components of wound or skin cancer screening, monitoring and diagnosis. To accomplish this task, practitioners often image the skin surface with commonly available off-the-shelf camera sensors. This has led to an overwhelming research focus on 2D methods while these objectives naturally benefit from 3D information. In this paper, we demonstrate that dense monocular 3D reconstructions, metric scale measurements and rich surface normal texture estimates are achievable for both dermoscopic and macroscopic cases without the need for additional hardware or multiple captures. We present DermDepth, the first single-view metric scale 3D model for the dermatological domain and D-Synth, the first synthetic dermoscopic dataset with pixel-perfect 3D information. Our experiments show training DermDepth on D-Synth corrects metric scale error from over 16x to under 1.1x for real dermoscopic data, while preserving geometric quality and increasing texture richness. Fine-tuning on a small amount of real clinical samples generalizes our method across three real-world benchmarks spanning the few mm to hundred cm range, diverse skin-tones, chronic wound cases and produces measurements broadly consistent with disease size reported in medical literature. All code, data and models are available at https://github.com/hectorcarrion/dermdepth.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DermDepth：面向皮肤科的单目度量级三维重建模型
皮肤科临床实践中，测量和追踪病变大小、形态和纹理是伤口或皮肤癌筛查、监测和诊断的关键环节。为完成此任务，临床医生常使用普通现成相机传感器拍摄皮肤表面图像。这导致研究过度聚焦于2D方法，而这些目标天然受益于3D信息。本文证明，无需额外硬件或多次拍摄，即可实现皮肤镜和宏观病例的稠密单目3D重建、度量级测量及丰富的表面法线纹理估计。我们提出DermDepth，这是首个面向皮肤科领域的单视角度量级3D模型，并构建了D-Synth，这是首个像素级精确3D信息的合成皮肤镜数据集。实验表明，以D-Synth训练DermDepth可将真实皮肤镜数据的度量级误差从16倍以上降至1.1倍以下，同时保持几何质量并增强纹理丰富度。在少量真实临床样本上微调后，该方法可泛化至涵盖毫米至百厘米范围、多种肤色及慢性伤口病例的三个真实世界基准，其测量结果与医学文献中报告的疾病尺寸基本一致。所有代码、数据和模型均在https://github.com/hectorcarrion/dermdepth公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate 3D measurements in dermatology to support lesion monitoring and diagnosis, this paper introduces DermDepth, a monocular 3D reconstruction model that estimates metric-scale depth, surface normals, and texture from single-view dermoscopic and macroscopic images without requiring additional hardware. The method is trained on D-Synth, a novel synthetic dataset with pixel-perfect 3D ground truth, which significantly reduces metric scale error from over 16x to under 1.1x on real dermoscopic data while preserving geometric fidelity and enhancing texture detail. Fine-tuning on minimal real clinical data enables generalization across diverse benchmarks including small lesions and large wound cases, producing measurements consistent with medical literature.
为在无需额外硬件的情况下实现皮肤病变的精准三维测量，该研究提出DermDepth模型，可从单张图像中重建具有真实尺度的深度、表面法线与纹理，适用于皮肤镜和宏观图像。该模型基于D-Synth合成数据集（含像素级三维真值）训练，将真实皮肤镜数据的尺度误差从16倍以上降至1.1倍以内，同时保持几何精度并增强纹理细节。在少量真实临床数据微调后，模型可泛化至毫米至百厘米尺度、多种肤色及慢性伤口等真实场景，其测量结果与医学文献中疾病尺寸报告高度一致。
Dynamic Resource Allocation for Ensemble Determinization MCTS
Authors: Jakub Kowalski, Adam Ciężkowski, Artur Krzyżyński, Mark H. M. Winands
First: 2026-07-14T17:51:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:51:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Simulation-based algorithms are especially suited for high-uncertainty environments such as adversarial board games with significant elements of randomness and hidden information. In particular, several Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) variants are commonly used in such domains. In this paper, we propose a series of enhancements for Ensemble Determinization MCTS, introducing two axes for dynamic resource allocation. First, Dynamic Number of Determinizations, increases or decreases the number of currently used determinization trees depending on the behavior of so-far search. Second, Dynamic Simulation Allocation, splits the simulation budget nonuniformly across the determinization trees, using simulation-to-simulation decisions to choose the tree with potentially the best knowledge gain. As benchmark domains, we used three popular tabletop games: Jaipur, Lost Cities, and Splendor. Testing our proposed enhancements in iteration- and time-based settings showed that particular configurations yield a statistically significant increase in the algorithm's strength.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于集成确定化MCTS的动态资源分配
基于模拟的算法特别适用于高不确定性环境，如包含显著随机性和隐藏信息的对抗性棋盘游戏。特别是，多种蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）变体常用于此类领域。本文提出对集成确定化MCTS的一系列改进，引入两个动态资源分配维度：第一，动态确定化数量，根据当前搜索行为动态增加或减少所用确定化树的数量；第二，动态模拟分配，非均匀地将模拟预算分配至各确定化树，通过模拟到模拟的决策选择潜在知识增益最大的树。我们以三款流行桌面游戏——《贾米尔》《失落的城市》和《璀璨宝石》作为基准领域。在迭代和时间两种设置下测试所提改进，结果显示特定配置能显著提升算法性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve Monte Carlo Tree Search performance in high-uncertainty games with randomness and hidden information, this paper introduces dynamic resource allocation for Ensemble Determinization MCTS. It proposes two mechanisms: dynamically adjusting the number of determinization trees based on search behavior, and non-uniformly allocating simulation budgets across trees using simulation-to-simulation decisions to maximize knowledge gain. Experiments on Jaipur, Lost Cities, and Splendor demonstrated that specific configurations of these enhancements led to statistically significant performance improvements in both iterative and time-based settings.
为提升蒙特卡洛树搜索在高不确定性、含随机性和隐藏信息的棋类游戏中表现，本文提出对集成确定化MCTS进行动态资源分配。方法根据搜索历史动态调整确定化树数量，并非均匀分配模拟预算，而是依据模拟间决策选择知识增益潜力最大的树。在Jaipur、Lost Cities和Splendor三个桌游上的实验表明，特定配置下该方法在迭代与时间限制场景中均显著提升了算法性能。
The Spectrum Is Not Enough: When Context Helps Time-Series Forecasting
Authors: Mert Onur Cakiroglu, Mehmet Dalkilic, Hasan Kurban
First: 2026-07-14T17:50:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:50:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A growing family of indices scores how predictable a series is from its spectrum. Practitioners increasingly read these scores as answering a different question: whether \emph{adding context}, a longer lookback, a retrieval plug-in, or a pretrained model, will help. These are not the same question. The value of context is a property of the operating point, not of the series. Any index built from the power spectrum is invariant under phase randomization, whereas the beyond-second-order value that retrieval and foundation models supply is not, because a phase-randomized series is asymptotically Gaussian. We state this as an impossibility result and isolate it with surrogate pairs that fix the spectrum and the marginal by construction. We then give a label-free, configuration-level diagnostic, the coverage deficit, whose principal term measures beyond-spectrum structure as the gain of analog over linear prediction. On seven benchmarks the prediction holds: window-keyed retrieval's value collapses across surrogate pairs (ECL median $+33\%\!\to\!-35\%$, $p{<}10^{-40}$) while every spectral index stays frozen; a foundation model's value splits into a surviving second-order part and a small beyond-linear margin that collapses; a longer linear window's value survives. Leave-one-dataset-out, the structure term predicts the sign of beyond-spectrum value where the spectral indices trail it, and the reverse holds for the second-order mechanism. We introduce no new forecaster; the contribution is the distinction, a controlled comparison, and a diagnostic for the deployment decision. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SINE.
中文标题/摘要
标题：频谱不足：上下文如何助力时间序列预测
一系列增长中的指标通过频谱评估序列的可预测性。从业者越来越多地将这些分数解读为回答另一个问题：添加上下文、延长回溯窗口、引入检索插件或预训练模型是否有助于提升预测。这两个问题并不相同。上下文的价值是操作点的属性，而非序列本身的属性。任何基于功率谱构建的指标在相位随机化下保持不变，而检索和基础模型提供的超越二阶的值则不然，因为相位随机化序列渐近服从高斯分布。我们将此表述为一个不可能性结果，并通过构造固定频谱与边际的代理对加以隔离。随后，我们提出一种无标签、配置级别的诊断工具——覆盖不足，其主项衡量超越频谱结构的收益，即模拟预测相对于线性预测的优势。在七个基准测试中，该预测成立：窗口键检索的性能在代理对间崩溃（ECL中位数从+33%降至-35%，p<10^{-40}），而所有频谱指标保持不变；基础模型的性能分裂为幸存的二阶部分和一个微小的超越线性边际，后者崩溃；延长线性窗口的性能保持稳定。在留一数据集外测试中，结构项预测超越频谱值的符号，而频谱指标滞后于它；反之，二阶机制则相反。我们未引入新预测器，贡献在于区分、受控比较及部署决策诊断。代码：https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SINE。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the common misinterpretation that spectral indices predict the value of context in time-series forecasting, this paper argues that such indices are invariant under phase randomization and thus cannot capture the value of retrieval or foundation models, which depend on beyond-second-order structure. The authors construct surrogate pairs that preserve spectrum and marginal distribution while varying higher-order structure, then introduce the 'coverage deficit' — a label-free diagnostic measuring the gain of analog over linear prediction — to quantify beyond-spectrum value. Experiments on seven benchmarks show that retrieval models lose predictive power across surrogates while spectral indices remain unchanged, foundation models split into a second-order component and a collapsing beyond-linear margin, and longer linear windows retain value. The structure term also predicts the sign of beyond-spectrum value where spectral indices fail, and vice versa for second-order mechanisms.
本文指出，谱基可预测性指标无法可靠判断添加上下文（如检索或预训练模型）是否能提升时间序列预测效果。作者证明，谱指标在相位随机化下保持不变，因而无法捕捉上下文方法所依赖的高阶结构。通过构造保持谱与边际分布的代理数据对，他们发现检索与基础模型的增益在控制下消失，而更长的线性窗口仍具价值。其新诊断指标“覆盖赤字”量化了超越谱结构的预测增益，实验证明其在七个基准上准确预测上下文是否有效，而谱指标始终不变。本文未提出新模型，而是厘清了谱指标的理论局限，并提供部署决策工具。
Propheticus: Machine Learning Framework for the Development of Predictive Models for Reliable and Secure Software
Authors: João R. Campos, Marco Vieira, Ernesto Costa
First: 2018-09-06T09:26:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:50:06+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in the 30th IEEE International Symposium on Software Reliability Engineering (ISSRE) 2019
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The growing complexity of software calls for innovative solutions that support the deployment of reliable and secure software. Machine Learning (ML) has shown its applicability to various complex problems and is frequently used in the dependability domain, both for supporting systems design and verification activities. However, using ML is complex and highly dependent on the problem in hand, increasing the probability of mistakes that compromise the results. In this paper, we introduce Propheticus, a ML framework that can be used to create predictive models for reliable and secure software systems. Propheticus attempts to abstract the complexity of ML whilst being easy to use and accommodating the needs of the users. To demonstrate its use, we present two case studies (vulnerability prediction and online failure prediction) that show how it can considerably ease and expedite a thorough ML workflow.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Propheticus：用于开发可靠安全软件预测模型的机器学习框架
软件日益复杂，亟需创新解决方案以支持可靠安全软件的部署。机器学习（ML）已证明其在各类复杂问题中的适用性，并常用于可靠性领域，既支持系统设计也支持验证活动。然而，使用ML过程复杂且高度依赖具体问题，易导致错误并影响结果。本文介绍Propheticus，一个可创建可靠安全软件系统预测模型的ML框架。Propheticus旨在抽象ML复杂性，同时易于使用并满足用户需求。为展示其应用，我们呈现两个案例研究（漏洞预测与在线故障预测），表明其可显著简化并加速完整的ML工作流程。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the increasing complexity of software systems, the paper introduces Propheticus, a machine learning framework designed to simplify the creation of predictive models for software reliability and security. The framework abstracts ML complexities while remaining user-friendly and adaptable to domain-specific needs. Through two case studies—vulnerability prediction and online failure prediction—the authors demonstrate that Propheticus significantly streamlines and accelerates the ML workflow, reducing the likelihood of human error and improving model deployment efficiency.
为应对软件系统日益增长的复杂性，Propheticus框架旨在简化机器学习在构建可靠安全软件模型中的应用。该框架通过抽象机器学习的复杂性，同时保持易用性和对用户需求的适应性。通过两个案例研究——漏洞预测和在线故障预测——Propheticus证明了其能有效简化并加速机器学习工作流程，显著降低人为错误风险，提升模型开发效率。
Watermark Forensics for Generative Models: An Information-Theoretic Perspective
Authors: Xiaoyu Li, Zheng Gao, Xiaoyan Feng, Jiaojiao Jiang, Yulei Sui, Jiankun Hu
First: 2026-07-14T17:49:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:49:52+00:00
Comments: The abstract has been shortened to comply with arXiv's length limit
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A watermark in a generative model's output is usually asked only whether a text is machine-made. The same mark can do more: attribute it to the user who produced it, extract a hidden payload, or localize the part that survives editing. These form a forensic ladder, and we ask what each rung costs in the sample length $n$. One object organizes the answers. Let $S$ be the secret the mark carries (a user's identity or payload), and let the information profile $ν(t)=I(S;X_t\mid X_{<t})$ record how much the $t$-th token reveals about $S$ given the earlier ones. Its total mass pays for attribution and extraction; how that mass is spread pays for localization; and detection alone is paid for not by information but by presence, the distance from the marked to the unmarked distribution. The literature's two quality models, a mark subtle on every token and one that stamps a few tokens loudly, are two incomparable ways of capping this profile. Our main theorem settles the ladder's entropy column. For statistically distortion-free schemes, attributing a text to one of $N$ users costs $Θ(\log N/h)$ tokens over every stationary-ergodic source of entropy rate $h$, sharp to a $(1+o(1))$ factor: to our knowledge the first tight entropy-rate law for multi-user attribution (via exact alignment). The natural collision-counting analysis overcharges without bound; only a decoder thresholding each candidate by its own realized surprisal attains the rate while almost never implicating an innocent user. A matching converse makes the law two-sided, and extraction of an $\ell$-bit payload costs $Θ(\ell/h)$. Two gaps are real, not modeling artifacts: a $Θ(\log N)$-token window in which a text is provably machine-made yet unattributable, and a footprint-resolution uncertainty principle. Experiments on GPT-2, Pythia-410M, and Qwen2.5 recover the predicted constants.
中文标题/摘要
标题：生成模型水印取证：信息论视角
生成模型输出中的水印通常仅用于判断文本是否由机器生成。但同一水印还能做更多：归因于特定用户、提取隐藏载荷，或定位编辑后仍保留的部分。这些构成一个取证阶梯，我们探讨每一步在样本长度n下的成本。 一个对象统合所有答案：设S为水印携带的秘密（如用户身份或载荷），信息轮廓ν(t)=I(S;X_t|X_{<t})记录第t个标记在已知前序标记条件下对S的揭示量。其总质量支付归因与提取成本；质量分布方式支付定位成本；而检测成本则由存在性支付，即标记分布与未标记分布之间的距离。文献中两种质量模型——一种在每个标记上微妙嵌入，另一种在少数标记上强烈标记——是此轮廓的两种不可比较的上限方式。 我们的主要定理解决了阶梯的熵列：对于统计失真无损方案，将文本归因于N个用户之一需消耗Θ(log N/h)个标记，适用于熵率为h的平稳遍历源，且精度为(1+o(1))因子——据我们所知，这是首个针对多用户归因的紧致熵率定律（通过精确对齐）。自然的碰撞计数分析会无界高估；唯有对每个候选用户按其实际惊讶值阈值解码，才能达到该速率且几乎从不误指无辜用户。匹配的逆定理使该定律双向成立，且提取ℓ比特载荷需消耗Θ(ℓ/h)个标记。两个差距是真实存在的，而非建模伪影：一个长度为Θ(log N)的窗口内，文本可被证明为机器生成却无法归因，以及足迹分辨率的不确定性原理。在GPT-2、Pythia-410M和Qwen2.5上的实验验证了预测常数。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the information-theoretic cost of watermarking generative model outputs for forensic tasks, the paper introduces an information profile ν(t) that measures how much each token reveals about a hidden secret S. It establishes that attribution to N users requires Θ(log N/h) tokens over sources with entropy rate h, matching a tight converse and confirming the first exact entropy-rate law for multi-user attribution. Extraction of an ℓ-bit payload costs Θ(ℓ/h) tokens. The study identifies two fundamental gaps: a Θ(log N) token window where outputs are machine-made but unattributable, and a footprint-resolution uncertainty principle. Experiments on GPT-2, Pythia-410M, and Qwen2.5 validate the predicted constants.
为理解生成模型输出水印在法证任务中的信息理论代价，本文提出一个框架，通过信息轮廓量化每个词元对隐藏秘密的泄露量，从而衡量归因、载荷提取和定位的成本。作者推导出紧致的熵率定律：将文本归因于N个用户需Θ(log N/h)个词元，提取ℓ比特载荷需Θ(ℓ/h)个词元，且有匹配的逆向界限。在GPT-2、Pythia-410M和Qwen2.5上的实验验证了预测常数，同时揭示两个本质性缺陷：即使文本为机器生成，仍存在可证明无法归因的词元窗口，以及定位分辨率的不确定性原理。
The TIME Machine: On The Power of Motion for Efficient Perception
Authors: Mantas Skackauskas, Xinyue Hao, Laura Sevilla-Lara
First: 2026-05-21T21:22:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:49:11+00:00
Comments: for project page, see https://time-model.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Video representation learning has seen tremendous progress in recent years. This has been driven by many factors, including the scale of training and the success of visual models trained contrastively with language. While these factors have pushed the boundaries of what video models can do, they also introduce their own set of limitations: first, scaling video models can reach prohibitive costs and second, learning from language restricts the range of concepts that can be learned to those in captions. As a result, video models still struggle with temporal understanding. In this paper we propose a novel approach that uses motion as the central modality for video representation. In particular, given the motion in a video in the form of point-tracks, we use a masked-autoencoder to mask some of the tracks and train the autoencoder to reconstruct the missing tracks. This allows us to learn a representation in a self-supervised manner. We show that using motion to represent videos actually addresses both of the core limitations of video technology. First, it allows us to massively reduce the scale of training data, as motion is inherently appearance-independent and hence needs fewer examples to generalize well. Second, motion allows us to bypass the language-dependent training paradigm, learning better fine-grained concepts. The result is an embedding that we call TIME (Temporally Informed Motion Embedding), a representation trained exclusively on synthetic motion data. We test this embedding on a wide set of tasks in a zero-shot manner. We observe that without bells and whistles, performance is on par with state-of-the-art models using up to 4 orders of magnitude less training data. This is a stepping stone towards a new paradigm of video models that are both more temporally aware as well as more scalable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：时间机器：运动在高效感知中的作用
近年来，视频表征学习取得了巨大进展，这得益于训练规模的扩大以及视觉模型与语言对比训练的成功。然而，这些因素也带来了自身局限：一是视频模型的扩展成本高昂，二是依赖语言训练限制了可学习概念的范围仅限于字幕内容。因此，视频模型在时间理解方面仍存在困难。本文提出一种新颖方法，以运动作为视频表征的核心模态。具体而言，给定视频中的点轨迹形式的运动数据，我们使用掩码自编码器遮蔽部分轨迹，并训练自编码器重建缺失轨迹，从而实现自监督学习。我们证明，利用运动表征视频实际上解决了视频技术的两大核心局限：首先，运动本质上与外观无关，因此可大幅减少训练数据规模，提升泛化能力；其次，运动使我们摆脱语言依赖的训练范式，学习更精细的概念。最终我们得到一种名为TIME（时间感知运动嵌入）的嵌入表示，该表示仅基于合成运动数据训练。我们在多种任务上以零样本方式测试该嵌入，结果表明，无需额外技巧，其性能与现有最先进模型相当，但训练数据量低达4个数量级。这为构建更具备时间感知能力且更具可扩展性的视频模型奠定了基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and language-dependency of current video models, this paper proposes TIME, a self-supervised method that learns video representations from motion alone. Using point-tracks extracted from video, it trains a masked autoencoder to reconstruct occluded motion trajectories, enabling efficient, appearance-agnostic learning. Experimental results show that TIME achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art models while requiring up to 4 orders of magnitude less training data, demonstrating its scalability and ability to capture fine-grained temporal concepts without relying on language.
为解决当前视频模型训练成本高、依赖语言标注的问题，本文提出TIME方法，通过点轨迹的掩码自编码学习运动主导的视频表征。该方法仅使用合成运动数据训练，无需外观或文本信息，显著降低样本需求并摆脱语言依赖。实验表明，TIME在零样本设置下性能可媲美现有最先进模型，且训练数据量减少达四个数量级，展现出高效性和对时序信息的强感知能力。
LapSurgie: Humanoid Robots Performing Surgery via Teleoperated Handheld Laparoscopy
Authors: Zekai Liang, Xiao Liang, Soofiyan Atar, Sreyan Das, Zoe Chiu, Peihan Zhang, Calvin Joyce, Florian Richter, Shanglei Liu, Michael C. Yip
First: 2025-10-03T21:55:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:47:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robotic laparoscopic surgery has gained increasing attention in recent years for its potential to deliver more efficient and precise minimally invasive procedures. However, adoption of surgical robotic platforms remains largely confined to high-resource medical centers, exacerbating healthcare disparities in rural and low-resource regions. To close this gap, a range of solutions has been explored, from remote mentorship to fully remote telesurgery. Yet, the practical deployment of surgical robotic systems to underserved communities remains an unsolved challenge. Humanoid systems offer a promising path toward deployability, as they can directly operate in environments designed for humans without extensive infrastructure modifications -- including operating rooms. In this work, we introduce LapSurgie, the first humanoid-robot-based laparoscopic teleoperation framework. The system leverages an inverse-mapping strategy for manual-wristed laparoscopic instruments that abides to remote center-of-motion constraints, enabling precise hand-to-tool control of off-the-shelf surgical laparoscopic tools without additional setup requirements. A control console equipped with a stereo vision system provides real-time visual feedback. Finally, a comprehensive user study across platforms demonstrates the effectiveness of the proposed framework and provides initial evidence for the feasibility of deploying humanoid robots in laparoscopic procedures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LapSurgie：通过远程手持腹腔镜操作的人形机器人进行手术
近年来，机器人腹腔镜手术因其在提供更高效、更精准的微创手术方面的潜力而受到越来越多关注。然而，手术机器人平台的采用仍主要局限于高资源医疗中心，加剧了农村和低资源地区医疗资源的不平等。为弥合这一差距，人们探索了从远程指导到完全远程远程手术等多种解决方案。但将手术机器人系统实际部署到服务不足的社区仍是一个未解决的挑战。人形系统提供了一条有前景的可部署路径，因为它们可在无需大量基础设施改造的环境中直接操作——包括手术室。本文中，我们提出了LapSurgie，这是首个基于人形机器人的腹腔镜远程操作框架。该系统采用逆映射策略，用于手动腕式腹腔镜器械，符合远程运动中心约束，无需额外设置即可实现对现成手术腹腔镜工具的精确手控。配备立体视觉系统的控制台提供实时视觉反馈。最后，跨平台的全面用户研究表明了所提框架的有效性，并为在腹腔镜手术中部署人形机器人提供了初步可行性证据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited access to robotic surgery in low-resource regions, this work proposes LapSurgie, a humanoid-robot-based teleoperation system for laparoscopic surgery. It employs an inverse-mapping strategy to translate human hand movements into precise tool motions while respecting remote center-of-motion constraints, enabling direct control of standard laparoscopic instruments without additional hardware. The system integrates a stereo vision console for real-time visual feedback and is evaluated through a multi-platform user study, which confirms its operational feasibility and usability in surgical contexts.
为解决机器人腹腔镜手术在资源匮乏地区难以普及的问题，本研究提出LapSurgie——首个基于人形机器人实现腹腔镜远程操作的框架。系统采用逆映射策略，将人手动作精确转化为手术器械运动，同时满足远程运动中心约束，无需额外配置即可操控标准腹腔镜器械。控制台配备立体视觉系统，提供实时视觉反馈。多平台用户实验结果表明，该框架操作可行且精度达标，证明人形机器人可在无需特殊基础设施的普通环境中完成腹腔镜手术任务。
Bringing Back Rule Induction to Fluid Intelligence Research? An Initial Validation of the ARC-AGI Benchmark in Humans
Authors: Jasmin Thelen, Oliver Wilhelm
First: 2026-07-13T08:45:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:47:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Two competing perspectives on fluid intelligence (gf) measures propose that performance is primarily constrained either by working memory capacity or by the ability to induce novel relations. The first perspective is currently dominant in measurement, as evident from the use of a limited set of recurring rules, whereas the second perspective is reflected in many definitions but rarely present in measurement. The ARC-AGI benchmark predominantly requires rule induction and was proposed as a measure of gf for both humans and artificial systems. However, its psychometric properties have not yet been examined in human samples. We therefore investigated the psychometric characteristics and nomological network of ARC-AGI in a first study with 100 participants. A compilation of ARC-AGI items showed good psychometric properties and correlated substantially with figural fluid intelligence as measured by a figural reasoning test (ρ= .63). Associations with figural originality were weak. These findings provide initial support for the validity of ARC-AGI as a measure of human fluid intelligence. Future research should include more rule induction tasks as well as additional multivariate covariates. This study is unusual by studying a task in humans that was initially designed for machines. We suggest systematically embedding AI benchmarks into the nomological network of human cognitive abilities to enable more systematic evaluation and interdisciplinary cooperation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：将规则归纳带回流体智力研究？ARC-AGI基准在人类中的初步验证
关于流体智力（gf）测量的两种竞争性观点认为，表现主要受限于工作记忆容量或诱导新关系的能力。第一种观点目前主导测量实践，体现在使用有限的重复规则；第二种观点虽在许多定义中有所体现，但在测量中罕见。ARC-AGI基准主要要求规则归纳，被提出作为人类和人工系统流体智力的测量工具。然而，其心理测量属性尚未在人类样本中检验。本研究首次在100名参与者中考察了ARC-AGI的心理测量特征及其范式网络。ARC-AGI题项组合表现出良好的心理测量属性，且与图式流体智力测试（ρ= .63）显著相关。与图式原创性关联较弱。这些发现为ARC-AGI作为人类流体智力测量工具的有效性提供了初步支持。未来研究应纳入更多规则归纳任务及额外多元协变量。本研究独特之处在于研究最初为机器设计的任务在人类中的表现。我们建议系统性地将AI基准嵌入人类认知能力的范式网络，以实现更系统的评估和跨学科合作。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether the ARC-AGI benchmark, originally designed for artificial intelligence, can serve as a valid measure of human fluid intelligence by testing its psychometric properties in 100 participants. Motivated by the debate over whether fluid intelligence is better captured by working memory or rule induction, the research focuses on the latter perspective. The ARC-AGI items demonstrated strong internal consistency and significantly correlated with figural fluid intelligence (ρ = .63), while showing weak links to figural originality. These results offer initial evidence supporting ARC-AGI’s validity as a human fluid intelligence measure and suggest its potential for integrating AI benchmarks into cognitive science research.
本研究旨在检验为人工智能设计的ARC-AGI基准是否适用于衡量人类流体智力，通过对100名参与者进行测试，评估其心理测量特性。研究动机源于对流体智力是受工作记忆还是规则归纳能力主导的争论，结果发现ARC-AGI与图式流体智力显著相关（ρ = .63），支持其有效性，但与图式原创性关联较弱，表明规则归纳可能是流体智力的更特异性成分。研究结果表明ARC-AGI是评估人类认知能力的有前景工具，并建议将AI基准系统纳入人类认知能力的理论网络中。
Polycepta: Object-Centric Appearance Estimation for Multi-Object Tracking
Authors: Mohamed Nagy, Naoufel Werghi, Jorge Dias, Majid Khonji
First: 2026-06-22T17:06:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:46:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The tracking-by-detection paradigm in multi-object tracking (MOT) typically relies on static appearance descriptors to complement motion estimation. However, these descriptors are frame-independent, limiting their robustness as visual cues. Since such descriptors are often obtained from computationally intensive pretrained backbones, real-time MOT systems frequently abandon appearance cues altogether and rely solely on motion prediction and geometric association. In this work, we introduce Polycepta, an object-centric appearance state estimation framework that reformulates appearance modeling as a recursive estimation problem rather than a frame-wise matching task. Polycepta constructs and continuously updates an independent appearance state for each tracked object, enabling future appearance representations to be estimated from accumulated observations. Polycepta is encouraged to learn the appearance-state construction of object-specific representations rather than memorize them through a proposed learning strategy, enabling appearance estimation for unseen classes. A key property of Polycepta is that the quality of appearance estimation improves as object states evolve during inference. While conventional appearance descriptors remain static or degrade over time, Polycepta progressively refines appearance estimates as additional observations are accumulated. Extensive experiments on KITTI, the Waymo Open Dataset, and MOT17 demonstrate consistent reductions in identity switches and improvements in tracking performance when integrated into the tracking-by-detection pipelines. Polycepta operates at 90.57 Hz and delivers state-of-the-art performance on the KITTI benchmark, achieving a MOTA of 92.27\%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Polycepta：面向多目标跟踪的以对象为中心的外观估计
多目标跟踪（MOT）中的检测跟踪范式通常依赖静态外观描述符来补充运动估计。然而，这些描述符帧间独立，限制了其作为视觉线索的鲁棒性。由于此类描述符常由计算密集型预训练骨干网络获得，实时MOT系统通常完全放弃外观线索，仅依赖运动预测和几何关联。本文提出Polycepta，一种以对象为中心的外观状态估计框架，将外观建模重构为递归估计问题而非帧间匹配任务。Polycepta为每个跟踪对象构建并持续更新独立的外观状态，使未来外观表示可从累积观测中估计。通过所提学习策略，Polycepta被鼓励学习对象特定表示的外观状态构建而非记忆，从而支持对未见类别的外观估计。Polycepta的关键特性是：在推理过程中，随着对象状态演化，外观估计质量逐步提升。与传统外观描述符保持静态或随时间退化不同，Polycepta在累积更多观测时逐步优化外观估计。在KITTI、Waymo开放数据集和MOT17上的大量实验表明，集成到检测跟踪管道中可稳定降低身份切换并提升跟踪性能。Polycepta运行频率达90.57 Hz，在KITTI基准上达到最先进性能，MOTA为92.27%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of static appearance descriptors in multi-object tracking, which hinder robustness and real-time performance, Polycepta introduces an object-centric appearance estimation framework that treats appearance modeling as a recursive state estimation problem. Instead of relying on frame-wise matching, it maintains and continuously updates individual appearance states for each tracked object, enabling progressive refinement of appearance representations through accumulated observations. This approach allows Polycepta to generalize to unseen classes without memorization, and its performance improves over time as object states evolve. Experimental results on KITTI, Waymo Open Dataset, and MOT17 show consistent reductions in identity switches and improved tracking accuracy, with Polycepta achieving a MOTA of 92.27% on KITTI while operating at 90.57 Hz.
为了解决传统多目标跟踪中静态外观描述符鲁棒性差、难以实时处理的问题，Polycepta提出一种以对象为中心的外观状态估计框架，将外观建模转化为递归状态估计问题。它为每个跟踪对象独立维护并持续更新外观状态，通过累积观测逐步优化外观表示，无需记忆特定外观，支持新类别。在KITTI、Waymo Open Dataset和MOT17上的实验表明，该方法显著降低身份切换率并提升跟踪性能，在KITTI上以90.57Hz的帧率实现92.27%的MOTA，达到当前最优水平。
VL-Nav: Neuro-Symbolic Reasoning-based Vision-Language Navigation
Authors: Yi Du, Taimeng Fu, Zhipeng Zhao, Shaoshu Su, Zitong Zhan, Qiwei Du, Zhuoqun Chen, Bowen Li, Chen Wang
First: 2025-02-02T21:44:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:46:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Navigating unseen, large-scale environments based on complex and abstract human instructions remains a formidable challenge for autonomous mobile robots. Addressing this requires robots to infer implicit semantics and efficiently explore large-scale task spaces. However, existing methods, ranging from end-to-end learning to foundation model-based modular architectures, often lack the capability to decompose complex tasks or employ efficient exploration strategies, leading to robot aimless wandering or target recognition failures. To address these limitations, we propose VL-Nav, a neuro-symbolic (NeSy) vision-language navigation system. The proposed system intertwines neural reasoning with symbolic guidance through two core components: (1) a NeSy task planner that leverages a symbolic 3D scene graph and image memory system to enhance the vision language models' (VLMs) neural reasoning capabilities for task decomposition and replanning; and (2) a NeSy exploration system that couples neural semantic cues with the symbolic heuristic function to efficiently gather the task-related information while minimizing unnecessary repeat travel during exploration. Validated on the DARPA TIAMAT Challenge navigation tasks, our system achieved an 83.4% success rate (SR) in indoor environments and 75% in outdoor scenarios. VL-Nav achieved an 86.3% SR in real-world experiments, including a challenging 483-meter run. Finally, we validate the system with complex instructions in a 3D multi-floor scenario.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VL-Nav：基于神经符号推理的视觉-语言导航
在复杂抽象的人类指令引导下，自主移动机器人在未知的大规模环境中导航仍是一项艰巨挑战。解决此问题需要机器人推断隐含语义并高效探索大规模任务空间。然而，现有方法，从端到端学习到基于基础模型的模块化架构，往往缺乏分解复杂任务或采用高效探索策略的能力，导致机器人无目标徘徊或目标识别失败。为解决这些局限，我们提出VL-Nav，一种神经符号（NeSy）视觉-语言导航系统。该系统通过两个核心组件将神经推理与符号引导结合：（1）NeSy任务规划器，利用符号3D场景图和图像记忆系统增强视觉语言模型（VLMs）的神经推理能力，实现任务分解与重规划；（2）NeSy探索系统，结合神经语义线索与符号启发函数，高效收集任务相关信息，同时减少探索过程中的无谓重复移动。在DARPA TIAMAT挑战导航任务中，系统在室内环境成功率达83.4%，室外为75%。在真实世界实验中，包括483米挑战路段，系统成功率达86.3%。最后，我们在3D多层复杂场景中验证了系统对复杂指令的处理能力。
Summary / 总结
To enable autonomous robots to navigate complex, unseen environments using abstract human instructions, VL-Nav introduces a neuro-symbolic vision-language navigation system that combines neural reasoning with symbolic planning and exploration. The method features a symbolic 3D scene graph and image memory to guide task decomposition and replanning, while a neuro-symbolic exploration module uses semantic cues and symbolic heuristics to minimize redundant travel. Evaluated on DARPA TIAMAT tasks and real-world trials, VL-Nav achieved 83.4% success rate indoors, 75% outdoors, and 86.3% in real-world tests including a 483-meter run, demonstrating robust performance on complex multi-floor navigation with natural language instructions.
为使自主机器人能在复杂未知环境中根据抽象人类指令导航，VL-Nav提出一种结合神经推理与符号规划的视觉-语言导航系统。该方法包含一个利用符号3D场景图和图像记忆的任务规划器，用于任务分解与重规划，以及一个融合神经语义线索与符号启发函数的探索模块，以减少冗余移动。在DARPA TIAMAT任务和真实世界测试中，系统在室内场景成功率达83.4%，室外75%，真实世界测试中达86.3%，包括483米长的挑战性路径，验证了其在多层3D环境复杂指令下的鲁棒性。
X-Lens: Real-Time Metric Depth Estimation with Heterogeneous Cameras
Authors: Heng Zhou, Shuhong Liu, Yonghao He, Bohao Zhang, Fa Fu, Chenhui Hou, Xianbao Hou, Lijun Han, Wei Sui
First: 2026-07-14T17:45:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:45:50+00:00
Comments: 24 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present X-lens, a compact feed-forward model for metric depth estimation from a variable number of calibrated fisheye and pinhole views. To support real-time downstream perception, X-lens is built around a geometry-aware heterogeneous camera formulation with two key components. Learnable calibration tokens provide a coarse alignment between fisheye and pinhole projective spaces, while a Jacobian-parameterized distortion bias injected into cross-attention models local projection changes and promotes cross-camera consistency, enabling robust generalization with only 0.04B parameters and up to 41 FPS. The model predicts dense depth together with a global metric scale, avoiding auxiliary reconstruction targets that increase computation and optimization complexity. To learn such cross-camera generalization at scale and depth, X-lens is trained on multiple public datasets and OmniScene, our newly released large-scale synthetic dataset containing approximately 266K synchronized six-view frames, 1.7M individual images, and 103 indoor and outdoor scenes. Extensive experiments on both real-world and synthetic indoor and outdoor datasets demonstrate superior heterogeneous-camera metric depth accuracy, reducing AbsRel by 25.4\% on OmniScene-Full over the strongest baseline while using 88.9\% fewer parameters, with competitive performance on conventional fisheye-only and pinhole-only settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：X-Lens：基于异构相机的实时度量深度估计
我们提出了X-Lens，一种用于从可变数量的校准鱼眼和针孔视图中进行度量深度估计的紧凑前馈模型。为支持实时下游感知，X-Lens基于一种几何感知的异构相机公式构建，包含两个关键组件：可学习校准标记提供鱼眼与针孔投影空间的粗略对齐；而注入到交叉注意力中的雅可比参数化畸变偏置则建模局部投影变化，促进跨相机一致性，仅用0.04B参数即可实现高达41 FPS的鲁棒泛化能力。该模型同时预测密集深度与全局度量尺度，避免使用增加计算与优化复杂度的辅助重建目标。为在大规模和深度上学习跨相机泛化能力，X-Lens在多个公开数据集及我们新发布的大型合成数据集OmniScene上进行训练，该数据集包含约26.6万个同步六视角帧、170万张独立图像及103个室内与室外场景。在真实世界与合成的室内室外数据集上的大量实验表明，X-Lens在异构相机度量深度估计方面表现优越，在OmniScene-Full上较最强基线降低AbsRel 25.4%，参数量减少88.9%，在传统鱼眼或针孔单一相机设置下亦保持竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time metric depth estimation across heterogeneous camera types, X-Lens introduces a compact feed-forward model that jointly processes fisheye and pinhole views using a geometry-aware formulation. It employs learnable calibration tokens to align projective spaces and injects Jacobian-parameterized distortion bias into cross-attention to enforce local projection consistency, enabling robust generalization with only 0.04B parameters and up to 41 FPS. Trained on multiple public datasets and a new large-scale synthetic dataset (OmniScene) with 266K synchronized six-view frames, X-Lens predicts dense depth and global metric scale without auxiliary targets. Experimental results show it reduces AbsRel by 25.4% on OmniScene-Full compared to the strongest baseline while using 88.9% fewer parameters, and maintains competitive performance on single-camera settings.
为实现异构相机下高效实时的度量深度估计，X-Lens提出一种紧凑的前馈模型，联合处理鱼眼与针孔相机视图，采用几何感知框架。模型通过可学习校准标记对齐投影空间，并在交叉注意力中注入雅可比参数化畸变偏置以增强局部投影一致性，仅用0.04B参数即可实现最高41 FPS的推理速度。模型在多个公开数据集及新合成数据集OmniScene（含26.6万组同步六视角帧）上训练，直接预测稠密深度与全局尺度，无需辅助重建目标。实验表明，其在OmniScene-Full上相较最强基线AbsRel降低25.4%，参数量减少88.9%，在单相机设置下亦保持竞争力。
SPECTRA: Context-Conditioned Spectral Movement Primitives for Robot Skill Generalization
Authors: Boxuan Zhang, Sheng Liu, Chenlin Ming, Ahmed Abdelrahman
First: 2026-07-08T04:02:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:44:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robot imitation learning for manipulation should preserve demonstrated task geometry while producing dynamically admissible robot motions. Existing pipelines often learn task-dependent trajectories and impose execution limits afterward through filtering, smoothing, clipping, or time scaling, which may distort task-critical end-effector paths. We propose the Spectral Movement Primitive (SMP), a frequency-domain imitation learning framework that couples task-space skill generation with joint-space execution regulation. Demonstrations are represented by truncated finite-horizon Fourier coefficients. An empirically selected low-frequency task band captures the dominant motion geometry, while higher harmonics contribute disproportionately to derivative growth. A frame-aware context-conditioned GMM/GMR prior predicts the task-band coefficients in a canonical task frame, and the resulting Cartesian trajectory is mapped to joint space through sequential inverse kinematics. A phase-coupled regulator then limits the requested phase progression without modifying the spectral coefficients, thereby enforcing joint velocity and acceleration limits while preserving the represented path. Experiments evaluate task-band reconstruction, robustness to composite demonstration corruption, out-of-distribution cross-board generalization, joint-space dynamic admissibility, end-effector path preservation, and deployment on a Franka Panda robot. Results show compact geometric reconstruction, consistent transfer across unseen task frames, substantial reductions in dynamic violations and jerk, and preservation of the intended end-effector path during phase regulation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SPECTRA：基于上下文条件的频谱运动原语用于机器人技能泛化
机器人操作模仿学习应保持演示任务的几何结构，同时生成动态可行的机器人运动。现有流程通常先学习任务依赖轨迹，再通过滤波、平滑、截断或时间缩放施加执行限制，这可能扭曲任务关键末端执行器路径。 我们提出频域模仿学习框架——频谱运动原语（SMP），将任务空间技能生成与关节空间执行调节耦合。演示通过截断的有限时域傅里叶系数表示。经经验选择的低频任务带捕捉主导运动几何，而高次谐波对导数增长贡献显著。帧感知的上下文条件GMM/GMR先验在规范任务帧中预测任务带系数，生成的笛卡尔轨迹通过顺序逆运动学映射至关节空间。相位耦合调节器随后限制请求的相位进展而不修改频谱系数，从而在保持路径的同时强制执行关节速度与加速度限制。 实验评估任务带重建、对复合演示污染的鲁棒性、跨板分布外泛化、关节空间动态可行性、末端执行器路径保持及在Franka Panda机器人上的部署。结果表明：几何结构紧凑重建、跨未见任务帧稳定迁移、显著降低动态违规与加速度突变、相位调节期间保持预期末端执行器路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to preserve task geometry while ensuring dynamically admissible robot motions, this work introduces Spectral Movement Primitives (SMP), a frequency-domain imitation learning method that separates task-space skill generation from joint-space execution regulation. Demonstrations are encoded as truncated Fourier coefficients, with low-frequency components capturing task geometry and higher harmonics influencing motion derivatives. A context-conditioned GMM/GMR prior predicts task-band coefficients in a canonical frame, which are then mapped to joint space via inverse kinematics. A phase-coupled regulator enforces joint velocity and acceleration limits without altering spectral content. Experiments show SMP achieves compact geometric reconstruction, robust generalization across unseen task frames, significant reductions in dynamic violations and jerk, and faithful preservation of end-effector paths during execution.
为在保持任务几何结构的同时确保机器人运动动态可执行，本文提出频域模仿学习框架Spectral Movement Primitives（SMP）。演示通过截断的有限时域傅里叶系数表示，低频分量捕捉主要运动几何，高频分量主导导数增长。一个上下文感知的GMM/GMR先验在标准任务坐标系中预测任务频段系数，再通过逆运动学映射到关节空间。相位耦合调节器限制相位进展而不改变频谱系数，从而强制执行关节速度与加速度限制。实验表明SMP实现紧凑几何重建、跨任务帧稳定迁移、显著降低动态违规与加速度抖动，并在执行过程中保持末端执行器路径不变。
ChunkFlow: Towards Continuity-Consistent Chunked Policy Learning
Authors: Zhao Yang, Yinan Shi, Mingyuan Yao, Wenyao Xue, Yawei Jueluo, Longjun Liu
First: 2026-07-14T17:43:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:43:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-language action (VLA) models increasingly adopt chunked action heads to satisfy real-time constraints; however, this introduces boundary jitter: overlapping regions between consecutive chunks often yield inconsistent predictions, degrading temporal coherence and the task success rate. Existing methods, such as inference-time blending, merely reweight mismatched proposals without correcting underlying errors, leading to residual accumulation under biased or noisy histories. We propose ChunkFlow, a seam-aware training-and-execution framework for chunked policies that aligns chunk structure with boundary execution. It partitions each chunk into frozen, editable, and future zones, applies deterministic overlap blending at execution, and trains raw predictions with seam and first- and second-order continuity losses. History corruption and scheduled sampling improve robustness to executed-history errors, while an AWAC fine-tuning stage adapts the policy without removing these structural regularizers. Under mild smoothness assumptions, pre-blending seam discrepancies provably decay with increasing overlap. Experiments on CALVIN, LIBERO, and real robots show an improved success-stability trade-off with low-latency inference. Project page: https://cytoderm-ai.github.io/chunkflow.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ChunkFlow：面向连续一致性分块策略学习
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型越来越多地采用分块动作头以满足实时约束；然而，这会引入边界抖动：连续分块之间的重叠区域常产生不一致的预测，降低时间连贯性和任务成功率。现有方法如推理时融合，仅重新加权不匹配的提案，而未修正底层错误，导致在偏置或噪声历史下残差累积。我们提出ChunkFlow，一种感知接缝的训练与执行框架，使分块结构与边界执行对齐。它将每个分块划分为冻结区、可编辑区和未来区，在执行时应用确定性重叠融合，并用接缝及一阶、二阶连续性损失训练原始预测。历史损坏与采样策略提升对已执行历史错误的鲁棒性，而AWAC微调阶段在保留这些结构正则化器的前提下适应策略。在温和平滑性假设下，预融合接缝差异随重叠增加而可证明衰减。在CALVIN、LIBERO及真实机器人上的实验表明，该方法在低延迟推理下改善了成功率-稳定性权衡。项目页面：https://cytoderm-ai.github.io/chunkflow。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the temporal inconsistency caused by boundary jitter in chunked vision-language action models, ChunkFlow introduces a seam-aware framework that aligns chunk structure with execution boundaries. It divides each chunk into frozen, editable, and future zones, applies deterministic overlap blending during execution, and trains raw predictions using seam-aware continuity losses. Experiments on CALVIN, LIBERO, and real robots demonstrate improved success-stability trade-off under low-latency inference, with provable decay of seam discrepancies under mild smoothness assumptions.
为解决分块视觉-语言动作模型因边界抖动导致的时间不一致性问题，ChunkFlow提出一种缝感知框架，使分块结构与执行边界对齐。每个分块被划分为冻结、可编辑和未来区域，在执行时应用确定性重叠融合，并通过缝感知连续性损失训练策略。在CALVIN、LIBERO和真实机器人上的实验表明，在低延迟约束下，该方法显著提升了成功率与稳定性之间的平衡，且在平滑性假设下，缝差异可证明随重叠度增加而衰减。
Growing a Tail: Increasing Output Diversity in Large Language Models
Authors: Michal Shur-Ofry, Bar Horowitz-Amsalem, Adir Rahamim, Yonatan Belinkov
First: 2024-11-05T10:52:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:36:11+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Machine Learning with Applications
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
How diverse are the outputs of large language models when diversity is desired? We examine the diversity of responses of several language models to questions with multiple possible answers, comparing them with human responses. Our findings suggest that models' responses are highly concentrated, reflecting narrow, mainstream outputs, in comparison to humans, whose responses exhibit a much longer-tail. We examine three simple and practical ways to increase output diversity: 1) increasing generation randomness via temperature sampling; 2) prompting models to answer from diverse perspectives using a single prompt; 3) aggregating outputs from several models. We find that these interventions, especially when combined, can substantially increase output diversity, although single-model outputs generally remain less diverse than the human baseline. We discuss potential implications of these findings for future work in AI policy and governance that wishes to preserve cultural diversity, an essential building block of a democratic social fabric.
中文标题/摘要
标题：长出尾巴：提升大语言模型输出多样性
当期望多样性时，大语言模型的输出有多多样？我们考察了多个语言模型对具有多种可能答案的问题的响应多样性，并与人类响应进行比较。研究发现，模型响应高度集中，反映出狭窄的主流输出，而人类响应则呈现出更长尾分布。我们考察了三种简单实用的方法以提升输出多样性：1）通过温度采样增加生成随机性；2）使用单一提示引导模型从多元视角作答；3）聚合多个模型的输出。我们发现，这些干预措施，特别是组合使用时，可显著提升输出多样性，尽管单模型输出通常仍不如人类基线多样。我们讨论了这些发现对未来希望保留文化多样性的AI政策与治理工作的潜在影响，文化多样性是民主社会结构的重要基石。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how diverse large language models are when generating answers to questions with multiple valid responses, finding that models produce highly concentrated, mainstream outputs compared to the broader, long-tailed diversity seen in human responses. To address this, the authors test three practical methods: increasing randomness via temperature sampling, prompting models to generate diverse perspectives from a single prompt, and aggregating outputs from multiple models. Results show that combining these methods significantly boosts output diversity, though even the best single-model approaches still fall short of human diversity levels. The findings suggest implications for AI governance, particularly in preserving cultural diversity as a key component of democratic societies.
该研究探讨了大语言模型在回答具有多种可能答案的问题时的输出多样性，发现模型倾向于生成高度集中、主流化的输出，而人类回答则呈现出更长尾、更广泛的多样性。为提升多样性，作者测试了三种实用方法：通过温度采样增加生成随机性、用单一提示引导模型从多元视角作答、以及聚合多个模型的输出。实验表明，组合使用这些方法可显著提升输出多样性，但即使最有效的单模型干预仍不及人类水平。研究结果对AI治理和文化多样性保护具有潜在意义。
Beyond Points: Spherical Distributional Part Prototypes for Interpretable Classification
Authors: Duarte Leão, Diogo Pereira Araújo, Catarina Barata, Carlos Santiago
First: 2026-06-25T22:16:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:34:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Prototype-based neural networks aim to provide intrinsic interpretability by grounding predictions in a small set of part prototypes. However, modern vision backbones typically operate in normalized, directional embedding spaces where each semantic part exhibits substantial intra-class variability. As a result, point prototypes often become redundant or unstable, hurting both explanation quality and robustness. We propose vMFProto, a distributional part-prototype framework that models each class as a mixture of von Mises-Fisher components on the hypersphere. Each prototype learns its own concentration, capturing part-specific variability, and we use entropic optimal transport (OT) to obtain structured patch-to-prototype assignments. A two-stage training schedule performs OT-driven prototype discovery followed by end-to-end refinement with patch-level distillation and distribution-aware diversity regularization. Experiments on CUB-200-2011, Stanford Dogs, and Stanford Cars with frozen DINO backbones show that vMFProto achieves state-of-the-art explanation quality (consistency, stability, and distinctiveness) with competitive accuracy. Qualitative results confirm that vMFProto yields localized, non-redundant part evidence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越点状原型：面向可解释分类的球面分布式部分原型
基于原型的神经网络旨在通过将预测与少量部分原型关联，提供内在可解释性。然而，现代视觉骨干网络通常在归一化方向嵌入空间中运行，其中每个语义部分表现出显著的类内变异性。因此，点状原型往往变得冗余或不稳定，损害解释质量和鲁棒性。我们提出vMFProto，一种分布式部分原型框架，将每个类别建模为超球面上的von Mises-Fisher混合分量。每个原型学习自身的浓度参数，捕捉部分特定的变异性，我们使用熵最优传输（OT）获得结构化的补丁到原型分配。两阶段训练方案先进行OT驱动的原型发现，再通过补丁级蒸馏和分布感知的多样性正则化进行端到端优化。在冻结DINO骨干网络的CUB-200-2011、Stanford Dogs和Stanford Cars数据集上的实验表明，vMFProto在解释质量（一致性、稳定性、独特性）上达到最先进水平，同时保持竞争力的准确率。定性结果证实，vMFProto生成局部化、非冗余的部分证据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability and redundancy of point-based prototypes in directional embedding spaces, the paper introduces vMFProto, a distributional prototype framework that models each class as a mixture of von Mises-Fisher components on the hypersphere. Each prototype learns its own concentration parameter to capture part-specific variability, and entropic optimal transport is used to assign patches to prototypes in a structured manner. Trained in two stages—first discovering prototypes via OT, then refining them with patch-level distillation and diversity regularization—vMFProto achieves state-of-the-art explanation quality (consistency, stability, distinctiveness) while maintaining competitive accuracy on CUB-200-2011, Stanford Dogs, and Stanford Cars with frozen DINO backbones, and produces localized, non-redundant part evidence in qualitative results.
为解决点状原型在方向性嵌入空间中易冗余和不稳定的问题，本文提出vMFProto框架，将每个类别建模为超球面上的von Mises-Fisher分布混合体，每个原型学习独立的浓度参数以捕捉部件特异性变异，并通过熵最优传输实现结构化补丁到原型的分配。采用两阶段训练：先进行OT驱动的原型发现，再结合补丁级蒸馏与分布感知多样性正则化进行端到端优化。在冻结DINO骨干网络的CUB-200-2011、Stanford Dogs和Stanford Cars数据集上，vMFProto在解释质量（一致性、稳定性、独特性）上达到最先进水平，同时保持竞争力的准确率，定性结果表明其生成局部化且非冗余的部件证据。
Controllable Generation of Diverse Dermatological Imagery for Fair and Efficient Malignancy Classification
Authors: Héctor Carrión, Narges Norouzi
Venue: MICCAI 2026
First: 2026-07-14T17:32:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:32:13+00:00
Comments: Accepted at MICCAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Accurate dermatological diagnosis naturally necessitates equitable performance across diverse populations, yet a systematic lack of expertly annotated images, especially for underrepresented skin tones and rare diseases, impedes progress toward measurably fair methods. We introduce cgDDI (Controllable Generation of Diverse Dermatological Imagery), a hybrid framework that (1) synthesizes realistic healthy skin samples without disturbing other input properties, (2) maps single-sample rare lesions onto novel skin-tones and locations non-parametrically, and (3) allows for efficient parametric generation with as few as 10 training samples. The framework supports both human and automated segmentation masking, enabling scalability to datasets without pre-made lesion masks. We grow a 656-image dataset by more than 400x and validate across two datasets: biopsy-confirmed Diverse Dermatology Images (DDI) and expert-verified Fitzpatrick17k (F17k). On the DDI benchmark, we achieve malignancy classification accuracy of 86.4% under synthetic-only training and 90.9% state-of-the-art performance with real data fine-tuning, alongside leading fairness metrics. Cross-dataset experiments show +13.9% accuracy improvements on unseen F17k data despite minimal disease overlap. We openly release 266k+ synthetic images, code, and generative models to further support fairness research at https://github.com/hectorcarrion/ControllableGenDDI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可控生成多样化的皮肤病影像以实现公平高效的恶性病变分类
准确的皮肤病诊断自然需要在不同人群中保持公平的性能表现，但专家标注的影像系统性匮乏，尤其是对肤色较少见群体和罕见疾病，阻碍了可衡量公平方法的发展。我们提出cgDDI（可控生成多样化的皮肤病影像），一种混合框架，可（1）在不干扰其他输入属性的情况下合成逼真的健康皮肤样本；（2）非参数化地将单样本罕见病变映射到新肤色和位置；（3）仅需10个训练样本即可实现高效的参数化生成。该框架支持人工与自动化分割掩码，可扩展至无预设病变掩码的数据集。我们通过该框架将656张图像数据集扩展超过400倍，并在两个数据集上进行验证：经活检确认的多样化皮肤病影像（DDI）和专家验证的Fitzpatrick17k（F17k）。在DDI基准测试中，仅使用合成数据训练时恶性病变分类准确率达86.4%，结合真实数据微调后达到90.9%的最先进性能，并伴随领先的公平性指标。跨数据集实验显示，即使疾病重叠极少，模型在未见的F17k数据上仍提升13.9%的准确率。我们公开发布26.6万+张合成图像、代码及生成模型，以进一步支持公平性研究：https://github.com/hectorcarrion/ControllableGenDDI。
Summary / 总结
To address the lack of diverse, expert-annotated dermatological images for fair malignancy classification, especially across underrepresented skin tones and rare diseases, the authors propose cgDDI, a hybrid generative framework that synthesizes realistic healthy skin, maps rare lesions to new skin tones and locations without altering other properties, and enables efficient generation from as few as 10 samples. The method supports both human and automated segmentation, allowing scalability to unlabeled datasets. Evaluated on DDI and F17k, cgDDI achieves 86.4% accuracy under synthetic-only training and 90.9% with real-data fine-tuning, outperforming prior methods in fairness and accuracy. Cross-dataset experiments show a 13.9% accuracy gain on unseen F17k data despite minimal disease overlap, demonstrating generalization. The authors release over 266k synthetic images, code, and models to support future fairness research.
为解决缺乏多样且专家标注的皮肤病图像，特别是对肤色和罕见疾病代表性不足的问题，作者提出cgDDI框架，该框架能合成真实健康皮肤、非参数映射罕见病灶至新肤色与位置，且仅需10个样本即可高效生成。该方法支持人工与自动分割掩码，便于扩展至无预标注数据集。在DDI和F17k两个基准上，仅使用合成数据时准确率达86.4%，结合真实数据微调后达90.9%，在公平性指标上领先，且在F17k未见数据上实现+13.9%的准确率提升，尽管疾病重叠度极低。
Win by Silence: Deletion Non-Monotonicity, Autonomous Exploitation, and Typed-State Gating in LLM Plan Evaluation
Authors: Aleh Manchuliantsau
First: 2026-07-14T17:29:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:29:28+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Plan evaluators can reward a strategic plan for becoming less explicit. This paper studies that failure in a staged expected-value scorer for LLM-generated venture routes. Proposition 1 gives the score change from deleting an interior transition while retargeting its predecessor and retaining downstream value: Delta_k = (prod_{i<k} p_i)[c_k + (1 - p_k)R_{k+1}]. On a frozen 26-route cohort, all 57 admissible deletions matched the analytic identity and threshold sign, and every route had at least one score-improving deletion. A score-seeking optimizer, allowed to restructure routes but not told the exploit mechanism, found baseline-beating uncovered structures in 21/26 routes. GATE refused score release for 26/26 silenced routes with 0/26 honest suspensions; after refusal, 47/54 next revisions repaired to a covered structure, and strict covered improvement rose from 1/26 to 13/26. An adaptive compiler-aware co-author exposed the registry-provenance boundary: obligation-channel evasions remained 6/6 across all four v1/v1.5 conditions, while delta-indexed cost floors reduced beat-honest routes from 6/6 to 3/6 and fundability-by-silence from 5/6 to 0/6 without establishing semantic completeness. If a plan scores better only because it omits necessary work, the plan did not improve; the evaluation created an omission incentive. PCSC detects and neutralizes post-hoc omission splices over model-mediated typed-state records. In the cooperative setting tested, GATE acts as a deterministic search-shaping constraint, not merely a post-hoc filter. It does not verify the semantic completeness or real-world quality of arbitrary LLM-generated strategies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：以沉默取胜：大语言模型计划评估中的删除非单调性、自主利用与类型状态门控
计划评估器可奖励策略因变得不那么明确而获得的收益。本文研究了在LLM生成的创业路线中，分阶段期望值评分器对此类失败的机制。命题1给出了删除中间转换并重新定向其前驱、保留下游价值时的评分变化：Delta_k = (prod_{i<k} p_i)[c_k + (1 - p_k)R_{k+1}]。在冻结的26条路线队列中，所有57个可接受的删除均符合分析恒等式且符号与阈值一致，每条路线至少存在一个评分提升的删除。允许重构路线但未告知利用机制的评分优化器，在21/26条路线中发现了超越基线的未被发现结构。GATE对26/26条沉默路线拒绝释放评分，且0/26条诚实暂停；拒绝后，54个后续修订中有47/54修复为覆盖结构，严格覆盖改进率从1/26升至13/26。一个自适应的编译器感知合著者揭示了注册-溯源边界：义务通道规避在所有四个v1/v1.5条件下仍保持6/6，而基于delta的代价下限将超越诚实路线从6/6降至3/6，沉默可融资路线从5/6降至0/6，但未建立语义完备性。若一计划仅因省略必要工作而评分更高，则该计划未真正改进，评估机制创造了省略激励。PCSC可检测并中和模型中介的类型状态记录中的事后省略拼接。在测试的协作场景中，GATE作为确定性搜索塑造约束发挥作用，而非仅事后过滤器。它不验证任意LLM生成策略的语义完备性或现实世界质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that LLM-generated plans can be strategically improved by omitting explicit steps, this paper investigates how deletion non-monotonicity affects plan evaluation. It introduces a staged expected-value scoring formula that quantifies score changes from deleting transitions while preserving downstream value. Experiments on 26 fixed routes show that every plan admits at least one score-improving deletion, and an optimizer autonomously discovers better structures in 21 of them. When GATE enforces silence by refusing score release, 47 out of 54 subsequent revisions recover to covered structures, increasing strict improvement from 1 to 13 routes. An adaptive co-author reveals that evasion of obligation channels persists, but cost floors reduce the number of beat-honest routes and silence-based fundability. The study concludes that omission incentives distort evaluation, and PCSC neutralizes such post-hoc splices using typed-state records, while GATE functions as a search-shaping constraint, not a semantic verifier.
该研究旨在揭示LLM生成计划因隐去必要步骤而获得更高评分的现象，进而分析其对评估机制的扭曲。作者提出一种分阶段期望值评分器，并推导出删除中间转移节点后评分变化的解析公式，该公式在26条固定路线的57次可删除操作中全部成立。一个未被告知机制的优化器在21/26条路线中发现超越基线的结构。当GATE因沉默拒绝评分后，54次后续修订中有47次修复为覆盖结构，严格改进路线从1/26增至13/26。一个自适应编译器感知的合作者揭示了注册溯源边界问题，成本下限使诚实路线从6/6降至3/6，沉默可融资路线从5/6降至0/6，但未实现语义完备性。研究指出，隐去必要步骤的计划并未真正改进，评估机制反而激励省略，PCSC可检测并中和模型中介的类型状态记录中的事后省略拼接，而GATE在协作场景中充当搜索塑造约束而非事后过滤器。
Resist and Update: Counterfactual Report Coordinates for Incentive-Compatible LLMs
Authors: Sen Yang, Yuen-Hei Yeung
First: 2026-07-14T17:28:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:28:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Aligned language models routinely misreport under non-evidential incentive pressure: they agree with a confident user or overstate certainty even when their internal belief is unchanged. We cast this as a failure of internal incentive-compatibility (IC) and present a method for learning and certifying counterfactual report mediators that hold a model's reports to a causal contract: invariant to forbidden influences (pressure, prestige, restyling) and responsive to licensed ones (genuine evidence). These two demands, resist and update, pull in opposite directions. We study them on a Bayesian-witness benchmark with known posteriors, in which the same user disagreement is licensed evidence or forbidden pressure purely by stated source reliability. We (i) causally identify, by interchange interventions rather than probe accuracy, low-rank report coordinates for answer, confidence, and caveat that are near-orthogonal and independently controllable, and (ii) introduce a training-free counterfactual report-coordinate (CRC) clamp that references the model's own report under a counterfactually incentive-neutralized context. On the witness benchmark the two-pass clamp attains resist and update of 1.00 jointly (Wilson 95% CI [0.99,1.00]), a causal certificate under a constructible reference, not a deployed solution. Global decoding and steering show a single-parameter tradeoff; output-level fine-tuning matches both objectives only when both are enumerated; resist-only training loses evidence-responsiveness. The deployable single-pass compilation is lossy (0.73/0.97). The mechanism and clamp reproduce across three model families and transfer to a natural sycophancy benchmark (SycophancyEval). Our contribution is the interface and certification method: activation-level counterfactual incentive-invariance as a structural primitive for internal IC.
中文标题/摘要
标题：抵抗与更新：反事实报告坐标用于激励相容的大语言模型
对齐的语言模型在非证据激励压力下常会虚假报告：即使其内部信念未变，也会对用户表示自信或夸大确定性。我们将此视为内部激励相容性（IC）的失败，并提出一种学习和认证反事实报告中介的方法，使模型报告遵循因果契约：对禁止影响（压力、声望、重写）保持不变，对许可影响（真实证据）保持响应。这两个需求——抵抗与更新——方向相反。我们在一个具有已知后验的贝叶斯见证基准上研究它们，其中同一用户分歧在纯由声明来源可靠性决定时，要么是许可证据，要么是禁止压力。我们（i）通过交换干预而非探查准确性，因果识别出答案、置信度和警示的低秩报告坐标，这些坐标近正交且可独立控制；（ii）引入一种无需训练的反事实报告坐标（CRC）钳制，其参考模型在反事实激励中立情境下的自身报告。在见证基准上，两阶段钳制同时实现抵抗与更新为1.00（威尔逊95%置信区间[0.99,1.00]），这是一种在可构造参考下的因果证书，而非部署方案。全局解码与引导显示单参数权衡；输出级微调仅在同时枚举两个目标时才能匹配两者；仅抵抗训练会丧失对证据的响应性。可部署的单阶段编译是损失性的（0.73/0.97）。该机制和钳制在三个模型家族中可复现，并可迁移至自然阿谀奉承基准（SycophancyEval）。我们的贡献是接口与认证方法：激活级反事实激励不变性作为内部IC的结构原语。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the tendency of aligned language models to misreport under non-evidential incentives, this work frames the issue as a failure of internal incentive compatibility and proposes learning counterfactual report mediators that enforce causal invariance against forbidden influences while remaining responsive to licensed evidence. The method identifies low-rank, near-orthogonal report coordinates for answer, confidence, and caveat via causal interventions, then introduces a training-free counterfactual report-coordinate clamp that references the model’s own report under incentive-neutralized contexts. On a Bayesian-witness benchmark, the two-pass clamp achieves perfect resist and update (1.00) with Wilson 95% CI [0.99,1.00], demonstrating causal certification. Single-parameter decoding or steering trade off the two objectives, while output-level fine-tuning can satisfy both only when both are explicitly enumerated; resist-only training loses evidence responsiveness. A deployable single-pass version is lossy (0.73/0.97), but the mechanism generalizes across three model families and transfers to a sycophancy benchmark.
为解决对齐语言模型在非证据激励下易误报的问题，本文将此视为内部激励相容性失效，并提出学习并验证反事实报告中介者，使其报告在禁止影响（如压力、声望、重写）下保持不变，而在许可影响（真实证据）下可响应。方法通过因果干预识别出答案、置信度和备注三个近正交且可独立控制的低秩报告坐标，并引入无需训练的反事实报告坐标钳制机制，参考模型在反事实激励中性情境下的报告。在贝叶斯见证基准测试中，双阶段钳制机制同时实现完美抵抗（1.00）与更新（1.00），并获得可构造参考下的因果认证。单参数解码或引导存在权衡，仅当同时指定两个目标时，输出层微调才能兼顾二者；仅训练抵抗机制会丧失对证据的响应能力。可部署的单阶段版本存在损失（0.73/0.97），但机制在三个模型家族中可复现，并可迁移至自然奉承性基准测试。
FormalAnalyticGeo: A Neural-Symbolic Based Framework for Multimodal Analytic Geometry Problem Generation
Authors: Ruoran Xu, Wending Gao, Qiufeng Wang
First: 2026-07-14T17:24:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:24:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Math reasoning has achieved significant progress with the rapid advancement of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), however analytic geometry remains largely underexplored, primarily due to the scarcity of annotated samples. Existing diagram generation approaches struggle with analytic geometry: template methods cannot handle constraint-driven layouts, and generative models lack the geometric precision to render annotated conic curves correctly. We present FormalAnalyticGeo, a scalable framework for fully automatic generation of multimodal analytic geometry problems. Leveraging the rigor of formal languages, we design the framework around CDL (Condition Description Language), a formal intermediate representation that bridges free-form problem text with precise diagram rendering via a Signed Distance Field (SDF) engine. The framework employs four specialized LLM components in sequence: a Generator that produces diverse analytic geometry problems, a Formalizer that converts each problem into CDL for SDF-based rendering, a Measurer that extracts ground-truth answers through vision-based measurement on the rendered diagrams, and a Quality Verifier that checks outputs at three stages. Structured feedback from the Quality Verifier drives automatic retry, forming a closed loop that eliminates any need for human annotation. Applying FormalAnalyticGeo at scale yields AnalyticGeo7K, a dataset of over 7K verified multimodal problems, each with aligned text, diagram, formal annotation, and ground truth.Experiments show that the generated problems achieve a median ground-truth relative error of 0.70\%, with 82.3\% of answers falling within 5\% of the exact symbolic solution. Our framework and dataset will be publicly released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FormalAnalyticGeo：一种基于神经符号的多模态解析几何问题生成框架
随着多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的快速发展，数学推理已取得显著进展，但解析几何仍被严重忽视，主要由于标注样本稀缺。现有图表生成方法在解析几何中表现不佳：模板方法无法处理约束驱动的布局，生成模型缺乏几何精度以正确绘制带标注的圆锥曲线。我们提出FormalAnalyticGeo，一种可扩展的框架，用于全自动生成多模态解析几何问题。该框架利用形式语言的严谨性，围绕CDL（条件描述语言）构建，CDL是一种形式化的中间表示，通过Signed Distance Field（SDF）引擎将自由形式问题文本与精确图表渲染连接起来。框架依次使用四个专用LLM组件：生成器生成多样化的解析几何问题，形式化器将每个问题转换为CDL以支持SDF渲染，测量器通过基于视觉的测量从渲染图表中提取真实答案，质量验证器在三个阶段检查输出。质量验证器的结构化反馈驱动自动重试，形成闭环，无需人工标注。大规模应用FormalAnalyticGeo生成AnalyticGeo7K数据集，包含7000多个经验证的多模态问题，每个问题均配有对齐的文本、图表、形式化标注和真实答案。实验表明，生成问题的中位真实相对误差为0.70%，82.3%的答案在精确符号解的5%范围内。我们的框架和数据集将公开发布。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for annotated analytic geometry problems to advance multimodal reasoning, FormalAnalyticGeo introduces a neural-symbolic framework that automatically generates multimodal problems using a formal intermediate representation called CDL. The system sequentially employs four LLM components: a Generator for problem creation, a Formalizer to translate problems into CDL for precise diagram rendering via SDF, a Measurer to extract ground-truth answers from rendered diagrams, and a Quality Verifier that iteratively refines outputs through structured feedback. Experimental results demonstrate the framework’s effectiveness, producing a 7K-problem dataset with a median ground-truth relative error of 0.70% and 82.3% of answers within 5% of the exact symbolic solution, without requiring human annotation.
为解决解析几何样本标注稀缺及现有图生成方法精度不足的问题，FormalAnalyticGeo提出一种神经符号框架，通过形式化中间表示CDL实现多模态问题的全自动生成。系统依次调用四个LLM组件：生成器创建问题、形式化器将问题转为CDL以驱动SDF渲染、测量器通过视觉提取真实答案、质量验证器通过结构化反馈迭代优化输出。实验表明，该框架生成7000+经验证问题，中位数真实相对误差为0.70%，82.3%的答案在精确符号解的5%误差范围内。
Sensing the properties of virtual objects without physical feedback
Authors: Rhoslyn Roebuck Williams, Harry J. Stroud, Luis E. Toledo, Mark D. Wonnacott, David R. Glowacki
First: 2026-07-14T17:21:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:21:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
People who have interacted with simulated worlds and simulated objects in extended reality (XR) often have a sense that they can 'feel' the objects being simulated despite them not being physical. Our sense of touch is essential for how we 'feel' the physical world, however, there is an open question as to what it means to 'feel' virtual objects when interacting with them in immersive digital environments. In prior research, we have reported that participants often describe a subjective experience of 'feeling' the properties of simulated molecular objects while using interactive molecular dynamics in extended reality (iMD-XR), a field-based interaction paradigm for manipulating real-time simulations of molecular objects without haptic feedback. To better understand these subjective reports of 'feeling', we used a psychophysics approach to quantify the threshold at which participants perceive differences in the rigidity of simulated molecular objects (C$_{60}$ molecules) in iMD-XR. To evaluate this, we carried out experiments to compare the just-noticeable differences (JNDs) in two conditions: (1) via direct interaction with a real-time C$_{60}$ simulation, and (2) via observation-only$\unicode{x2013}$i.e. watching another person interacting with the simulations. Our findings show that direct interaction enabled participants to perceive more subtle rigidity differences of 11.5%, compared to 18.5% for observation-only. Furthermore, participants who undertook interaction first were better able to distinguish rigidity differences in the subsequent observation-only condition, suggesting that interaction trained participants to better perceive differences in molecular properties. These findings demonstrate a novel and flexible approach for sensing the properties of virtual objects in XR, and offer new insights into iMD-XR's potential in molecular research and education.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需物理反馈感知虚拟物体的属性
在扩展现实（XR）环境中与模拟世界和模拟物体交互的人们常有一种感觉，即他们能‘感知’到这些非物理物体。然而，在沉浸式数字环境中与虚拟物体交互时，‘感知’虚拟物体的含义仍是一个开放性问题。在先前研究中，我们报告称，参与者在使用无触觉反馈的扩展现实分子动力学（iMD-XR）交互范式时，常主观描述能‘感知’模拟分子物体的属性。为更好地理解这些主观报告，我们采用心理物理学方法，量化参与者在iMD-XR中感知模拟分子物体（C₆₀分子）刚度差异的阈值。为此，我们进行了实验，比较两种条件下的最小可觉差（JNDs）：（1）直接交互实时C₆₀模拟；（2）仅观察他人交互模拟。结果表明，直接交互使参与者能感知11.5%的刚度细微差异，而仅观察者为18.5%。此外，先进行交互的参与者在后续仅观察条件下更能区分刚度差异，表明交互训练提升了参与者对分子属性差异的感知能力。这些发现展示了XR中感知虚拟物体属性的一种新颖且灵活的方法，并为iMD-XR在分子研究与教育中的潜力提供了新见解。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how users perceive the physical properties of virtual objects in extended reality (XR) without haptic feedback, motivated by the common subjective experience of 'feeling' simulated objects despite their lack of physicality. Using a psychophysical method, researchers measured just-noticeable differences (JNDs) in perceived rigidity of simulated C60 molecules under two conditions: direct interaction and observation-only. Results showed that direct interaction allowed participants to detect rigidity differences at a 11.5% threshold, significantly better than the 18.5% threshold for observation-only. Moreover, participants who interacted first performed better in subsequent observation tasks, indicating that interaction enhances perceptual sensitivity to virtual object properties. These findings suggest a novel approach for sensing virtual object properties in XR and highlight potential applications in molecular research and education.
该研究探讨了在无触觉反馈的扩展现实（XR）环境中，用户如何感知虚拟物体的物理属性，动机源于用户常主观感受到‘触摸’到模拟物体的现象。研究采用心理物理学方法，测量了在直接交互与仅观察两种条件下，参与者对模拟C60分子刚度差异的最小可觉差（JND）。结果表明，直接交互使参与者能感知11.5%的刚度差异，显著优于仅观察条件下的18.5%。此外，先进行交互的参与者在后续仅观察任务中表现更佳，说明交互训练提升了对虚拟物体属性的感知能力。这些发现提出了一种感知虚拟物体属性的新方法，并凸显了iMD-XR在分子研究与教育中的潜力。
Ensemble Controlled-Flow Filtering for Implicit Data Assimilation
Authors: Zhuoyuan Li, Yue Zhao, Ming Li
First: 2026-07-14T17:16:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:16:37+00:00
Comments: 26 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Data assimilation estimates the state of a dynamical system from model forecasts and incoming observations. Many observation mechanisms, however, are many-to-one, implicit, non-smooth, or accessible only through simulation, and need not provide the residual structures or likelihood guidance required by existing ensemble filters. We introduce implicit data assimilation, in which the analysis law is defined as an energy tilt of the forecast distribution. We then propose the Ensemble Controlled-flow Filter (EnCF), which realizes this update through a stochastic controlled flow and learns the observation-dependent control by adjoint matching from terminal energy gradients. For simulator-defined observations, EnCF-LF learns a surrogate conditional energy from samples and applies the same controlled-flow solver. We prove ideal exactness, derive a one-step error decomposition, and establish non-accumulation of local errors under filter stability. Numerical results show that Kalman-type filters remain preferable for smooth additive-Gaussian observations, while the proposed methods are better suited to non-Gaussian, many-to-one, multimodal, and implicit observation models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于隐式数据同化的大气集合控制流滤波器
数据同化通过模型预报和观测数据估计动力系统的状态。然而，许多观测机制是多对一、隐式、非光滑的，或仅可通过模拟获得，且无需提供现有集合滤波器所需的残差结构或似然指导。我们引入隐式数据同化，其中分析律定义为预报分布的能量倾斜。随后提出集合控制流滤波器（EnCF），通过随机控制流实现该更新，并通过终端能量梯度的伴随匹配学习观测依赖的控制。对于模拟器定义的观测，EnCF-LF从样本学习代理条件能量，并应用相同的控制流求解器。我们证明了理想精确性，推导了一步误差分解，并在滤波器稳定条件下建立了局部误差不累积的理论。数值结果表明，对于光滑加性高斯观测，卡尔曼型滤波器仍更优，而所提方法更适合非高斯、多对一、多模态和隐式观测模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional ensemble filters in handling implicit, non-smooth, and many-to-one observation models, this paper introduces implicit data assimilation via an energy tilt update and proposes the Ensemble Controlled-flow Filter (EnCF), which uses a stochastic controlled flow to implement the analysis law and learns observation-dependent controls via adjoint matching. For simulator-defined observations, EnCF-LF constructs a surrogate conditional energy from samples and applies the same controlled-flow solver. Theoretical analysis proves ideal exactness and non-accumulation of local errors under filter stability, while numerical experiments show that Kalman-type filters remain optimal for smooth Gaussian observations, but EnCF and EnCF-LF outperform them for non-Gaussian, multimodal, and implicit observation models.
为了解决传统集合滤波器在处理隐式、非光滑及多对一观测模型时的不足，本文提出通过预报分布的能量倾斜实现隐式数据同化，并设计了基于随机控制流的集合控制流滤波器（EnCF），通过伴随匹配学习观测依赖的控制。对于由模拟器定义的观测，EnCF-LF 从样本中构建条件能量代理并应用相同控制流求解器。理论分析证明其理想精确性与在滤波器稳定条件下局部误差不累积，数值实验表明，对于光滑高斯观测，卡尔曼型滤波器仍更优，而对于非高斯、多模态及隐式观测模型，EnCF 和 EnCF-LF 表现更佳。
Insights on Harmonic Tones from a Generative Music Experiment
Authors: Emmanuel Deruty, Maarten Grachten
First: 2025-06-08T10:25:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:07:42+00:00
Comments: 15th International Workshop on Machine Learning and Music, September 9, 2024, Vilnius, Lithuania
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The ultimate purpose of generative music AI is music production. The studio-lab, a social form within the art-science branch of cross-disciplinarity, is a way to advance music production with AI music models. During a studio-lab experiment involving researchers, music producers, and an AI model for music generating bass-like audio, it was observed that the producers used the model's output to convey two or more pitches with a single harmonic complex tone, which in turn revealed that the model had learned to generate structured and coherent simultaneous melodic lines using monophonic sequences of harmonic complex tones. These findings prompt a reconsideration of the long-standing debate on whether humans can perceive harmonics as distinct pitches and highlight how generative AI can not only enhance musical creativity but also contribute to a deeper understanding of music.
