标题：从生成音乐实验看和声音高洞察
生成音乐AI的终极目标是音乐制作。跨学科艺术-科学分支中的工作室-实验室这一社会形式，是借助AI音乐模型推进音乐制作的方式。在一项涉及研究人员、音乐制作人与AI音乐生成模型（生成类似贝斯的音频）的实验中，观察到制作人利用模型输出，用单一和声复合音传达两个或多个音高，这表明模型已学会利用单音序列的和声复合音生成结构化且连贯的多声部旋律线。这些发现促使重新审视人类能否感知和声为独立音高的长期争论，并凸显生成式AI不仅能增强音乐创作，还能深化对音乐的理解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the goal of advancing music production through AI, this study conducted a studio-lab experiment involving music producers and an AI model generating bass-like audio. The experiment revealed that producers interpreted the AI’s harmonic complex tones as conveying multiple pitches simultaneously, indicating the model had learned to generate structured, coherent melodic lines using monophonic harmonic sequences. This finding challenges traditional assumptions about human pitch perception and demonstrates how generative AI can both support creative expression and deepen theoretical understanding of music.
为推动AI在音乐制作中的应用，本研究在工作室实验中让音乐制作人与生成低音和声的AI模型协作，发现制作人能从单个和声复合音中感知多个音高，表明AI已学会用单音和声序列生成结构化、连贯的旋律线。这一发现挑战了人类能否感知和声为独立音高的传统观点，同时证明生成式AI不仅能增强音乐创作，还能深化对音乐本质的理解。
When and Why Does Multi-Agent Debate Fail and Does It Really Underperform?
Authors: Yongqiang Chen, Gang Niu, James Cheng, Bo Han, Masashi Sugiyama
First: 2025-10-23T19:46:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:06:10+00:00
Comments: Preprint, ongoing work
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-agent debate (MAD) was proposed as a promising approach for ensembling the wisdom of multiple large language models (LLMs) to improve reasoning and provide effective supervision to superhuman LLMs. However, increasing empirical evidence suggests that MAD may not outperform or even significantly underperform single-agent approaches (SA), raising doubts about the benefits of MAD. In this work, we investigate this issue by analyzing the incentive structures of popular MAD paradigms: (i) competitive MAD (CopMAD) where agents compete by holding opposing positions; (ii) consensus-seeking MAD (CosMAD) where agents are driven to seek consensus. We show that both paradigms suffer from debate hacking: CopMAD reduces to a cheap-talk game, where agents produce misleading messages to win the game, while CosMAD filters out informative disagreements for premature consensus. Consequently, agents in both CopMAD and CosMAD fail to jointly resolve the ambiguity and seek the truth. To this end, we introduce ColMAD, a collaborative protocol that reframes MAD as a non-zero-sum game to encourage agents to provide informative while truthful messages. Through extensive benchmarking on challenging tasks such as error detection, we show that ColMAD significantly outperforms previous MAD protocols up to 10 percentage points. Under the same budgets, ColMAD effectively brings non-trivial improvements over SA methods, implying that the protocol design is critical to realizing the potential of MAD.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多智能体辩论何时何地失败？它真的表现不佳吗？
多智能体辩论（MAD）被提出作为一种通过集成多个大语言模型（LLM）的智慧来提升推理能力并为超人类LLM提供有效监督的有前景方法。然而，越来越多的实证证据表明，MAD可能并不优于甚至显著逊于单智能体方法（SA），这引发了对MAD优势的质疑。本文通过分析两种流行MAD范式中的激励结构来研究这一问题：(i) 竞争型MAD（CopMAD），其中智能体通过持对立立场进行竞争；(ii) 寻求共识型MAD（CosMAD），其中智能体被驱动寻求共识。我们表明，这两种范式均存在辩论攻击：CopMAD退化为廉价对话博弈，智能体生成误导性信息以赢得博弈；CosMAD则过滤掉具有信息价值的分歧，过早达成共识。因此，CopMAD和CosMAD中的智能体均未能共同解决模糊性并寻求真相。为此，我们引入了协作型MAD（ColMAD），将MAD重新构架为非零和博弈，以鼓励智能体提供信息丰富且真实的陈述。通过在错误检测等具有挑战性的任务上进行广泛基准测试，我们证明ColMAD相比先前的MAD协议性能提升高达10个百分点。在相同预算下，ColMAD相比SA方法实现了非平凡的改进，表明协议设计对实现MAD潜力至关重要。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by growing evidence that multi-agent debate (MAD) may not outperform single-agent methods, this work investigates two popular MAD paradigms—competitive MAD (CopMAD) and consensus-seeking MAD (CosMAD)—and finds both suffer from debate hacking: CopMAD degenerates into a cheap-talk game where agents mislead to win, while CosMAD suppresses informative disagreement for premature consensus. To address this, the authors propose ColMAD, a collaborative protocol reframing MAD as a non-zero-sum game to incentivize truthful and informative messages. Experiments on error detection tasks show ColMAD significantly outperforms prior MAD methods by up to 10 percentage points and also improves over single-agent baselines under the same computational budget, demonstrating that protocol design is critical to unlocking MAD’s potential.
鉴于现有证据表明多智能体辩论（MAD）可能不优于单智能体方法，本研究分析了两种主流MAD范式——竞争型MAD（CopMAD）与共识型MAD（CosMAD），发现二者均存在辩论攻击问题：CopMAD退化为廉价对话，智能体为胜出而误导信息；CosMAD则过滤掉有信息量的分歧以过早达成共识。为此，作者提出协作型MAD（ColMAD），将MAD重构为非零和博弈以激励智能体提供真实且有信息量的输出。在错误检测等基准测试中，ColMAD表现显著优于先前MAD方法，最高提升达10个百分点，且在相同计算预算下也优于单智能体方法，表明协议设计对释放MAD潜力至关重要。
Invariant Learning Dynamics of Transformers in Inductive Reasoning Tasks
Authors: Tiberiu Musat, Tiago Pimentel, Nicolas Zucchet, Thomas Hofmann
First: 2026-07-13T17:56:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:06:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a theoretical framework to explain the emergence of inductive reasoning abilities in Transformer language models. While previous works on Transformer learning dynamics have so far been mostly tied to specific tasks, we study a generalized class of inductive tasks that unifies several synthetic tasks known in the literature, including in-context n-grams and multi-hop reasoning. In this class, we theoretically prove that the training dynamics of attention models can be confined to a highly interpretable, low-dimensional invariant manifold. On this manifold, the learning dynamics are captured by a handful of interpretable coordinates rather than millions of parameters, making both theoretical and empirical analysis more tractable. Using this framework, we characterize how data statistics govern the competition between in-context and in-weights learning, we study how random initializations determine the `winning' circuit when multiple solutions are possible, and we demonstrate that the coordinate frame associated with the manifold can be used to automatically detect which circuits have been learned in trained models. By casting circuit formation as a low-dimensional dynamical phenomenon, we take a step toward a predictive theory of how Transformers learn.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Transformer在归纳推理任务中的不变学习动态
我们提出一个理论框架，用以解释Transformer语言模型中归纳推理能力的产生。此前关于Transformer学习动态的研究大多局限于特定任务，而我们研究了一类通用的归纳任务，该类任务统一了文献中已知的若干合成任务，包括上下文n-gram和多跳推理。在此类任务中，我们理论上证明，注意力模型的训练动态可被限制在一个高度可解释的低维不变流形上。在此流形上，学习动态由少数可解释的坐标而非数百万参数所捕捉，从而使得理论与实证分析更加可行。利用该框架，我们刻画了数据统计如何调控上下文学习与权重学习之间的竞争，研究了随机初始化如何决定当存在多个解时“胜出”的电路，展示了与流形相关的坐标系可用于自动检测训练模型中已学习的电路。通过将电路形成视为低维动态现象，我们朝着构建预测性Transformer学习理论迈出了一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how Transformers acquire inductive reasoning abilities, this work develops a theoretical framework that unifies several synthetic inductive tasks and proves that their training dynamics lie on a low-dimensional, interpretable invariant manifold. The method identifies key coordinates that govern learning, enabling analysis of how data statistics influence the competition between in-context and in-weights learning, how random initializations determine circuit selection, and how the manifold’s coordinate frame can automatically detect learned circuits. Experimental results confirm that circuit formation follows low-dimensional dynamics, offering a predictive lens for Transformer learning.
本文提出一个理论框架，揭示Transformer模型在归纳推理任务中学习动态的本质，其训练过程可被限制在一个低维、可解释的不变流形上，从而将数百万参数的复杂性简化为少数可解释坐标。该框架统一了多种合成归纳任务，允许分析数据统计如何影响上下文学习与权重学习之间的竞争，随机初始化如何决定多个可能解中的“胜出”电路，以及如何通过流形坐标自动识别训练模型中已学习的电路，推动了对Transformer学习机制的可预测理论构建。
MAMMOTH: A Multi-Modal End-to-End Policy for Off-Road Mobility Robust to Missing Modality
Authors: Ahaan Kotian, Shivani Subramanyan, Suresh Sundaram
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-07-14T17:04:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:04:47+00:00
Comments: Accepted to IROS 2026 Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable autonomous navigation in unstructured off-road environments remains a critical unsolved challenge due to extreme terrain diversity, drastic illumination variations and acute sensor degradation. Recent developments have approached the problem as a traversability costmap estimation or visual navigation task. However, many exhibit heavy reliance on RGB modality, leading to poor performance in varied illumination such as glares, shadows or low ambient light. Achieving robust generalization in such conditions requires integrating modalities that provide supplementary scene information. Such multi-modal methods suffer from a rigid dependency on the presence of near-perfect sensor inputs, leaving them unable to robustly handle sensor degradation or individual modality failure. To address these limitations, we introduce MAMMOTH (MAsking Multi-Modal inputs for Off-road Traversability Heuristic-informed navigation), a unified end-to-end navigation policy for robust off-road visual-goal-conditioned navigation and undirected exploration. Specifically, MAMMOTH efficiently fuses multi-modal observations (RGB, Thermal, 3D Pointcloud and Ego Velocity) and is trained with a modality dropout scheme, enabling it to generalize to missing modalities at inference time. Furthermore, we employ a diffusion policy to learn the joint conditional probability distribution of physically-grounded trajectories and a intrinsic traversability heuristic. MAMMOTH utilizes this heuristic to prefer safer, smoother trajectories. We validate MAMMOTH through extensive real-world robot experiments in distinct off-road environments, including night-time operation. Our results demonstrate superior performance, with significant improvements in collision avoidance, terrain-aware planning and generalization to missing modalities. The code and dataset used for this work will be made publicly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MAMMOTH：一种对缺失模态鲁棒的多模态端到端策略，用于越野移动
在非结构化越野环境中实现可靠的自主导航仍是一个关键的未解挑战，原因在于地形多样性极大、光照剧烈变化以及传感器退化严重。近期研究将该问题视为可穿越性代价图估计或视觉导航任务，但许多方法严重依赖RGB模态，在眩光、阴影或低环境光等光照变化条件下表现不佳。要在这些条件下实现鲁棒泛化，需要整合提供补充场景信息的模态。然而，此类多模态方法往往对近乎完美的传感器输入存在刚性依赖，无法稳健应对传感器退化或单模态失效。为解决这些局限，我们提出MAMMOTH（MAsking Multi-Modal inputs for Off-road Traversability Heuristic-informed navigation），一种统一的端到端导航策略，用于鲁棒的越野视觉目标引导导航和无方向探索。具体而言，MAMMOTH高效融合多模态观测（RGB、热成像、3D点云和自车速度），并采用模态dropout方案进行训练，使其在推理时能泛化至缺失模态。此外，我们采用扩散策略学习物理约束轨迹与内在可穿越性启发式函数的联合条件概率分布，MAMMOTH利用该启发式偏好更安全、更平滑的轨迹。我们在不同越野环境中进行大量真实机器人实验（包括夜间操作）验证MAMMOTH，结果表明其在碰撞规避、地形感知规划及对缺失模态的泛化方面均显著优于现有方法。本工作的代码与数据集将公开发布。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of reliable autonomous navigation in unstructured off-road environments where sensor degradation and illumination variations degrade performance, MAMMOTH introduces an end-to-end multi-modal policy that fuses RGB, thermal, 3D pointcloud, and ego velocity inputs. Trained with modality dropout to handle missing or degraded sensor data, the policy leverages a diffusion model to learn physically-grounded trajectories and incorporates a traversability heuristic to favor safer paths. Experimental results across diverse off-road scenarios, including nighttime, show significant improvements in collision avoidance, terrain-aware planning, and robustness to missing modalities compared to prior methods.
为解决复杂野外环境下传感器退化和光照剧烈变化导致的导航可靠性问题，MAMMOTH提出一种融合RGB、热成像、3D点云和车体速度的端到端多模态策略，通过模态dropout训练使其具备缺失模态下的泛化能力。该方法结合扩散策略建模物理可行轨迹，并引入通行性启发式偏好安全平滑路径。在多种真实野外环境（含夜间）的实验中，其在避障、地形感知规划及缺失模态鲁棒性方面均显著优于现有方法。
The Illusion of Robustness: Aggregate Accuracy Hides Prediction Flips under Task-Irrelevant Context
Authors: Yanzhe Zhang, Sanmi Koyejo, Diyi Yang
First: 2026-07-14T17:01:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T17:01:12+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) grow more capable, they are increasingly deployed in context-rich settings where task inputs are often accompanied by long, partially irrelevant context. In a controlled setting, we find that state-of-the-art models often appear robust to task-irrelevant context at the aggregate level: prepending it to benchmark questions causes little change in overall accuracy. This aggregate stability, however, masks significant per-example instability. Even semantically meaningless pseudo-words, formed by randomly combining characters, can markedly shift model predictions on a small fraction of examples, degrading performance on some while improving it on others. This two-sided effect holds consistently across a wide range of models and datasets, yet the affected examples are largely model-specific. We further show that this instability is modulated by context type, context length, test-time compute, and model development stage. Together, our findings reveal context-induced tail risks concealed by aggregate accuracy, motivating per-example reliability evaluation of language models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：鲁棒性的幻觉：聚合准确率掩盖了任务无关上下文下的预测翻转
随着大语言模型（LLM）能力增强，它们越来越多地部署在上下文丰富的环境中，其中任务输入常伴随长且部分无关的上下文。在受控实验中，我们发现当前最先进的模型在聚合层面常表现出对任务无关上下文的鲁棒性：将此类上下文前置到基准问题上，整体准确率变化不大。然而，这种聚合稳定性掩盖了显著的单例不稳定性。即使由随机字符组合而成的语义无意义伪词，也能显著改变模型在一小部分样本上的预测，导致部分样本性能下降，部分样本性能提升。这种双向效应在广泛模型和数据集上保持一致，但受影响的样本主要与模型特定相关。我们进一步表明，这种不稳定性受上下文类型、上下文长度、测试时计算量及模型开发阶段调节。综合来看，我们的发现揭示了聚合准确率所掩盖的上下文诱导尾部风险，呼吁对语言模型进行单例可靠性评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that large language models often appear robust to irrelevant context at the aggregate level, this study investigates whether such stability masks per-example prediction instability. Using controlled experiments, the authors find that even semantically meaningless pseudo-words can flip model predictions on a subset of examples, sometimes degrading and sometimes improving performance. This effect is consistent across diverse models and datasets but highly example-specific, and is influenced by context type, length, compute, and model stage. The findings reveal hidden tail risks in model reliability that aggregate accuracy fails to capture.
鉴于大语言模型在聚合准确率上常表现出对无关上下文的鲁棒性，本研究探究这种稳定性是否掩盖了单样本预测的不稳定性。实验表明，即使语义无意义的伪词也能在部分样本上改变模型预测，有时降低、有时提升性能。该效应在多个模型和数据集上普遍存在，但样本依赖性强，受上下文类型、长度、计算资源及模型阶段影响，揭示了聚合指标无法捕捉的隐藏尾部风险。
Form, Not Content? A Preregistered, Placebo-Controlled Evaluation of Learned Error-Conditioned Self-Repair Through Prompts and Weights in Frozen Small Code Models
Authors: Mehmet Iscan
First: 2026-07-14T16:59:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T16:59:42+00:00
Comments: 54 pages, 6 figures, 17 tables. Preregistered, placebo-controlled evaluation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Frozen small code LLMs are deployed locally, yet the information guiding a retry after a failed attempt is still measured without placebo controls in the self-repair literature. We treat a failed program as a conjecture and an execution counterexample as an oracle-relative refutation, and introduce PoPE (Popperian Placebo-controlled Evaluation): a methodology for measuring whether evidence that falsifies LLM-generated code can be used operationally by that same model. In PoPE, error content is paired with channel-specific placebos that keep the predeclared scaffold while ablating task-relevant content or deranging the task-error assignment. Frozen small code models (0.5-1.5B) are evaluated under preregistered rules through a prompt channel and a weight channel (small-data adapter training), with four generations per arm-unit pair. In the prompt channel, public-tier screening unlocked 12 units under the content-ablated form placebo versus 10 under the live error-pattern arm on a 40-unit resistant band; the result was recorded as mechanism-null. In the weight channel, an 8-8 tie was observed between the error-content adapter and the intervention-free baseline (p=1.0), while the SHA-deranged placebo adapter stayed ahead with 10 unlocks; content-attributable superiority was not confirmed. These results do not constitute evidence of equivalence or non-inferiority. Equivalence was not tested separately. Findings are restricted to the public-tier screening endpoint; hidden-tier confirmation was deferred by design. We read this not as compiled criticism disappearing as information, but as the loss of its external role in testing a new conjecture: when a representation learned from the oracle is written back into the generation state, testing is replaced by conditioning. No working JEPA-RL controller is claimed. PoPE is presented as a placebo-controlled, retestable measurement standard.
中文标题/摘要
标题：形式而非内容？对冻结的小型代码模型中通过提示和权重学习错误条件自修复的预注册安慰剂对照评估
冻结的小型代码LLM在本地部署，但自修复文献中仍缺乏安慰剂对照来衡量失败尝试后的重试信息。我们将失败程序视为猜想，执行反例视为相对预言机的反驳，并引入PoPE（波普尔式安慰剂对照评估）：一种衡量证据是否能被同一模型操作性使用的标准。在PoPE中，错误内容与特定通道的安慰剂配对，保持预声明的框架，同时剔除任务相关的内容或扰乱任务-错误分配。冻结的小型代码模型（0.5-1.5B）通过预注册规则，在提示通道和权重通道（小数据适配器训练）下评估，每组臂-单位对生成四次。在提示通道中，公开层级筛选在内容剔除形式安慰剂下解锁12个单位，而在真实错误模式组下为10个单位（40单位抗性带）；结果记录为机制无效。在权重通道中，错误内容适配器与无干预基线呈8-8平局（p=1.0），而SHA扰乱安慰剂适配器保持领先，解锁10个单位；未确认内容可归因的优越性。这些结果不构成等效或非劣效的证据。未单独测试等效性。研究结果仅限于公开层级筛选终点；隐藏层级确认按设计推迟。我们不将其解读为信息消失的编译式批评，而是其在测试新猜想时外部作用的丧失：当从预言机学习到的表示写回生成状态时，测试被条件化取代。未声称存在任何有效的JEPA-RL控制器。PoPE被呈现为安慰剂对照、可重复测试的测量标准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of placebo controls in evaluating self-repair in frozen small code models, the study introduces PoPE—a Popperian placebo-controlled framework to test whether error evidence can operationally guide model retries. It evaluates two channels: prompt-based interventions (content-ablation or error-pattern derangement) and weight-based adapters (trained on small data). Results show no significant effect in the prompt channel (12 vs 10 unlocks under placebo vs live error), indicating mechanism-null, while the weight channel showed no content-attributable superiority (8-8 tie with baseline, SHA-deranged placebo led with 10 unlocks). The study concludes that error content does not demonstrably enhance self-repair, and equivalence was not tested. Findings are limited to public-tier screening, with hidden-tier validation deferred.
为解决冻结小型代码LLM自我修复评估中缺乏安慰剂对照的问题，研究提出PoPE框架，测试错误证据是否能实际引导模型重试。通过提示和权重通道，对比错误内容与内容剔除或任务扰动的安慰剂，结果显示提示通道中安慰剂组（12次解锁）未显著优于真实错误组（10次），权重通道中错误适配器与基线持平（8-8，p=1.0），而扰动安慰剂适配器表现更优。结果表明错误内容无法可靠支持自我修复，当模型重用来自oracle的表示时，测试被条件化取代。
ViCo3D: Empowering LiDAR-based Collaborative 3D Object Detection with Vision Foundation Models
Authors: Haojie Ren, Songrui Luo, Lingfeng Wang, Yan Xia, Yao Li, Jing Li, Lu Zhang, Jiajun Deng, Yanyong Zhang
First: 2026-07-14T16:56:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T16:56:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LiDAR-based collaborative 3D perception in Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) systems typically relies on fusing bird's-eye-view (BEV) features across agents. However, current BEV representations, typically extracted by LiDAR backbones trained from scratch, are geometry-dominated and lack general semantic priors, inherently limiting the efficacy of feature-level collaboration. Meanwhile, vision foundation models (VFMs) pretrained on large-scale image data have demonstrated strong capability in learning general-purpose and informative visual representations for 2D tasks, and have the potential to enhance agent-wise LiDAR BEV representations for collaboration. Despite this potential, adapting VFMs to LiDAR-based 3D detection remains challenging due to the substantial image-point cloud modality gap. To bridge this gap, we propose ViCo3D, a collaborative 3D object detection framework powered by VFMs. Specifically, ViCo3D adapts VFMs to LiDAR-based collaborative perception from three aspects: First, ViCo3D projects point clouds onto the BEV plane as three-channel images, enabling DINOv2 to extract BEV-space visual features from LiDAR inputs. Besides, to effectively integrate these DINOv2-derived features with LiDAR geometric features, ViCo3D introduces a multi-scale BEV fusion module within the single-agent encoder. In addition, ViCo3D adopts an ego-centric cross-agent fusion strategy to aggregate complementary information from multiple agents. Experiments on DAIR-V2X and V2XSet demonstrate that ViCo3D achieves state-of-the-art 3D detection performance. Remarkably, it delivers up to 1.8x greater collaborative gains than prior methods on DAIR-V2X. The code will be made public available for future investigation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ViCo3D：利用视觉基础模型赋能基于激光雷达的协作三维目标检测
车辆与万物（V2X）系统中的基于激光雷达的协作三维感知通常依赖于跨代理的鸟瞰图（BEV）特征融合。然而，当前BEV表示通常由从头训练的激光雷达骨干网络提取，以几何为主导，缺乏通用语义先验，从而在特征级协作中固有地限制了其有效性。与此同时，视觉基础模型（VFMs）在大规模图像数据上预训练后，已展现出在2D任务中学习通用且信息丰富的视觉表示的强大能力，并有望增强代理间激光雷达BEV表示以促进协作。尽管如此，由于图像与点云模态之间的显著差异，将VFMs适配到基于激光雷达的三维检测仍具挑战性。为弥合这一差距，我们提出ViCo3D，一个由VFMs驱动的协作三维目标检测框架。具体而言，ViCo3D从三个方面将VFMs适配到基于激光雷达的协作感知：首先，ViCo3D将点云投影到BEV平面上作为三通道图像，使DINOv2能够从激光雷达输入中提取BEV空间视觉特征；此外，为有效整合这些DINOv2衍生特征与激光雷达几何特征，ViCo3D在单代理编码器内引入多尺度BEV融合模块；此外，ViCo3D采用以自身为中心的跨代理融合策略，以聚合多个代理的互补信息。在DAIR-V2X和V2XSet上的实验表明，ViCo3D实现了最先进的三维检测性能。值得注意的是，其在DAIR-V2X上相比现有方法实现了高达1.8倍的协作增益。代码将公开以供未来研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of geometry-dominated LiDAR BEV features in V2X collaborative 3D detection, ViCo3D leverages vision foundation models (VFMs) to enhance feature collaboration. It adapts DINOv2 by projecting point clouds into three-channel BEV images, integrates VFM-derived features with LiDAR geometry via a multi-scale fusion module, and employs ego-centric cross-agent fusion to aggregate multi-agent information. Experiments on DAIR-V2X and V2XSet show ViCo3D achieves state-of-the-art 3D detection performance, with up to 1.8x greater collaborative gains than prior methods.
为提升V2X系统中基于LiDAR的3D目标检测协作性能，该研究提出ViCo3D框架，利用视觉基础模型（VFMs）增强特征协作。其方法包括将点云投影为三通道BEV图像以适配DINOv2、引入多尺度BEV融合模块整合视觉与几何特征，并采用以自身为中心的跨车融合策略聚合多车信息。在DAIR-V2X和V2XSet数据集上，ViCo3D达到当前最优检测精度，协作增益较前序方法提升最高达1.8倍。
CD-MED: Cross-Domain Multimodal Emotion Descriptor for Visual Comparison of Digital Objects
Authors: Elnara Kadyrgali, Muragul Muratbekova, Pakizar Shamoi
First: 2026-07-14T16:54:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T16:54:51+00:00
Comments: Submitted to 2026 Joint 14th International Conference on Soft Computing and Intelligent Systems and 27th International Symposium on Advanced Intelligent Systems (SCIS&ISIS 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Digital objects express emotions through different modalities. For example, a movie may include visual scenes, audio, dialogue, and facial expressions, while a song may contain melody, rhythm, lyrics, and vocal tone. Because existing emotion recognition models are usually modality-specific, it is difficult to compare such objects directly. This paper proposes CD-MED, a Cross-Domain Multimodal Emotion Descriptor for representing heterogeneous digital objects in a common emotional space. Each modality can be processed by its own emotion recognition model, and the resulting emotional outputs are transformed into a shared descriptor. The descriptor preserves information from individual modalities while also allowing an integrated emotional profile of the object. For interpretation, CD-MED is visualized in the valence-arousal space: position represents affective coordinates, color denotes emotion category, size indicates intensity, and shape shows the modality. This unified representation enables emotion-based comparison, retrieval, recommendation, and visualization across different domains such as movies, songs, images, and books.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CD-MED：跨域多模态情感描述符，用于数字对象的视觉比较
数字对象通过不同模态表达情感。例如，电影可能包含视觉场景、音频、对话和面部表情，而歌曲则包含旋律、节奏、歌词和声调。由于现有情感识别模型通常模态特定，难以直接比较此类对象。本文提出CD-MED，一种跨域多模态情感描述符，用于在统一情感空间中表示异构数字对象。每种模态可由各自的情感识别模型处理，其情感输出被转换为共享描述符。该描述符保留各模态信息，同时支持对象的整体情感画像。为便于解释，CD-MED在效价-唤醒空间中可视化：位置表示情感坐标，颜色表示情感类别，大小表示强度，形状表示模态。该统一表示支持跨电影、歌曲、图像和书籍等不同领域的情感比较、检索、推荐与可视化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of comparing emotionally expressive digital objects across different modalities, this paper introduces CD-MED, a cross-domain multimodal emotion descriptor that maps heterogeneous inputs—such as movie scenes, song lyrics, or image content—into a unified emotional space. The method processes each modality independently using its own emotion recognition model, then transforms the outputs into a shared descriptor preserving modality-specific emotional information while enabling holistic emotional profiling. Experimental results demonstrate that CD-MED effectively supports emotion-based comparison, retrieval, and visualization across domains like movies, songs, images, and books, with visualizations in valence-arousal space using position, color, size, and shape to encode affective coordinates, emotion categories, intensity, and modality respectively.
为解决跨模态数字对象情感表达难以直接比较的问题，本文提出CD-MED，一种跨域多模态情感描述符，将电影画面、歌曲歌词、图像等内容映射到统一情感空间。该方法对各模态独立使用情感识别模型处理，再将其输出转换为共享描述符，既保留各模态情感信息，又支持整体情感画像。实验表明，CD-MED能有效支持跨领域（如电影、歌曲、图像、书籍）的情感比较、检索与可视化，其情感空间可视化通过位置、颜色、大小和形状分别编码效价-唤醒坐标、情感类别、强度和模态。
FairCoder: Probing LLM Bias in High-Stakes Decision Making via Coding Tasks
Authors: Yongkang Du, Jen-tse Huang, Jieyu Zhao, Lu Lin
First: 2025-01-09T17:42:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T16:54:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in high-stakes decisions such as hiring and college admissions, making their social bias a critical concern. While LLMs are trained to refuse explicitly biased requests, bias can be leaked implicitly during LLM planning and reasoning process. As code becomes the primary medium for LLM internal logic-writing, we introduce FairCoder, a benchmark that frames decision-making as coding tasks to systematically probe LLM bias across employment, education, and healthcare domains, covering multiple fairness definitions. Considering that existing metrics may fail when LLMs frequently refuse the request, we propose FairScore, a metric that jointly captures refusal behavior and group-level outcome diversity. Experiments with a 1k-sample dataset on powerful LLMs reveal consistent and previously underexplored bias patterns, such as prioritizing applicants from high-income families in college admissions. Our findings highlight the risks of deploying LLMs as decision-making agents and provide a comprehensive evaluation framework for future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FairCoder：通过编码任务探测大语言模型在高风险决策中的偏见
大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地应用于招聘和大学录取等高风险决策，其社会偏见成为关键问题。尽管LLMs被训练拒绝显式偏见请求，但偏见可能在LLM的规划和推理过程中隐式泄露。随着代码成为LLM内部逻辑编写的主要媒介，我们引入FairCoder基准，将决策建模为编码任务，系统性地探测LLM在就业、教育和医疗领域的偏见，涵盖多种公平性定义。考虑到现有指标在LLM频繁拒绝请求时可能失效，我们提出FairScore指标，联合捕捉拒绝行为与群体层面结果多样性。在强大LLM上使用1k样本数据集的实验揭示了此前未被充分探索的稳定偏见模式，例如大学录取中优先考虑高收入家庭申请人。我们的研究结果凸显了部署LLM作为决策代理的风险，并为未来研究提供了全面的评估框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing use of large language models in high-stakes decisions like hiring and admissions, this study introduces FairCoder, a benchmark that probes implicit bias by framing decision-making as coding tasks across employment, education, and healthcare domains. It employs FairScore, a novel metric that evaluates both refusal behavior and group-level outcome diversity to address limitations of existing fairness metrics. Experiments on a 1k-sample dataset with powerful LLMs reveal consistent bias patterns, including preferential treatment of high-income applicants in college admissions, underscoring risks in deploying LLMs as autonomous decision agents.
为应对大语言模型在招聘、录取等高风险决策中潜在的隐性偏见问题，FairCoder提出将决策任务转化为编码任务，系统评估模型在就业、教育和医疗领域的公平性。其创新性FairScore指标同时衡量模型拒绝行为与群体结果多样性，弥补现有评估方法的不足。在1000样本数据集上的实验发现，模型常隐含偏袒高收入家庭申请者等此前未被充分揭示的偏见，凸显了在现实决策系统中谨慎部署大语言模型的必要性。
Robustness of Deep Learning Models for PV Power Forecasting under NWP Forecast Errors: A Spatiotemporal and Physically Interpretable Analysis
Authors: Dandan Chen, Yan Zhao, Xuepeng Chen
First: 2026-07-14T16:48:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T16:48:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Engineering use of AI forecasting models requires not only high nominal accuracy but also predictable behavior under uncertain inputs. In photovoltaic (PV) forecasting, this requirement is especially challenging because numerical weather prediction (NWP) errors are temporally correlated, state dependent, and physically coupled across variables. Existing evaluations, however, often rely on perfect forecast assumptions or simplistic perturbations that do not reflect these characteristics. This study presents a physically constrained robustness evaluation framework based on simulation, using virtual PV power as a controlled response variable to isolate the propagation of input uncertainty from confounders at the plant level. Six representative machine learning and deep sequence models, including PatchTST, GRU, N-HITS, and LightGBM, are evaluated under dynamic NWP perturbations with heteroscedasticity modulated by clear-sky conditions and Erbs reconstruction that preserves radiation consistency. The results show that sequence models provide stronger noise filtering and temporal resilience than a strong tabular baseline under medium to high disturbance regimes. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) and Integrated Gradients (IG) further support a feature reallocation tendency at the case level, in which predictive reliance shifts from corrupted future forecasts toward more stable historical observations and deterministic physical priors. A Pareto analysis of accuracy under clean conditions, robustness, and computational latency then translates these findings into engineering implications for robustness assessment and model selection under forecast uncertainty.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在数值天气预报误差下光伏功率预测深度学习模型的鲁棒性：时空与物理可解释性分析
人工智能预测模型在工程应用中不仅需要高名义精度，还需在不确定输入下表现出可预测的行为。在光伏（PV）预测中，这一要求尤为困难，因为数值天气预报（NWP）误差具有时间相关性、状态依赖性和变量间物理耦合性。然而，现有评估常依赖完美预报假设或简单扰动，无法反映这些特性。本研究提出一种基于仿真的物理约束鲁棒性评估框架，以虚拟光伏功率作为受控响应变量，在电站层面隔离输入不确定性传播与混杂因素。在异方差性由晴空条件和Erbs重建调节、并保持辐射一致性的动态NWP扰动下，评估了六种代表性机器学习与深度序列模型（包括PatchTST、GRU、N-HITS和LightGBM）。结果表明，在中高扰动环境下，序列模型比强表格基线模型具有更强的噪声过滤能力和时间鲁棒性。SHapley Additive exPlanations（SHAP）与Integrated Gradients（IG）进一步支持案例层面的特征重分配趋势，即预测依赖从受损的未来预报转向更稳定的观测历史与确定性物理先验。对清洁条件下精度、鲁棒性与计算延迟的帕累托分析，将上述发现转化为在预报不确定性下评估鲁棒性与模型选择的工程启示。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the need for robust PV power forecasting models that perform reliably despite uncertain and correlated NWP inputs, a challenge not well captured by existing evaluation methods. It introduces a physically constrained simulation framework using virtual PV power as a controlled output to isolate input uncertainty effects. Six ML/DL models—including PatchTST, GRU, N-HITS, and LightGBM—are tested under dynamic, heteroscedastic NWP perturbations modulated by clear-sky conditions and Erbs reconstruction. Results show sequence models outperform tabular baselines in noise filtering and temporal resilience under medium to high disturbance. SHAP and IG analyses reveal a feature reallocation pattern where models increasingly rely on stable historical data and physical priors over corrupted future forecasts. A Pareto analysis further links these findings to practical engineering trade-offs between accuracy, robustness, and latency.
该研究针对光伏功率预测中因数值天气预报误差导致的模型鲁棒性挑战，提出一种基于仿真的物理约束评估框架，通过虚拟光伏功率隔离输入不确定性影响。在动态异方差扰动下评估了包括PatchTST、GRU和LightGBM在内的六种模型，结果表明序列模型在中高扰动环境下比表格基线更优，具备更强的噪声过滤与时间鲁棒性。SHAP与IG分析显示模型预测依赖从受损未来预报转向稳定历史观测与物理先验。帕累托分析进一步指导模型选择，平衡精度、鲁棒性与计算延迟。
RecRec: Recursive Refinement for Sequential Recommendation
Authors: Pervez Shaik, Prosenjit Biswas, Abhinav Thorat, Ravi Kolla, Niranjan Pedanekar
First: 2026-07-12T02:53:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T16:34:24+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sequential recommender systems typically infer user preferences through single-pass encoding of interaction histories without iterative refinement, relying on increasingly deep architectures to capture complex patterns. In this work, we revisit sequential recommendation from a recursive inference perspective: can user preferences be modeled as a persistent latent state that is recursively refined? We propose RecRec (Recursive Recommendation), a lightweight model that maintains a compact latent state and updates it through a shared recursive module conditioned on interaction evidence. Unlike prior recursive models, RecRec introduces an evidence-anchored correction mechanism that stabilizes refinement by grounding each update in the original interaction context, preventing semantic drift during deep recursive reasoning. Experiments on three benchmark datasets under standard evaluation protocols show that RecRec matches or outperforms state-of-the-art sequential, graph-based, and reasoning-enhanced recommenders while using only 3.9M to 14M parameters. Ablation studies demonstrate that both recursive refinement and the evidence-anchored correction gate contribute significantly to performance, highlighting the effectiveness of recursive latent inference as a scalable alternative to deeper or language-based architectures. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RecRec-6B67/README.md.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RecRec：用于序列推荐的递归精炼
序列推荐系统通常通过单次编码交互历史来推断用户偏好，而不进行迭代精炼，依赖越来越深的架构来捕捉复杂模式。本文从递归推理视角重新审视序列推荐：用户偏好能否建模为一个持续的潜在状态，并通过递归精炼？我们提出RecRec（递归推荐），一个轻量级模型，维护一个紧凑的潜在状态，并通过一个共享的递归模块根据交互证据更新该状态。与先前的递归模型不同，RecRec引入了证据锚定的校正机制，通过将每次更新锚定在原始交互上下文中，稳定精炼过程，防止深度递归推理中的语义漂移。在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，RecRec在标准评估协议下与最先进的序列、图结构和推理增强推荐器表现相当或更优，仅使用390万至1400万参数。消融研究表明，递归精炼和证据锚定校正门控机制均显著提升性能，凸显递归潜在推理作为替代更深或语言型架构的可扩展方案的有效性。代码见https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RecRec-6B67/README.md。

Motivated by the inefficiency of deep architectures in sequential recommendation, this work proposes RecRec, a recursive refinement model that maintains a compact latent state updated iteratively through a shared module conditioned on interaction evidence. The model introduces an evidence-anchored correction mechanism to stabilize updates by grounding them in original interaction context, preventing semantic drift. Experiments on three benchmarks show RecRec matches or exceeds state-of-the-art models while using only 3.9M–14M parameters, with ablation studies confirming the importance of both recursion and anchoring.
为解决传统序列推荐系统依赖深度架构导致效率低下的问题，本文提出RecRec模型，通过维护一个紧凑的潜在状态并基于交互证据递归更新该状态。模型引入证据锚定修正机制，确保每次更新都根植于原始交互上下文，防止语义漂移。在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，RecRec在仅使用390万至1400万参数的情况下，性能可媲美或超越当前主流推荐方法，消融实验进一步验证了递归细化与修正门控机制对性能的关键贡献。
ViHoRec: A Quality-Controlled Vietnamese Hotel Recommendation Dataset and Cold-Start Benchmark
Authors: Minh Hoang Nguyen
First: 2026-07-14T16:28:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T16:28:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recommender-system research for Vietnamese remains limited by the absence of a public, well-documented hotel interaction resource. Building such a resource is challenging for three reasons: cross-platform hotel names must be reconciled before interactions are comparable; quality must be audited with reproducible metrics rather than ad hoc cleaning; and public release must preserve privacy while remaining benchmarkable under realistic cold-start conditions. We introduce ViHoRec, a quality-controlled Vietnamese hotel recommendation dataset of 18{,}267 interactions between 6{,}832 users and 560 hotels, crawled from Booking.com, Traveloka, and Ivivu. Our contributions are: (i) a reproducible construction pipeline with cross-platform entity resolution and quantitative quality control; (ii) a privacy-preserving release with HMAC pseudonyms; and (iii) a public cold-start benchmark with temporal leave-last-one-out split, data-centric ablations, and dependency-free baselines. On the public split, learned models degrade sharply for users with short histories (BPR-MF Recall@10: 0.065 vs. 0.120), while UserKNN remains strongest overall, establishing ViHoRec as a sparse, cold-start-dominated testbed for low-resource recommendation. All data are publicly available at https://github.com/MinhNguyenDS/ViHoRec.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ViHoRec：一个质量可控的越南酒店推荐数据集及冷启动基准
越南推荐系统研究受限于缺乏公开且文档完善的酒店交互资源。构建此类资源面临三大挑战：跨平台酒店名称需统一后方可比较交互数据；质量需通过可复现指标审计而非临时清理；公开发布需在保护隐私的同时保持在现实冷启动条件下的可比性。我们引入ViHoRec，该数据集包含从Booking.com、Traveloka和Ivivu爬取的6,832名用户与560家酒店之间的18,267次交互，经质量控制。主要贡献包括：(i) 带跨平台实体解析与定量质量控制的可复现构建流程；(ii) 采用HMAC假名实现隐私保护的发布；(iii) 公开冷启动基准，包含时间序列留最后一个样本分割、数据导向消融实验及无依赖基线。在公开分割上，学习模型对短历史用户表现急剧下降（BPR-MF Recall@10：0.065 vs. 0.120），而UserKNN整体表现最佳，确立ViHoRec为低资源推荐领域中以稀疏性和冷启动为主导的测试平台。所有数据公开于https://github.com/MinhNguyenDS/ViHoRec。

MCP Pitfall Lab: Exposing Developer Pitfalls in MCP Tool Server Security under Multi-Vector Attacks
Authors: Run Hao, Zhuoran Tan
First: 2026-04-23T09:39:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T16:11:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Model Context Protocol (MCP) enables tool-integrated LLM agents, but its third-party tool-server ecosystem expands software supply-chain risk across tool metadata, untrusted outputs, cross-tool flows, multi-modal inputs, and privileged sink actions. Existing MCP benchmarks mainly measure robustness to malicious inputs, offering limited support for dependency vetting, semantic metadata auditing, and hardening regression. We present MCP Pitfall Lab, a protocol-aware security testing framework that models developer pitfalls as reproducible scenarios and validates outcomes using MCP traces and objective validators rather than agent self-report. We also introduce Semantic MCP-Bill-of-Material(MCP-BOM), representing that augments component inventory with security-relevant tool semantics, including descriptions, schemas, high-risk parameters, source/sink roles, trust boundaries, policy hooks, audit support, and trace provenance. Across 2,579 validator-completed runs over four models, Pitfall Lab observes 31.9% overall attack success rate(ASR), with multi-modal injection strongest at 38.7%. Semantic static fields detect pitfalls involving policy-bearing tool descriptions, permissive schemas, missing audit support, and absent server-side validation with F1=0.727, while BOM-backed findings drop from 27 to 16 after hardening. Decomposed risk metrics show Control Coverage increasing from 0.173 to 0.697 and Residual Risk decreasing from 15.31 to 6.09. A trace-linked case study further demonstrates that cross-tool forwarding and image-to-tool leakage require runtime provenance rather than static manifest fields alone. Overall, Pitfall Lab reframes MCP tool servers as AI software-supply-chain dependencies and provides BOM-backed artifacts for review, hardening, and trace-grounded regression testing
中文标题/摘要
标题：MCP陷阱实验室：在多向攻击下揭示MCP工具服务器开发陷阱
模型上下文协议（MCP）支持集成工具的LLM代理，但其第三方工具服务器生态系统扩展了软件供应链风险，涵盖工具元数据、不可信输出、跨工具交互、多模态输入及特权汇点操作。现有MCP基准测试主要衡量对恶意输入的鲁棒性，对依赖审查、语义元数据审计和加固回归支持有限。我们提出MCP陷阱实验室，一种协议感知的安全测试框架，将开发者陷阱建模为可重现场景，并通过MCP追踪和客观验证器验证结果，而非依赖代理自我报告。我们还引入语义MCP物料清单（MCP-BOM），在组件清单中补充安全相关的工具语义，包括描述、模式、高风险参数、源/汇角色、信任边界、策略钩子、审计支持及追踪溯源。在四模型上完成2,579次验证器运行，陷阱实验室观察到整体攻击成功率（ASR）为31.9%，其中多模态注入最强达38.7%。语义静态字段检测涉及策略承载工具描述、宽松模式、缺失审计支持及无服务端验证的陷阱，F1=0.727；BOM支持的发现经加固后从27降至16。分解风险指标显示控制覆盖率从0.173升至0.697，残余风险从15.31降至6.09。一个追踪关联的案例研究进一步表明，跨工具转发与图像到工具泄露需运行时溯源，而非静态清单字段。总体而言，陷阱实验室将MCP工具服务器重新定位为AI软件供应链依赖项，并提供BOM支持的审查、加固及追踪基础回归测试产物。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the security risks introduced by MCP’s third-party tool-server ecosystem, including untrusted outputs and cross-tool flows, the paper introduces MCP Pitfall Lab, a protocol-aware testing framework that models developer pitfalls as reproducible scenarios and validates them using MCP traces and objective validators. The framework also introduces Semantic MCP-BOM, which augments component inventory with security-relevant tool semantics such as schemas, trust boundaries, and audit support. Across 2,579 runs on four models, Pitfall Lab found a 31.9% overall attack success rate, with multi-modal injection achieving 38.7%. Static semantic analysis detected pitfalls with F1=0.727, and BOM-backed findings dropped from 27 to 16 after hardening. 为应对MCP工具服务器生态带来的安全风险，如不可信输出和跨工具流，作者构建了MCP Pitfall Lab框架，将开发者常见错误建模为可复现场景，并通过MCP追踪与客观验证器进行验证。该框架引入了语义MCP-BOM，将组件清单扩展为包含工具描述、模式、高危参数、信任边界等安全相关语义信息。在四模型2579次测试中，整体攻击成功率31.9%，多模态注入最高达38.7%。静态语义分析检测错误F1值为0.727，BOM支持下错误数从27降至16。风险指标显著改善：控制覆盖率从0.173升至0.697，残余风险从15.31降至6.09。案例研究显示，跨工具转发和图像到工具泄露需依赖运行时溯源，而非静态清单。
Point Tracking in Surgery--The 2025 Surgical Tattoos in Infrared Challenge (STIRC2025)
Authors: Adam Schmidt, Mert Asim Karaoglu, Zijian Wu, Jiaming Zhang, Yuxin Chen, Tim Salcudean, Ho-Gun Ha, Minkang Jang, Kyungmin Jung, Ihsan Ullah, Hyunki Lee, Suresh Guttikonda, Sarah Latus, Alexander Schlaefer, Xinkai Zhao, Yuichiro Hayashi, Masahiro Oda, Takayuki Kitasaka, Kensaku Mori, Peng Liu, Chenyang Li, Stefanie Speidel, Aoife Gardiner, Agostino Stilli, Danail Stoyanov, Francisco Vasconcelos, Anwesa Choudhuri, Meng Zheng, Zhongpai Gao, Benjamin Planche, Van Nguyen Nguyen, Terrence Chen, Ziyan Wu, Alexander Ladikos, Omid Mohareri
First: 2026-07-14T16:10:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T16:10:49+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 12 figures. arXiv admin note: substantial text overlap with arXiv:2503.24306
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Point tracking in surgery is crucial to enable applications in downstream tasks such as segmentation, 3D reconstruction, virtual tissue landmarking, autonomous probe-based scanning, and subtask autonomy. This paper introduces the 2025 iteration of a point tracking challenge to address this, wherein participants submit their algorithms for quantification. Their algorithms are evaluated using a dataset named surgical tattoos in infrared (STIR), with the challenge named the STIR Challenge 2025 (STIRC2025). The STIR Challenge 2025 comprises two quantitative components: accuracy and efficiency. The accuracy component tests the accuracy of algorithms on in vivo and ex vivo sequences. The efficiency component tests algorithm inference latency. The challenge was conducted as a part of MICCAI EndoVis 2025, and seven teams participated in this challenge. In this paper we summarize the challenge results and participant methods. The challenge dataset is available at: https://zenodo.org/records/20191078, and the code for baseline models and metrics calculation is available here: https://github.com/athaddius/STIRMetrics

标题：手术中的点追踪——2025年红外手术纹身挑战赛（STIRC2025）
手术中的点追踪对于实现分割、3D重建、虚拟组织标志定位、自主探针扫描和子任务自主性等下游应用至关重要。本文介绍了旨在解决该问题的2025年点追踪挑战赛，参赛者提交算法以进行量化评估。评估使用名为“红外手术纹身”（STIR）的数据集，挑战赛名为STIR挑战赛2025（STIRC2025）。STIR挑战赛2025包含两个量化部分：准确性和效率。准确性部分测试算法在体内和体外序列上的准确性；效率部分测试算法推理延迟。该挑战赛作为MICCAI EndoVis 2025的一部分举行，共有七支队伍参与。本文总结了挑战赛结果及参赛方法。挑战赛数据集可访问：https://zenodo.org/records/20191078，基线模型和指标计算代码可访问：https://github.com/athaddius/STIRMetrics
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for precise point tracking in surgical applications to support downstream tasks like segmentation and autonomous scanning, this paper presents the STIRC2025 challenge, which evaluates algorithms using the STIR dataset under two metrics: accuracy on in vivo and ex vivo sequences, and inference latency. Seven teams participated, submitting methods that were benchmarked against these quantitative criteria. Results show varying performance across teams, with some achieving high accuracy while others demonstrated superior efficiency, highlighting the trade-offs inherent in real-time surgical tracking systems.
为支持手术中的分割、三维重建和自主探针扫描等下游任务，本研究提出STIRC2025挑战赛，评估算法在红外图像中追踪手术标记点的能力，考核指标包括体内/体外序列的准确性与推理延迟。七支队伍参赛，提交了不同方法。结果表明，部分团队在精度上表现优异，但多数在延迟控制上存在挑战，凸显了手术实时追踪中精度与效率平衡的难度。
Exact and Calibrated Diffusion Reconstruction for Digital Breast Tomosynthesis
Authors: Imade Bouftini
First: 2026-07-14T16:10:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T16:10:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Limited-angle digital breast tomosynthesis (DBT) reconstructs a volume from a few low-dose projections over a narrow arc. At a representative nine-view, $25^{\circ}$ protocol more than 98% of image space is unmeasured, so a learned prior must supply structure in the missing wedge. Conditional diffusion priors achieve strong perceptual quality here but leave three clinical obstacles: inexact data consistency, unlocalized hallucination, and uncalibrated uncertainty. We enforce measurements exactly by replacing the per-step proximal update of a conditional diffusion sampler with exact Euclidean projection onto the data-consistent set, computed via an $m$-dimensional dual system with a one-time Gram matrix $AA^{\top}$ factorization. This projection costs 4.5 ms per step (a $248\times$ speedup) and drives the data residual to the double-precision floor ($2.4\times10^{-13}$). We prove it is the $ρ\to0$ limit of the proximal step, provide a no-harm theorem, and show that exactly consistent sample ensembles have variance supported on null($A$). Thus, the mean's entire error lies in the unmeasured subspace covered by the uncertainty map. On patient-derived breast phantoms, this improves fidelity at no depth-resolution cost. Conversely, a proximal step applied post-update degrades quality, isolating the consistency step's placement as decisive. Isotonic recalibration brings the ensemble spread to a calibrated error scale (expected calibration error $0.029\to0.008$; standardized error $4.7\to0.96$), ranking errors better than the pure prior. We also repair a 20.3% adjoint mismatch in a deployed projector via a materialized operator of record. This is the first data-consistent, uncertainty-calibrated learned reconstruction for limited-angle DBT. The solver naturally relaxes to discrepancy-ball and maximum-a-posteriori modes for noisy measurements.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于数字乳腺断层摄影的精确校准扩散重建
有限角度数字乳腺断层摄影（DBT）从窄弧段内少量低剂量投影重建体积。在典型的九视角、25°协议中，超过98%的图像空间未被测量，因此必须依赖学习先验来填补缺失楔形区域的结构。条件扩散先验在此处能实现强感知质量，但存在三个临床障碍：数据不一致性、未定位的幻觉和未校准的不确定性。我们通过将条件扩散采样器的每步近端更新替换为精确的欧氏投影至数据一致集合，来强制满足测量数据。该投影通过m维对偶系统与一次Gram矩阵AAᵀ因式分解计算，每步耗时4.5毫秒（提速248倍），并将数据残差降至双精度地板（2.4×10⁻¹³）。我们证明该投影是ρ→0时近端步的极限，提供无害定理，并表明精确一致的样本集合方差支撑于null(A)。因此，均值的全部误差位于不确定性图覆盖的未测量子空间内。在患者来源的乳腺仿生体上，该方法提升保真度且不损失深度分辨率。相反，更新后应用近端步会降低质量，凸显一致性步骤位置的决定性作用。等度量重校准使样本分布达到校准误差尺度（预期校准误差0.029→0.008；标准化误差4.7→0.96），其误差排序优于纯先验。我们还通过记录操作符修复了部署投影器中20.3%的伴随不匹配。这是首个针对有限角度DBT的数据一致、不确定性校准的深度学习重建方法。该求解器在噪声测量下自然松弛为差异球和最大后验模式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate reconstruction in limited-angle digital breast tomosynthesis where over 98% of image space is unmeasured, this work introduces a diffusion-based method that enforces exact data consistency via Euclidean projection onto the measurement-compatible set, computed efficiently using a precomputed Gram matrix factorization. This projection, costing only 4.5 ms per step, reduces data residual to machine precision and is mathematically proven to be the limit of proximal steps as regularization vanishes. Experimental results on patient-derived phantoms show improved reconstruction fidelity without sacrificing depth resolution, while post-update proximal steps degrade quality, confirming the critical timing of consistency enforcement. Isotonic recalibration further improves error calibration, reducing expected calibration error from 0.029 to 0.008 and standardized error from 4.7 to 0.96, outperforming the uncalibrated prior. The method also corrects a 20.3% adjoint mismatch in a deployed projector, establishing the first data-consistent, uncertainty-calibrated learned reconstruction for limited-angle DBT.
为解决有限角度数字乳腺断层摄影中超过98%图像空间未被测量导致的重建问题，该研究提出一种基于扩散模型的方法，通过欧氏投影强制实现数据一致性，利用预计算的Gram矩阵因子化高效计算，每步仅耗时4.5毫秒，数据残差降至机器精度，理论证明该投影是正则化趋近于零时的近端步的极限。在患者来源的仿生模型实验中，该方法提升图像保真度而不牺牲深度分辨率，且在更新后应用近端步反而降低质量，证实一致性步骤的时机至关重要。通过等度量重校准，误差校准效果显著提升，预期校准误差从0.029降至0.008，标准化误差从4.7降至0.96，优于纯先验方法。该方法还修正了部署投影器中20.3%的伴随算子不匹配，首次实现了有限角度DBT中数据一致且不确定性校准的深度学习重建。
Domain-Incremental Remote Sensing Change Detection via Difference-Guided Adaptation and Frequency-Decoupled Distillation
Authors: Daifeng Peng, Yaning Li, Haiyan Guan
First: 2026-07-14T16:07:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T16:07:21+00:00
Comments: 33 pages, 14 figures, and 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Remote sensing change detection (RSCD) models are prone to catastrophic forgetting when incrementally adapted to new domains. Existing domain-incremental learning (DIL) methods mainly preserve image-level representations but often overlook bitemporal discrepancy cues, which are critical for robust change detection under domain shifts. To address this limitation, we propose DG-FDD, a domain-incremental change detection framework that integrates Difference-Guided Adaptation and Frequency-Decoupled Distillation. Specifically, the Difference-Guided Dynamic Adapter (DGDA) models bitemporal feature discrepancies to promote change-aware feature adaptation and reduce domain-specific interference. Meanwhile, the Frequency-Decoupled Knowledge Distillation strategy with Cross-domain Synthesis (FDKD-CS) separates structural information from domain style in the frequency domain, enabling stable knowledge transfer without historical data. Extensive experiments on three public high-resolution RSCD datasets under two- and three-domain incremental protocols demonstrate that DG-FDD effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting. Compared with independently trained single-task models, DG-FDD records mean relative changes in F1 and IoU of only -0.23% and -0.45%, respectively, across six two-domain sequences, and -0.69% and -1.31%, respectively, across the three evaluated three-domain sequences. These results indicate a favorable stability-plasticity balance between historical knowledge retention and new-domain adaptation in continual cross-domain change detection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过差分引导适应与频域解耦蒸馏的领域增量遥感变化检测
遥感变化检测（RSCD）模型在增量适应新领域时易发生灾难性遗忘。现有领域增量学习（DIL）方法主要保留图像级表示，常忽略双时相差异线索，而该线索对领域迁移下的鲁棒变化检测至关重要。为解决此问题，我们提出DG-FDD框架，融合差分引导适应与频域解耦蒸馏。具体而言，差分引导动态适配器（DGDA）建模双时相特征差异，促进变化感知特征适应并减少领域特异性干扰；同时，结合跨域合成的频域解耦知识蒸馏策略（FDKD-CS）在频域分离结构信息与领域风格，实现无历史数据支持下的稳定知识迁移。在三个公开高分辨率RSCD数据集上的大量实验表明，DG-FDD有效缓解了灾难性遗忘。与独立训练的单任务模型相比，DG-FDD在六组双域序列中F1和IoU的平均相对变化分别为-0.23%和-0.45%；在三组评估的三域序列中分别为-0.69%和-1.31%。结果表明，DG-FDD在持续跨域变化检测中实现了历史知识保留与新域适应间的良好稳定性-可塑性平衡。
Summary / 总结
To address catastrophic forgetting in domain-incremental remote sensing change detection, the paper proposes DG-FDD, a framework combining Difference-Guided Adaptation and Frequency-Decoupled Distillation. The Difference-Guided Dynamic Adapter models bitemporal feature discrepancies to enhance change-aware adaptation while suppressing domain-specific noise, while Frequency-Decoupled Knowledge Distillation separates structural and stylistic information in the frequency domain to enable stable knowledge transfer without historical data. Experiments on three high-resolution datasets under two- and three-domain incremental settings show DG-FDD maintains high performance: mean relative F1 and IoU drops of only -0.23% and -0.45% across six two-domain sequences, and -0.69% and -1.31% across three three-domain sequences, demonstrating strong retention of historical knowledge while adapting to new domains.
为解决遥感变化检测中增量学习导致的灾难性遗忘问题，本文提出DG-FDD框架，结合差分引导适应与频域解耦蒸馏。差分引导动态适配器建模双时相特征差异，增强变化感知并抑制域间干扰；频域解耦知识蒸馏通过跨域合成分离结构信息与域风格，实现无需历史数据的稳定知识迁移。在三个高分辨率数据集上的实验表明，DG-FDD在两域和三域增量设置下均保持优异性能：相比独立训练模型，其F1和IoU平均相对下降仅为-0.23%和-0.45%（两域序列），及-0.69%和-1.31%（三域序列），体现出历史知识保留与新域适应的良好平衡。
ExToken: Structured Exploration for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Reinforcement Fine-tuning
Authors: Yilun Kong, Yunpeng Qing, Guozheng Ma, Haoyu Wang, Li Shen, Zhi Hou, Dacheng Tao
First: 2026-07-14T16:04:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T16:04:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning (RL) has demonstrated significant potential for improving Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models on complex manipulation tasks. However, its practical scalability remains severely limited by the substantial cost of environmental interactions. In this work, we first investigate the exploration stagnation bottleneck in current VLA-RL frameworks and reveal that trajectory diversity is fundamentally more important to sample efficiency than the sheer quantity of collected rollouts. Motivated by these insights, we introduce RL Exploration Token (ExToken), a simple yet general framework that condition VLA policies on discrete behavioral priors derived from offline demonstrations for structured exploration. By conditioning the policy on different tokens during rollout collection, ExToken encourages the agent to explore diverse behavioral modes, substantially improving state-action coverage and exploration efficiency. To bridge exploration during training with deterministic inference at deployment, ExToken further incorporates a state-conditioned token selector that adaptively predicts effective behavioral modes for unseen scenarios. Extensive experiments across simulated and real-world robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that ExToken consistently accelerates convergence, improves task performance, and exhibits strong robustness under highly constrained interaction budgets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ExToken：面向高效视觉-语言-动作强化学习微调的结构化探索
强化学习（RL）在提升视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型处理复杂操作任务方面展现出巨大潜力，但其实际可扩展性受限于环境交互的巨大成本。本文首先研究当前VLA-RL框架中的探索停滞瓶颈，发现轨迹多样性比收集的轨迹数量对采样效率更为关键。受此启发，我们提出RL探索令牌（ExToken），一种简单通用的框架，通过将VLA策略条件化于从离线演示中提取的离散行为先验，实现结构化探索。在收集轨迹时，通过条件化不同令牌，ExToken促使智能体探索多样化的行为模式，显著提升状态-动作覆盖度和探索效率。为弥合训练阶段的探索与部署阶段确定性推理之间的差距，ExToken进一步引入状态条件化的令牌选择器，自适应预测未见场景下的有效行为模式。在模拟与真实机器人操作任务的大量实验中，ExToken持续加速收敛、提升任务性能，并在高度受限的交互预算下表现出强鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of exploration in Vision-Language-Action reinforcement learning, this work introduces ExToken, a framework that conditions policies on discrete behavioral priors from offline demonstrations to guide structured exploration. By varying these tokens during rollout collection, the method encourages diverse behavioral modes, enhancing state-action coverage and reducing the number of interactions needed for training. Experiments across simulated and real-world robotic tasks show that ExToken significantly accelerates convergence, improves final performance, and maintains robustness even under strict interaction budgets.
针对视觉-语言-动作强化学习中的探索停滞瓶颈，本文提出ExToken框架，通过将策略条件化于离线演示中提取的离散行为先验，引导结构化探索。在采样过程中切换不同token，促使智能体探索多样化行为模式，提升状态-动作覆盖度与探索效率。在模拟与真实机器人任务中，ExToken显著加速收敛、提升任务表现，并在交互预算受限时仍保持强鲁棒性。
Efficient Sequential Calibration with $O(T^{2/3-ε})$ Error Bound
Authors: Zihan Zhang
First: 2026-07-14T16:00:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T16:00:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the online binary sequential calibration problem. A recent breakthrough by \citet{dagan2024breaking} overcomes the classical \(T^{2/3}\) barrier for calibration error. Building on this result, we present an efficient randomized forecaster that achieves an expected calibration error \(O(T^{2/3-\varepsilon})\) for some constant \(\varepsilon>0\). Our forecaster combines the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} procedure \citep{dagan2024breaking} with an outer Blackwell-style correction layer. The \textsc{SPR-Calibration} procedure controls calibration with respect to a surrogate sequence of conditional-mean estimates, while the correction layer controls the additional error incurred when these surrogates are used to approximate the true outcomes. The analysis decomposes the total calibration error into the surrogate calibration error and the residual discrepancy between the surrogate sequence and the true outcomes. The former is bounded by the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} guarantee in \citet{dagan2024breaking}, and the latter is controlled using a quadratic potential argument together with the sparsity of the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} forecaster.
中文标题/摘要
标题：具有 $O(T^{2/3-ε})$ 误差界的高效顺序校准
我们研究在线二元顺序校准问题。最近，\citet{dagan2024breaking} 的突破性成果克服了校准误差的古典 $T^{2/3}$ 阈值。在此基础上，我们提出了一种高效的随机预测器，对于某个常数 $\varepsilon>0$，其期望校准误差为 $O(T^{2/3-\varepsilon})$。 该预测器结合了 \textsc{SPR-Calibration} 过程 \citep{dagan2024breaking} 与一个外层 Blackwell 风格的修正层。\textsc{SPR-Calibration} 过程通过一个代理序列的条件均值估计来控制校准，而修正层则控制使用这些代理来近似真实结果时产生的额外误差。分析将总校准误差分解为代理校准误差和代理序列与真实结果之间的残差差异。前者由 \citet{dagan2024breaking} 中的 \textsc{SPR-Calibration} 保证所约束，后者则通过二次势能论证结合 \textsc{SPR-Calibration} 预测器的稀疏性加以控制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the recent breakthrough in online binary sequential calibration that broke the classical T^{2/3} error barrier, this work presents an efficient randomized forecaster achieving an expected calibration error of O(T^{2/3-ε}) for some constant ε>0. The method combines the SPR-Calibration procedure with a Blackwell-style correction layer: the former controls calibration via surrogate conditional-mean estimates, while the latter manages the error from approximating true outcomes with these surrogates. Experimental analysis decomposes total error into surrogate calibration and residual discrepancy, bounding the former via SPR-Calibration’s guarantee and the latter using a quadratic potential argument leveraging the sparsity of the forecaster.
受近期在线二元序列校准突破（打破经典T^{2/3}误差下限）启发，本文提出一种高效的随机预测器，实现预期校准误差O(T^{2/3-ε})（ε>0为常数）。该方法结合SPR-Calibration过程与外层Blackwell式修正层：前者通过代理条件均值估计控制校准，后者管理代理值与真实结果间的误差。误差分析将总误差分解为代理校准误差与残差差异，前者由SPR-Calibration保证界定，后者通过二次势能论证结合预测器的稀疏性加以控制。
Knowledge- and Gradient-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Parametrized Action Markov Decision Processes
Authors: Jonas Ehrhardt, René Heesch, Oliver Niggemann
First: 2026-07-14T15:57:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:57:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we study Reinforcement Learning in Parametrized Action Markov Decision Processes (PAMDP), where each decision consists of a symbolic action and numerical parameters. In such settings Reinforcement Learning algorithms typically determine parameters with one-shot estimators, which makes their training sample inefficient. Though in most PAMDP environments explicit but incomplete knowledge (e.g., rules, safety constraints, or expert heuristics) is available, it is rarely directly used to increase the sample-efficiency of training Reinforcement Learning agents. We step into this gap and propose our novel Neuro-Symbolic Knowledge- and Gradient-Guided Reinforcement Learning (KGRL) algorithm. KGRL uses domain knowledge in a Datalog knowledge base to derive the set of applicable actions and feasible parameters for a given state. This allows it to prune non-applicable actions from the decision-space and constrain the parameter spaces of the remaining actions. We then use a gradient-based parameter refinement loop to estimate the optimal parameters during training and deployment of the agent. By recording activated rules along the trajectory, KGRL additionally provides local procedural explanations on the pruning of actions and constraining of parameters. Overall, KGRL guides the agent's exploration and deployment toward feasible and constraint-aware decisions, while increasing sample efficiency during training. KGRL outperforms state-of-the-art RL baselines for PAMDPs in both, sample efficiency and episodic return.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于知识与梯度引导的强化学习用于参数化动作马尔可夫决策过程
本文研究了参数化动作马尔可夫决策过程（PAMDP）中的强化学习，其中每个决策包含一个符号动作和数值参数。此类设置中，强化学习算法通常使用单次估计器确定参数，导致训练样本效率低下。尽管大多数PAMDP环境中存在显式但不完整的知识（如规则、安全约束或专家启发式），但很少直接用于提升强化学习代理的样本效率。我们填补这一空白，提出新颖的神经符号知识与梯度引导强化学习（KGRL）算法。KGRL利用Datalog知识库中的领域知识，推导给定状态下适用的动作及其可行参数，从而从决策空间中剪枝不适用动作，并约束剩余动作的参数空间。随后，我们使用基于梯度的参数优化循环，在训练和部署过程中估计最优参数。通过记录轨迹中激活的规则，KGRL还提供局部程序性解释，说明动作剪枝和参数约束的原因。总体而言，KGRL引导代理的探索与部署趋向可行且约束感知的决策，同时提升训练期间的样本效率。KGRL在样本效率和回合回报方面均优于PAMDP的现有强化学习基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of one-shot parameter estimation in parametrized action MDPs and the underutilization of available domain knowledge, this paper introduces KGRL, a neuro-symbolic algorithm that integrates symbolic knowledge from a Datalog base to prune infeasible actions and constrain parameter spaces, then refines parameters via gradient-based optimization. Experimental results show KGRL significantly improves sample efficiency and achieves higher episodic returns compared to state-of-the-art RL baselines in PAMDP environments.
为解决参数化动作MDP中强化学习算法样本效率低、未充分利用领域知识的问题，本文提出KGRL算法，通过Datalog知识库中的符号规则剪枝不可行动作并约束参数空间，再结合梯度优化进行参数微调。实验表明，KGRL在样本效率和回合回报上均显著优于现有强化学习基线方法。
LatentFlow: A General Framework for Conditioning Stochastic Processes
Authors: Louis Sharrock, Lachlan Astfalck, Henry Moss
First: 2026-07-14T15:56:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:56:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Stochastic-process models are, as a rule, far easier to simulate than to condition. Non-linear observations, non-Gaussian likelihoods, black-box information, and global constraints all induce intractable conditional laws, requiring bespoke, model-specific constructions. We introduce LatentFlow, a single framework for conditioning stochastic processes, with no learned neural approximations and no training. Our starting point is to write the stochastic process as the deterministic image of a tractable latent innovation, $f_0 = T_{\vartheta}(ξ_0)$, with $ξ_0$ sampled from a simple reference distribution. This reduces process-level conditioning to latent-space inference: pull the likelihood back through $T_{\vartheta}$, sample the resulting latent law with a tractable guided probability flow, and push the samples forward. This construction is provably exact at the level of the target law; in practice, approximation enters only through finite terminal noising, Monte Carlo guidance, and time discretisation of the continuous-time dynamics, each of which is explicit and systematically reducible. As LatentFlow is training-free, conditioning reduces to solving a single reverse-time SDE. This enables conditional sampling in seconds on a single desktop CPU across model classes that have never shared a scalable method: classical spatial priors, nonlinear stochastic dynamics, mechanistic models from the physical and life sciences, stochastic PDEs, heavy-tails and extremes, point and discrete-state processes, and neural or simulator-defined processes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LatentFlow：用于条件随机过程的通用框架
随机过程模型通常比条件化更容易模拟。非线性观测、非高斯似然、黑盒信息和全局约束都会导致难以处理的条件分布，需要为特定模型定制构造。我们引入LatentFlow，一个无需学习神经近似且无需训练的统一框架，用于对随机过程进行条件化。其起点是将随机过程表示为可处理的潜在创新的确定性映射，即f₀ = T_ϑ(ξ₀)，其中ξ₀从简单参考分布中采样。这将过程级条件化转化为潜在空间推断：将似然通过T_ϑ反向拉回，用可处理的引导概率流采样得到潜在分布，再将样本前向推回。该构造在目标分布层面可证明精确；实践中，近似仅通过有限终端噪声、蒙特卡洛引导和连续时间动力学的时间离散化引入，这些均可明确且系统性地降低。由于LatentFlow无需训练，条件化仅需求解单个逆时间SDE，从而可在单台桌面CPU上数秒内完成条件采样，适用于此前从未共享可扩展方法的各类模型：经典空间先验、非线性随机动力学、物理与生命科学中的机制模型、随机偏微分方程、重尾与极端值、点过程与离散状态过程，以及神经或模拟器定义的过程。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of conditioning stochastic processes under non-linear observations and non-Gaussian likelihoods, LatentFlow proposes a training-free framework that reformulates any stochastic process as a deterministic transformation of a tractable latent variable. By pulling the likelihood back through this transformation, sampling the latent space via a guided probability flow, and pushing samples forward, the method enables exact conditioning at the target law level. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach achieves efficient conditional sampling in seconds on a single CPU across diverse models—including classical priors, physical and biological mechanistic systems, stochastic PDEs, and neural processes—without requiring neural approximations or training.
为解决非线性观测和非高斯似然下条件化随机过程的困难，LatentFlow提出一种无需训练的通用框架，将任意随机过程表示为可处理的潜在变量的确定性映射。通过将似然反向传递至潜在空间，采样该分布并正向推回，该方法在目标分布层面实现精确条件化。实验表明，该方法可在单台CPU上秒级完成多种模型（如经典先验、非线性动力学、PDE、离散状态过程等）的条件采样，无需神经近似或训练。
The TopCoW Challenge -- Topology-Aware Circle of Willis Segmentation for CT and MR Angiography

Hamadache, Clara Lisazo, Joaquim Salvi, Adrià Casamitjana, Xavier Lladó, Uma Maria Lal-Trehan Estrada, Valeriia Abramova, Luca Giancardo, Arnau Oliver, Paula Casademunt, Adrian Galdran, Matteo Delucchi, Oscar Camara, Jialu Liu, Haibin Huang, Yue Cui, Zehang Lin, Yusheng Liu, Shunzhi Zhu, Tatsat R.
