Comments: Summary paper for the TopCoW Challenge: 4 figures, 1 table, and supplementary material in appendix. Accepted for publication in NEJM AI. Datasets and best-performing algorithm Dockers are available at https://zenodo.org/records/15692630 and https://zenodo.org/records/15665435
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Circle of Willis (CoW) is an important network of arteries connecting major circulations of the brain. Its vascular architecture is believed to influence the risk, severity, and outcome of serious neurovascular diseases. However, characterizing the highly variable CoW anatomy remains a manual and time-consuming expert task. The CoW is commonly imaged by two non-invasive angiographic imaging modalities, magnetic resonance angiography (MRA) and computed tomography angiography (CTA), yet few datasets with annotated CoW anatomy exist, and there have been no established benchmarks for comparing CoW segmentation algorithms. We organized the TopCoW benchmark challenge alongside the release of an annotated CoW dataset with 125 paired MRA and CTA scans from the same patients. Voxel-level annotations for 13 vessel components were created using virtual reality technology and verified by clinical experts. Participants submitted algorithms for CoW segmentation and variant classification, which we evaluated on internal and external test sets comprising 226 scans from over five centers. The benchmark includes voxel-level segmentation, CoW component detection, CoW variant classification, and two clinical application tasks. We received submissions from over 250 participants across six continents. Top-performing teams achieved over 90% Dice scores for CoW segmentation, over 80% F1 scores for detecting key vessel components, and over 70% balanced accuracy in CoW variant classification across nearly all test sets. The best algorithms also supported clinically relevant downstream tasks by accurately classifying fetal-type posterior cerebral arteries and localizing aneurysms in relation to CoW anatomy. This benchmark demonstrated the utility of CoW segmentation algorithms for some downstream clinical applications with explainability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TopCoW挑战赛——面向CT和MR血管造影的拓扑感知Willis环分割
Willis环（CoW）是连接大脑主要循环的重要动脉网络，其血管结构被认为影响严重神经血管疾病的发病风险、严重程度和预后。然而，描述高度变异的CoW解剖结构仍需人工操作且耗时。CoW常通过两种无创血管造影成像方式——磁共振血管造影（MRA）和计算机断层血管造影（CTA）进行成像，但标注CoW解剖结构的数据集稀少，且尚无标准化基准用于比较CoW分割算法。我们与一个包含125对来自同一患者的MRA和CTA扫描的标注CoW数据集同步发布了TopCoW基准挑战赛。使用虚拟现实技术创建了13个血管成分的体素级标注，并由临床专家验证。参赛者提交了CoW分割及变异分类算法，我们在包含来自五个以上中心的226个扫描的内部和外部测试集上进行了评估。该基准包括体素级分割、CoW成分检测、CoW变异分类及两个临床应用任务。我们收到了来自六大洲超过250名参赛者的提交。表现最佳的团队在CoW分割中Dice分数超过90%，关键血管成分检测F1分数超过80%，CoW变异分类的平衡准确率超过70%（几乎在所有测试集上）。最佳算法还通过准确分类胎儿型后循环动脉并定位与CoW解剖结构相关的动脉瘤，支持了临床相关的下游任务。该基准证明了CoW分割算法在部分下游临床应用中的实用性，并具备可解释性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate the manual segmentation of the variable Circle of Willis (CoW) anatomy for clinical use in neurovascular disease risk assessment, the TopCoW Challenge organized a benchmark using 125 annotated MRA and CTA scans with voxel-level labels created via virtual reality and verified by experts. Participants submitted algorithms for CoW segmentation, component detection, variant classification, and clinical tasks, evaluated on 226 scans from multiple centers. Top-performing teams achieved over 90% Dice scores for segmentation, over 80% F1 scores for component detection, and over 70% balanced accuracy in variant classification, with algorithms also successfully supporting downstream tasks like fetal-type artery classification and aneurysm localization.
为推动脑部 Willis 环（CoW）自动分割在神经血管疾病评估中的临床应用，本研究组织了 TopCoW 挑战赛，基于一个包含 125 对 MRA 和 CTA 扫描的新标注数据集，其标注通过虚拟现实技术创建并经临床专家验证。参赛者提交了 CoW 分割、组件检测、变异分类及临床任务算法，评估数据涵盖 226 例多中心扫描。表现最佳的算法在分割 Dice 分数超过 90%，组件检测 F1 分数超 80%，变异分类平衡准确率超 70%，并能有效支持下游任务，如胎儿型后循环动脉识别与动脉瘤定位。
Significance-First Splitting: Aligning Treatment Heterogeneity Detection with Honest Estimation
Authors: Pantelis Z. Hadjipantelis, Weng Man Chiang, Karthik Nagesh
First: 2026-07-04T19:50:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:52:07+00:00
Comments: Typos/omissions corrected, author name corrected
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Estimating heterogeneous treatment effects (CATE) requires simultaneously detecting effect modification and quantifying estimation uncertainty. Existing tree-based methods make an uneasy trade-off: significance-based approaches (Radcliffe and Surry 2011) identify subgroup interactions directly but lack valid inference; honest causal trees (Athey and Imbens 2016) deliver nominal confidence interval coverage but use outcome-agnostic splitting criteria that sacrifice interaction sensitivity. We introduce a hybrid algorithm that fuses significance-based splitting with honest sample-splitting and cross-validation. Our splitting criterion uses the squared $t$-statistic for the treatment $\times$ side interaction ($t^2$), which is shown to be directly aligned with the honest $\text{EMSE}_τ$ criterion when the interaction is strong. Post-hoc honest cross-validation selects the cost-complexity penalty, giving a single principled estimator with nominal CI coverage at the leaf level. For forests, we retain bootstrap count vectors to enable an infinitesimal jackknife (IJ) variance estimate of Monte-Carlo convergence rather than formal pointwise inference. On the three synthetic designs from (Athey and Imbens 2016) the single tree achieves approximately 90% leaf-average CI coverage at the 90% nominal level across all three designs (200 replications each); on the Criteo, Hillstrom and Starbucks uplift datasets we match Qini coefficient performance of S-, T-learner and GRF baselines. An open-source Python package with reproducible seeds, sklearn-compatible API, and full test coverage accompanies this work (https://codeberg.org/hadjipantelis/rattus).
中文标题/摘要
标题：先验重要性分割：使治疗异质性检测与诚实估计对齐
估计异质性治疗效应（CATE）需要同时检测效应修饰并量化估计不确定性。现有基于树的方法存在权衡：基于显著性方法（Radcliffe 和 Surry 2011）直接识别亚组交互但缺乏有效推断；诚实因果树（Athey 和 Imbens 2016）提供名义置信区间覆盖率，但使用与结果无关的分割标准，牺牲了交互敏感性。我们引入一种混合算法，融合基于显著性的分割与诚实样本分割和交叉验证。我们的分割标准使用治疗×组交互的平方t统计量（t²），当交互较强时，该标准与诚实EMSEτ准则直接对齐。事后诚实交叉验证选择成本复杂度惩罚项，从而得到一个在叶节点层面具有名义置信区间覆盖率的单一原则性估计量。对于森林，我们保留自助法计数向量，以实现蒙特卡洛收敛的无穷小 jackknife（IJ）方差估计，而非正式逐点推断。在（Athey 和 Imbens 2016）提出的三个合成设计中，单棵树在所有三个设计（各200次重复）中实现约90%的叶节点平均置信区间覆盖率（名义水平90%）；在Criteo、Hillstrom和Starbucks提升数据集上，我们与S-、T-学习器和GRF基线方法的Qini系数表现相当。本工作附带一个开源Python包（https://codeberg.org/hadjipantelis/rattus），支持可重现种子、sklearn兼容API和完整测试覆盖。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to simultaneously detect treatment heterogeneity and provide valid statistical inference, the paper proposes a hybrid tree-based method that combines significance-based splitting with honest sample-splitting and cross-validation. The algorithm uses the squared t-statistic for treatment-by-side interactions as its splitting criterion, which aligns with the honest EMSEτ criterion under strong interactions. A post-hoc honest cross-validation selects the cost-complexity penalty to produce a single estimator with nominal confidence interval coverage at the leaf level. For forests, it employs bootstrap count vectors to estimate variance via infinitesimal jackknife rather than pointwise inference. Experiments on three synthetic datasets show 90% average leaf-level CI coverage at the 90% nominal level, and on real uplift datasets, it matches or exceeds the Qini coefficient performance of S-, T-learner, and GRF baselines.
为同时检测治疗异质性并提供有效统计推断，该论文提出一种混合树方法，结合基于显著性的分割与诚实样本分割及交叉验证。其分割标准采用治疗与协变量交互作用的t统计量平方，该标准在强交互作用下与诚实EMSEτ准则一致。通过事后交叉验证选择成本复杂性惩罚项，确保叶节点处名义置信区间覆盖率，森林中则利用Bootstrap计数向量通过无穷小杰克knife估计方差而非逐点推断。在三个合成数据集和真实提升数据集上，单棵树在90%名义水平下实现约90%的叶节点平均置信区间覆盖率，且Qini系数表现与S-、T-学习器及GRF基线相当。
Contrastive-Collapsed Loss for Flexible and Geometrically Optimal Embeddings and Faster Convergence
Authors: Blanca Cano-Camarero, Ángela Fernández-Pascual, José R. Dorronsoro
First: 2026-07-14T15:51:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:51:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this work, we introduce CoCo, a loss function aimed at learning normalized and well-structured representations. The proposed loss encourages intra-class collapse and inter-class contrast while preserving sufficient flexibility for neural networks to approximate geometrically optimal embeddings with large angular separation between classes. We provide a theoretical analysis positioning CoCo with respect to related objectives such as dot regression and cross-entropy, showing that the new proposed loss benefits from closer initialization to the optimal configuration, more informative gradients, and stronger incentives for class-wise representation collapse. Extensive experiments on diverse tabular datasets from the OpenML-CC18 benchmark show that CoCo achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods, including kernel SVM, Random Forest, dot regression, and cross-entropy-based neural networks. In addition, both theoretical arguments and empirical analyses demonstrate that the proposal promotes tighter class clustering and faster convergence. These results highlight CoCo loss as an effective objective for learning discriminative representations while maintaining competitive predictive performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对比坍缩损失：用于灵活且几何最优嵌入与更快收敛
本文提出CoCo损失函数，旨在学习归一化且结构良好的表示。该损失鼓励类内坍缩与类间对比，同时保留足够的灵活性，使神经网络能够逼近具有大类间角分离的几何最优嵌入。我们通过理论分析将CoCo与相关目标（如点积回归和交叉熵）进行比较，表明新损失函数具有更接近最优配置的初始化、更信息丰富的梯度以及更强的类间表示坍缩激励。在OpenML-CC18基准的多样化表格数据集上的大量实验表明，CoCo在性能上与当前最先进方法（包括核SVM、随机森林、点积回归和基于交叉熵的神经网络）具有竞争力。理论分析和实证结果均表明，该方法促进更紧密的类聚类和更快的收敛。这些结果凸显CoCo损失作为学习判别性表示的有效目标，同时保持有竞争力的预测性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for geometrically optimal and discriminative embeddings that converge faster, this work proposes CoCo, a contrastive-collapsed loss function that encourages intra-class collapse while maximizing inter-class contrast, allowing neural networks to learn representations with large angular separations. Theoretically, CoCo is shown to benefit from better initialization proximity to optimal configurations, more informative gradients, and stronger incentives for class-wise collapse compared to alternatives like dot regression and cross-entropy. Empirically, on diverse tabular datasets from OpenML-CC18, CoCo achieves competitive performance against kernel SVM, Random Forest, and neural networks using dot regression or cross-entropy, while also promoting tighter class clustering and faster convergence.
为提升表示学习中几何最优且判别性强的嵌入效果，本文提出CoCo损失函数，通过鼓励类内坍缩与类间对比，使神经网络学习到归一化、结构良好的表示，并保持大角度类间分离。该方法基于理论分析，与点积回归和交叉熵等方法对比，展现出更接近最优配置的初始化、更信息丰富的梯度及更强的类内坍缩激励。在OpenML-CC18多个表格数据集上的实验表明，CoCo在性能上可与核SVM、点积回归和交叉熵神经网络等前沿方法媲美，同时促进更紧密的类聚类和更快的收敛速度。
Open-KNEAD: Knowledge-grounded Nutrition Estimation via Agentic Decomposition
Authors: Bruce Coburn, Jingbo Yue, Jinge Ma, Siddeshwar Raghavan, Gautham Vinod, Fengqing Zhu
First: 2026-07-14T15:48:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:48:25+00:00
Comments: 10 pages main paper, 5 pages supplementary
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly used for dietary assessment from meal images, where retrieval-augmented grounding was shown to sharpen nutrition estimates. However, we find this premise no longer holds for current MLLMs. A modern MLLM's direct estimate now matches or surpasses the full retrieval pipeline. This raises a question: if retrieval no longer improves the overall estimate, can it still deliver the two things clinicians value, accurate portions and a traceable, item-by-item record? We pursue this while preserving what matters for clinical adoption: minimal user burden (a single, unannotated meal image), explainability (an auditable record), and privacy (locally hosted inference). We introduce Open-KNEAD, a knowledge-grounded agentic framework for meal nutrition estimation that is training-free and locally deployable. Each decomposed food item is grounded to a Food and Nutrient Database for Dietary Studies (FNDDS) code via selective, nutrient-aware retrieval, composing an auditable per-item record. Across two open MLLM families and three cuisines, Open-KNEAD improves portion estimates over both prior grounding methods and direct estimation in most backbone-dataset settings. An agent-internal recipe-prior step further recovers the invisible cooking-added energy that biases estimates on non-US cuisine. The advantage is largest on the dietitian-verified ACETADA dataset, where the local open agent surpasses the direct portion estimates of two frontier closed models by roughly $30\%$ and $53\%$, all while keeping every meal image on local hardware. We release the Open-KNEAD framework and its agent-ready FNDDS knowledge base.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Open-KNEAD：基于知识的营养估算代理分解框架
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）正越来越多用于从餐食图像中进行饮食评估，其中检索增强的定位被证明能提升营养估算精度。然而，我们发现这一前提对当前MLLMs不再成立。现代MLLMs的直接估算现已匹配或超越完整检索流程。这引发一个问题：如果检索不再提升整体估算精度，它是否仍能提供临床人员所重视的两项能力——准确的分量估算和可追溯的逐项记录？我们在保持临床采纳所需要素（最小用户负担：仅需一张未标注的餐食图像；可解释性：可审计记录；隐私性：本地部署推理）的前提下探索此问题。我们提出了Open-KNEAD，一个无需训练、可本地部署、基于知识的代理框架，用于餐食营养估算。每个分解的食品项通过选择性、营养感知的检索，关联至《膳食研究食物与营养数据库》（FNDDS）代码，形成可审计的逐项记录。在两个开源MLLM家族和三种菜系上，Open-KNEAD在多数骨干数据集设置中，均优于此前的定位方法和直接估算。代理内部的食谱先验步骤进一步恢复了烹饪添加能量，从而修正非美国菜系估算中的偏差。优势在经营养师验证的ACETADA数据集上最为显著，其中本地开源代理在保持所有餐食图像本地处理的前提下，其分量估算精度分别超越两个前沿闭源模型约30%和53%。我们开源了Open-KNEAD框架及其适配代理的FNDDS知识库。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that retrieval-augmented methods no longer consistently improve nutrition estimates from meal images, the authors explore whether retrieval can still deliver clinically valuable outputs—accurate portion sizes and traceable item-level records—without increasing user burden or compromising privacy. They introduce Open-KNEAD, a training-free, locally deployable agentic framework that decomposes meals into individual food items, grounding each to FNDDS codes via nutrient-aware retrieval to build an auditable record. Experiments across two MLLM families and three cuisines show Open-KNEAD outperforms both prior retrieval methods and direct estimation in most settings, with the largest gains on the ACETADA dataset, where local inference surpasses two frontier closed models by 30% and 53% in portion accuracy while keeping all data local.
鉴于现有多模态大语言模型的直接估计已不逊于检索增强方法，作者探究检索是否仍能提供临床所需的关键能力——准确的分量估计和可追溯的逐项记录——同时保持低用户负担、可解释性和隐私性。他们提出Open-KNEAD，一个无需训练、本地部署的代理框架，通过营养感知检索将餐食分解为食品项并关联至FNDDS代码，生成可审计记录。在两个大模型家族和三种菜系的实验中，Open-KNEAD在多数设置下优于传统检索方法和直接估计，尤其在经营养师验证的ACETADA数据集上，本地代理的分量估计比两个前沿闭源模型分别高出30%和53%，且全程数据本地化。
Real-time fall detection based on vision for low-power edge platforms
Authors: Wenjun Xia, Zhicheng Peng, Haopeng Li, Zhengdi Zhang
First: 2026-07-14T15:43:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:43:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Falling detection is vital for elderly care and intelligent surveillance; however, prevailing vision-based approaches predominantly frame it as static pose classification or discrete temporal pattern matching, fundamentally overlooking the instability dynamics of the human support system. This paper proposes a physics-informed falling detection framework that recasts falling as a stability-loss event in a coupled dynamical system. We introduce a novel dual-LTC architecture comprising a Center-of-Mass (CoM) subsystem and a Base-of-Support (BoS) subsystem, both instantiated as Liquid Time-Constant (LTC) neural networks to continuously model inertial trajectory evolution and ground-contact adjustment through adaptive time constants, Physical interpretability of falling motion. A learnable coupling module emulates physical interaction between the two subsystems, while a Stability Manifold classifier operates in the joint latent space to detect boundary crossing via Lyapunov-inspired stability metrics. Complementary counterfactual trajectory projection and Time-to-Collision (TTC) estimation further enable irreversibility assessment and early warning. The architecture is designed to support a three-state prediction paradigm (Normal, Falling, Fallen); in this preliminary study, we validate the core stability discrimination capability on a two-class dataset (Normal vs. Falling), leaving the full three-state temporal transition to future work. Unlike conventional CNN--RNN pipelines, the proposed formulation encodes continuous-time mechanical inertia, yielding a sub-50K-parameter network capable of real-time inference on resource-constrained edge devices. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive accuracy with superior physical interpretability, validating its efficacy for low-compute visual fall detection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向低功耗边缘平台的基于视觉的实时跌倒检测
跌倒检测对老年护理和智能监控至关重要；然而，现有基于视觉的方法主要将其视为静态姿态分类或离散时间模式匹配，根本忽视了人体支撑系统的不稳定性动力学。本文提出一种物理信息驱动的跌倒检测框架，将跌倒重新定义为耦合动力系统中的稳定性丧失事件。我们引入一种新颖的双LTC架构，包含质心（CoM）子系统和支撑基底（BoS）子系统，二者均以液态时间常数（LTC）神经网络实现，通过自适应时间常数连续建模惯性轨迹演化与地面接触调整，赋予跌倒运动物理可解释性。一个可学习的耦合模块模拟两个子系统间的物理交互，而稳定性流形分类器在联合潜在空间中通过李雅普诺夫启发式稳定性指标检测边界穿越。互补的反事实轨迹投影与碰撞时间（TTC）估计进一步实现不可逆性评估与早期预警。该架构支持三状态预测范式（正常、跌倒、已跌倒）；本初步研究在二分类数据集（正常 vs. 跌倒）上验证核心稳定性判别能力，完整三状态时序转换留待未来工作。与传统CNN-RNN流水线不同，该方法编码连续时间机械惯性，构建参数量低于5万的网络，可在资源受限的边缘设备上实现实时推理。大量实验表明其在保持竞争力精度的同时具备更优的物理可解释性，验证其在低计算量视觉跌倒检测中的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, real-time fall detection on low-power edge devices, this paper introduces a physics-informed framework that models falling as a stability-loss event in a coupled dynamical system. The method employs a dual-LTC architecture with separate subsystems for Center-of-Mass and Base-of-Support dynamics, both using Liquid Time-Constant networks to adaptively model motion and contact. A learnable coupling module simulates physical interaction, while a Stability Manifold classifier detects falling via Lyapunov-inspired metrics in joint latent space. Experimental results on a two-class dataset show competitive accuracy with superior physical interpretability, and the model achieves real-time inference on edge platforms using under 50K parameters.
为实现低功耗边缘设备上的实时跌倒检测，本文提出一种基于物理原理的框架，将跌倒建模为支撑系统稳定性丧失的动态事件。方法采用双LTC架构，分别建模质心与支撑基底的动态，使用液态时间常数网络自适应捕捉运动与接触变化；耦合模块模拟物理交互，稳定性流形分类器通过李雅普诺夫启发的指标在联合潜在空间检测跌倒。实验表明，该方法在二分类数据集上精度竞争力强，具备物理可解释性，且模型参数少于5万，可在资源受限设备上实时推理。
Harness VLA: Steering Frozen VLAs into Reliable Manipulation Primitives via Memory-Guided Agents
Authors: Yixian Zhang, Huanming Zhang, Feng Gao, Xiao Li, Zhihao Liu, Chunyang Zhu, Jiaxing Qiu, Yuchen Yan, Jiyuan Liu, Wenhao Tang, Zhengru Fang, Yi Nie, Changxu Wei, Yu Wang, Wenbo Ding, Chao Yu
First: 2026-07-09T13:08:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:39:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language-conditioned manipulation requires both precise contact-rich control and robust reasoning over language, scenes, and long horizons. End-to-end Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide strong local visuomotor skills, but they are trained on in-distribution task trajectories and often fail under deployment perturbations such as semantic retargeting, goal re-binding, spatial-layout shifts, and unstable local contacts. LLM coding agents provide complementary semantic and compositional reasoning, but purely analytic primitives struggle with irregular grasping, constrained placement, and articulated-object interaction. We present Harness VLA, a memory-augmented agentic framework that exposes a frozen VLA as a retryable contact-rich primitive and composes it with a small fixed library of analytic primitives for grounding, staging, transport, navigation, and release. Rather than expanding the skill library, the harness learns the operating range of these fixed primitives from task-specific execution traces, global success rules, and failure models. By lifting semantic re-grounding, non-contact execution, and VLA re-staging to the planner while reserving the frozen VLA for local contact-rich phases, Harness VLA extends pretrained VLAs beyond their original trajectory distribution without finetuning. Across perturbed tabletop, household kitchen, and clean-to-randomized bimanual manipulation, Harness VLA improves over the strongest relevant baselines by 38.6 and 25.4 percentage points on LIBERO-Pro and RoboCasa365, respectively, and reaches 58.4% on RoboTwin C2R.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Harness VLA：通过记忆引导代理将冻结的视觉-语言-动作模型引导至可靠的操控原语
语言引导的操控需要精确的接触密集型控制和对语言、场景及长时程的鲁棒推理。端到端视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型具备强大的局部视觉-运动技能，但其训练基于分布内任务轨迹，常在部署扰动（如语义重定向、目标重新绑定、空间布局变化和不稳定局部接触）下失效。大型语言模型编码代理提供互补的语义与组合推理能力，但纯分析型原语难以处理不规则抓取、受限放置及刚体对象交互。我们提出Harness VLA，一种记忆增强的代理框架，将冻结的VLA作为可重试的接触密集型原语，并与小型固定分析原语库组合，用于定位、预置、运输、导航和释放。该框架不扩展技能库，而是从任务特定执行轨迹、全局成功规则和失败模型中学习这些固定原语的操作范围。通过将语义重定位、非接触执行和VLA重置提升至规划器层面，同时保留冻结VLA用于局部接触密集阶段，Harness VLA在无需微调的情况下扩展了预训练VLA的适用范围。在扰动桌面、家庭厨房及清洁到随机化双臂操控任务中，Harness VLA在LIBERO-Pro和RoboCasa365上分别比最强基线提升38.6和25.4个百分点，并在RoboTwin C2R上达到58.4%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to deploy vision-language-action models in real-world manipulation tasks under distributional shifts, the authors propose Harness VLA, a memory-guided framework that combines a frozen VLA with a small set of analytic primitives. The method uses task-specific traces, global success rules, and failure models to learn how to operate within the fixed primitive library without expanding it. By offloading semantic re-grounding and non-contact phases to an agent while reserving the VLA for contact-rich local control, the system achieves robustness without finetuning. Experimental results show significant performance gains: 38.6 and 25.4 percentage point improvements over baselines on LIBERO-Pro and RoboCasa365, respectively, and 58.4% success on RoboTwin C2R.
为提升视觉语言动作模型在现实世界操作任务中的鲁棒性，作者提出Harness VLA框架，将冻结的VLA与少量分析型操作原语结合，通过任务轨迹和失败模型学习原语的操作边界，实现语义重定位与非接触规划，保留VLA处理接触密集阶段。实验表明，该方法在LIBERO-Pro和RoboCasa365上分别比最强基线提升38.6和25.4个百分点，在RoboTwin C2R上达到58.4%成功率。
Rank-1 Identity Consensus Predicts Gallery Enrollment in 1:N Face Matching More Accurately than Score Thresholding
Authors: Gabriella Pangelinan, Aman Bhatta, Michael C. King, Kevin W. Bowyer
First: 2026-07-14T15:38:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:38:08+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 8 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In operational 1:N face identification, a crucial question arises for each probe: is this person enrolled in the gallery or not? The stakes are high and asymmetric. Rejecting a mate-present (MP) probe loses a valid lead; accepting a mate-absent (MA) probe makes every returned candidate a false identification, at worst a wrongful arrest. Most approaches threshold match scores, but scores shift substantially with image quality and gallery size and composition, making thresholds fixed before deployment brittle under realistic conditions. Our prior work introduced 1-consistency, the only method based on rank consensus across multiple independently trained matchers: a probe is labeled MP if all matchers return the same rank-1 identity. This work stress-tests 1-consistency across 36 (gallery, probe quality) scenarios spanning four quality levels and two structural axes: images per identity and total enrolled identities. We benchmark against two score-thresholding methods that bracket what any deployed threshold could achieve. Fixed Score-Thresholding (FST), calibrated once on baseline conditions, collapses asymmetrically as quality degrades: MP recall falls below 2% while MA recall holds near 100%. Oracle Score-Thresholding (OST), re-tuned per scenario, is the best any threshold could theoretically do, yet for degraded probes 1-consistency matches it with zero tuning. The two differ mainly in error type (OST favors MP recall, 1-consistency favors MA recall), but on one axis 1-consistency does not merely match the oracle: when it labels a probe MP, it returns the correct mate 97-100% of the time versus OST's 66-84% under severe degradation. In short, 1-consistency delivers oracle-level accuracy without the impossible requirement: it sets no threshold, so it needs no advance knowledge of the conditions a probe will arrive in, which is what makes it usable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于排名一致性的1:1身份共识比分数阈值更准确地预测1:N人脸匹配中的库内注册情况
在实际1:N人脸识别中，每个探针的关键问题是：此人是否在库中注册？后果严重且不对称。拒绝库内存在（MP）探针会丢失有效线索；接受库内不存在（MA）探针则会导致所有返回候选均为错误识别，最坏情况下引发错误逮捕。多数方法采用分数阈值，但分数随图像质量和库大小及构成显著变化，导致部署前设定的固定阈值在真实条件下脆弱。我们先前工作引入了1-一致性，即唯一基于多个独立训练匹配器间排名共识的方法：若所有匹配器均返回同一排名第一身份，则标记为MP。本研究在涵盖四个质量等级和两个结构轴（每身份图像数、总注册身份数）的36种（库、探针质量）场景中对1-一致性进行压力测试。我们与两种分数阈值方法对比，后者代表任何部署阈值所能达到的极限。固定分数阈值（FST）在基准条件下校准一次，当质量下降时不对称崩溃：MP召回率降至2%以下，而MA召回率仍接近100%。理想分数阈值（OST）每场景重新调优，是任何阈值理论上能达到的最佳效果，但在探针质量下降时，1-一致性无需调优即可匹配OST。二者主要区别在于错误类型（OST偏好MP召回，1-一致性偏好MA召回），但在一个轴上，1-一致性不仅匹配理想阈值：当其标记探针为MP时，在严重退化条件下，正确返回匹配对象的比例为97-100%，而OST仅为66-84%。简言之，1-一致性无需任何不可能的先验条件（如设定阈值），即可达到理想阈值精度，这正是其可用性的关键。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable 1:N face matching under variable image quality and gallery conditions, this work evaluates 1-consistency—a method that labels a probe as mate-present if all independently trained matchers agree on the same rank-1 identity—against traditional score-thresholding approaches. Experiments across 36 scenarios varying in image quality and gallery structure show that 1-consistency outperforms fixed-thresholding, which collapses under degradation, and nearly matches oracle-thresholding without requiring re-tuning. While both methods differ in error trade-offs, 1-consistency achieves higher accuracy in identifying correct mates under severe degradation, returning the true match 97–100% of the time versus 66–84% for oracle-thresholding, demonstrating robustness without needing prior knowledge of deployment conditions.
为应对图像质量与图库条件变化下1:N人脸匹配的可靠性需求，本文评估了1-一致性方法——即当多个独立训练的匹配器均一致返回同一排名第一身份时，判定探针为匹配对象——与传统分值阈值法的性能。在36种不同场景下，1-一致性显著优于固定阈值法（其在图像质量下降时性能急剧崩溃），且几乎达到理想阈值法的水平而无需任何调参。尤其在严重退化条件下，当1-一致性判定探针匹配时，其正确识别真实匹配对象的准确率高达97–100%，远超理想阈值法的66–84%。该方法的核心优势在于无需设定阈值，因此无需预知实际运行环境，具备高鲁棒性和部署可行性。
Accelerated Mixing Time of Randomized Hamiltonian Monte Carlo
Authors: Siddharth Mitra, Vishwak Srinivasan, Xiuyuan Wang, Andre Wibisono
First: 2026-07-14T15:38:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:38:04+00:00
Comments: 74 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We show the Randomized Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (RHMC) algorithm has accelerated mixing time guarantees for sampling from log-concave probability distributions. RHMC proceeds by repeatedly simulating the continuous-time Hamiltonian dynamics for some random integration times, and resetting the velocity to be an independent Gaussian random variable between each simulation. We show that when the target distribution is log-concave and satisfies an $α$-Talagrand inequality (for example, if the target distribution is $α$-strongly log-concave), if we use a random integration time from either the triangular or the exponential distribution with mean $Θ(α^{-1/2})$, then RHMC converges exponentially fast in KL divergence, and the total integration time to reach error $\varepsilon$ in KL divergence scales as $O(α^{-1/2} \log(\varepsilon^{-1}))$. We also show that when the target distribution is log-concave, if we use a sequence of random integration times from the triangular distribution with exponentially increasing means, then the total integration time to reach error $\varepsilon$ in KL divergence scales as $O(\varepsilon^{-1/2})$. Our analysis relies on a bound on the average KL divergence along Hamiltonian dynamics, which is inspired by an analogous result on accelerated optimization methods based on Hamiltonian dynamics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：随机哈密顿蒙特卡洛的加速混合时间
我们证明了随机哈密顿蒙特卡洛（RHMC）算法在从对数凹概率分布采样时具有加速混合时间保证。RHMC通过反复模拟连续时间哈密顿动力学，且在每次模拟之间将速度重置为独立的高斯随机变量，其积分时间随机选取。我们表明，当目标分布为对数凹且满足α-Talagrand不等式（例如，若目标分布为α-强对数凹）时，若使用均值为Θ(α^{-1/2})的三角分布或指数分布作为随机积分时间，则RHMC在KL散度中呈指数收敛，达到KL散度误差ε所需的总积分时间按O(α^{-1/2} log(ε^{-1}))缩放。此外，当目标分布为对数凹时，若使用均值呈指数增长的三角分布序列作为随机积分时间，则达到KL散度误差ε所需的总积分时间按O(ε^{-1/2})缩放。我们的分析依赖于哈密顿动力学过程中平均KL散度的界，该界受基于哈密顿动力学的加速优化方法类似结果的启发。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for faster convergence in sampling from log-concave distributions, this paper analyzes Randomized Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (RHMC), which integrates Hamiltonian dynamics with random time steps and Gaussian velocity resets. The method leverages α-Talagrand inequalities to establish exponential convergence in KL divergence, showing that with triangular or exponential random integration times of mean Θ(α^{-1/2}), the total integration time to achieve error ε scales as O(α^{-1/2} log(ε^{-1})). Additionally, using exponentially increasing triangular time steps for general log-concave targets yields a scaling of O(ε^{-1/2}), demonstrating improved efficiency under specific conditions. The analysis is grounded in a novel bound on average KL divergence along trajectories, inspired by accelerated optimization techniques.
为提升对对数凹分布采样的收敛速度，本文研究了随机哈密顿蒙特卡洛（RHMC）算法，其通过随机积分时间模拟哈密顿动力学，并在每步重置速度为高斯噪声。研究发现，当目标分布满足α-Talagrand不等式（如α强对数凹分布）时，使用均值为Θ(α^{-1/2})的三角或指数分布积分时间，RHMC在KL散度下呈指数收敛，达到误差ε所需总积分时间为O(α^{-1/2} log(ε^{-1}))。若采用均值指数增长的三角分布积分时间，对一般对数凹分布，收敛时间可降至O(ε^{-1/2})。分析基于沿轨迹的平均KL散度上界，受加速优化方法中哈密顿动力学结果启发。
UniMedSeg: Unified In-Context Learning for Multi-Paradigm 2D/3D Medical Image Segmentation
Authors: Yunzhou Li, Jiesi Hu, Yanwu Yang, Hanyang Peng, Chenfei Ye, Jianfeng Cao, Yixuan Yuan, Ting Ma
First: 2026-07-14T15:34:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:34:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Medical image segmentation foundation models are expected to generalize across diverse clinical scenarios, yet existing universal methods remain fragmented by prompt paradigms and spatial dimensions. Visual in-context learning, interactive segmentation, and language-guided segmentation are typically handled by paradigm-specific models, while 2D and 3D images are also modeled separately. Such isolation prevents heterogeneous annotations and data from being jointly absorbed by a single scalable model and limits cross-paradigm knowledge transfer. To address this bottleneck, we propose UniMedSeg, a Transformer-centric universal segmentation framework that maps visual examples, geometric interactions, language instructions, and 2D/3D images into a shared sequence space, enabling heterogeneous medical supervision to be jointly learned through a unified in-context interface without prompt- or dimension-specific branches. To overcome the long-sequence memory bottleneck caused by visual contexts, we introduce Decoupled Split Attention, which reduces attention complexity to linear while preserving hardware-friendly computation and focused context-target interaction. Extensively trained and evaluated on a large corpus curated from 27 public datasets, UniMedSeg achieves state-of-the-art performance across visual in-context, interactive, and language-guided segmentation without task-specific fine-tuning, demonstrating strong generalization on diverse held-out tasks. The code and model weights are publicly available at https://github.com/Lii1228/UniMedSeg
中文标题/摘要
标题：UniMedSeg：面向多范式2D/3D医学图像分割的统一上下文学习
医学图像分割基础模型期望能在多样化的临床场景中泛化，但现有通用方法仍因提示范式和空间维度而割裂。视觉上下文学习、交互式分割和语言引导分割通常由范式特定模型处理，2D与3D图像也分别建模。这种隔离阻碍了异构标注和数据被单一可扩展模型联合吸收，限制了跨范式知识迁移。为解决此瓶颈，我们提出UniMedSeg，一种以Transformer为核心的通用分割框架，将视觉示例、几何交互、语言指令及2D/3D图像映射至共享序列空间，通过统一上下文接口无需提示或维度特定分支即可联合学习异构医学监督。为克服视觉上下文引发的长序列记忆瓶颈，我们引入解耦分割注意力机制，将注意力复杂度降至线性，同时保持硬件友好计算与聚焦的上下文-目标交互。在27个公开数据集构建的大规模语料上广泛训练与评估，UniMedSeg在视觉上下文、交互式及语言引导分割任务中无需任务特定微调即达到最先进性能，展现出对多样化未见任务的强大泛化能力。代码与模型权重公开于https://github.com/Lii1228/UniMedSeg
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragmentation of existing medical image segmentation models across different paradigms and spatial dimensions, UniMedSeg introduces a unified Transformer-based framework that maps visual examples, geometric interactions, language instructions, and 2D/3D images into a shared sequence space, enabling joint learning of heterogeneous supervision without task-specific branches. To handle long visual sequences efficiently, it employs Decoupled Split Attention, reducing attention complexity to linear while maintaining focused context-target interaction. Evaluated across 27 public datasets, UniMedSeg achieves state-of-the-art performance in visual in-context, interactive, and language-guided segmentation without fine-tuning, demonstrating strong generalization across diverse tasks.
为解决现有医学图像分割模型在不同范式与空间维度上割裂的问题，UniMedSeg提出一种统一的Transformer框架，将视觉示例、几何交互、语言指令及2D/3D图像映射至共享序列空间，实现异构监督的联合学习，无需任务特定分支。为应对长序列视觉上下文带来的内存瓶颈，该框架采用解耦分段注意力机制，将注意力复杂度降至线性，同时保持硬件友好计算与精准上下文交互。在27个公开数据集上广泛评估，UniMedSeg在视觉上下文、交互式及语言引导分割任务中无需微调即达到最先进性能，展现出强大的跨任务泛化能力。
Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0: Efficient Physical-World Agents
Authors: Ziyi Wang, Xumin Yu, Yongming Rao, Yonggen Ling, Yunheng Li, Oran Wang, Mingqi Gao, Yuchen Zhou, Yves Liang, Zuyan Liu, Yani Zhang, Rui Huang, Xiaoran Xu, Bowen Yuan, Yifu Yuan, Xu Tan, He Zhang, Yufei Huang, Shenghao Zhang, Hongsheng Wu, Han Hu, Zhengyou Zhang
First: 2026-07-14T15:34:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:34:17+00:00
Comments: Tech Report. Code and models are open-sourced at https://github.com/Tencent-Hunyuan/HY-Embodied
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Building capable embodied agents requires not only multimodal perception and understanding, but also agentic capabilities for reasoning about actions, adapting to evolving situations, and interacting with the physical world. In this report, we introduce Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0, an efficient and powerful embodied foundation model specifically designed for embodied agents operating in the physical world. To cultivate such capabilities from the pre-training stage onward, we define an action-centric capability taxonomy comprising three progressive dimensions: Action-Relevant State Understanding, Action-Transition Reasoning, and Sequential and Adaptive Reasoning. Guided by this taxonomy, we develop a systematic data pipeline and curate data mixtures spanning both pre-training and post-training. To deliver strong physical-world understanding and interaction capabilities while supporting latency-sensitive deployment, we build our model on the Hy3-A3B language backbone and the Hy-ViT2 vision encoder. Its efficient Mixture-of-Experts architecture combines strong model capacity with high inference efficiency. We evaluate Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0 on a comprehensive suite of 38 benchmarks covering embodied perception, physical-world understanding, and embodied reasoning. The model achieves the best performance among similarly sized models on 19 of the 38 benchmarks and substantially outperforms strong competitors, including Qwen3.6-A3B and Cosmos 3. Compared with the previous-generation Hy-Embodied-0.5 MoT-2B, Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0 improves average performance by 8.4%. Despite activating only 3B parameters, it achieves performance close to that of the previous-generation model with 32B activated parameters. Beyond static benchmark evaluation, Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0 also demonstrates strong performance on embodied agentic tasks requiring multi-turn interaction and long-horizon reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0：高效的物理世界代理
构建具备能力的具身代理不仅需要多模态感知与理解，还需具备行动推理、适应动态环境及与物理世界交互的代理能力。本报告介绍Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0，一款专为物理世界具身代理设计的高效强大具身基础模型。为从预训练阶段即培养此类能力，我们定义了以行动为中心的能力分类体系，包含三个递进维度：行动相关状态理解、行动转换推理及序列化与自适应推理。基于该体系，我们构建系统化数据管道并整理涵盖预训练与后训练阶段的数据混合集。为在支持低延迟部署的同时实现强大的物理世界理解与交互能力，我们基于Hy3-A3B语言骨干与Hy-ViT2视觉编码器构建模型。其高效的Mixture-of-Experts架构兼顾强大模型容量与高推理效率。我们在涵盖具身感知、物理世界理解与具身推理的38项基准测试中评估该模型，其在19项基准中表现优于同等规模模型，显著超越Qwen3.6-A3B与Cosmos 3等强竞争者。相比上一代Hy-Embodied-0.5 MoT-2B，Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0平均性能提升8.4%。仅激活3B参数时，其性能已接近上一代激活32B参数的模型。除静态基准评估外，该模型在需多轮交互与长时推理的具身代理任务中亦表现优异。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for embodied agents capable of reasoning, adapting, and interacting in physical environments, this work introduces Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0, a foundation model built on a Hy3-A3B language backbone and Hy-ViT2 vision encoder, optimized for efficiency and physical-world interaction. The model is trained using a structured data pipeline guided by an action-centric taxonomy that progresses from state understanding to sequential reasoning. Evaluated across 38 benchmarks, it outperforms comparable models on 19 tasks and significantly surpasses competitors like Qwen3.6-A3B and Cosmos 3, achieving performance near that of a 32B model while activating only 3B parameters. It also excels in multi-turn and long-horizon embodied tasks, demonstrating strong agentic capabilities.
为构建能在物理世界中感知、推理与交互的具身智能体，作者提出Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0模型，基于Hy3-A3B语言架构与Hy-ViT2视觉编码器构建。该模型依据三阶段动作中心能力分类体系（动作相关状态理解、动作转换推理、序列与自适应推理）进行训练，采用定制化数据管道。在38项基准测试中，其在19项上表现最佳，显著超越Qwen3.6-A3B与Cosmos 3等强竞争模型，仅激活3B参数即达32B模型水平。同时在多轮交互与长时推理任务中表现优异。
MemOps: Benchmarking Lifecycle Memory Operations in Long-Horizon Conversations
Authors: Xixuan Hao, Zeyu Zhang, Zehao Lin, Yihang Sun, Ziliang Guo, Xichong Zhang, Yuxuan Liang, Feiyu Xiong, Zhiyu Li
First: 2026-07-14T15:33:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:33:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-term memory has become a foundational capability for LLM-based agents that accompany users across extended, multi-session interactions. Existing benchmarks, however, evaluate such memory almost exclusively through downstream question answering, scoring only the correctness of a final answer. This black-box formulation conflates the heterogeneous causes of memory failure, such as missing the introduction of a relevant fact, binding an operation to the wrong target, or relying on stale values after a correction. As a result, it can credit correct answers despite their reliance on inconsistent or unsafe memory states. In this paper, we argue that, in dynamic long-horizon interactions, memory is not a static collection of facts but a lifecycle of explicit operations, including remembering, forgetting, updating, reflecting, and their compositions. We introduce MemOps, a benchmark that reformulates conversational memory as a sequence of lifecycle operations and represents each memory event with a structured trace specifying its trigger, target, scope, state transition, and supporting evidence. A controllable generation pipeline embeds these operations into long, task-oriented conversations and produces gold operation traces together with six categories of operation-level probes, evaluated under both adjacent-evidence and long-context settings. Across long-context, retrieval-based, parametric and managed-memory systems, MemOps disentangles failure modes that final-answer accuracy alone conceals, revealing that current systems remain far from uniformly reliable. For instance, session-level retrieval outperforms turn-level retrieval, and long-context models remain notably weak at reconstructing ordered memory-state trajectories. These results move long-term memory evaluation from final-answer scoring toward interpretable, operation-level diagnosis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MemOps：长时对话中生命周期内存操作的基准测试
长期记忆已成为支持用户跨多会话、长时间交互的基于大语言模型（LLM）代理的基础能力。然而，现有基准测试几乎仅通过下游问答来评估此类记忆，仅衡量最终答案的正确性。这种黑箱形式混淆了记忆失败的异质原因，例如遗漏相关事实的引入、将操作绑定到错误目标，或在更正后仍依赖过时值。因此，它可能因依赖不一致或不安全的记忆状态而给予正确答案。本文认为，在动态长时交互中，记忆并非静态事实集合，而是一系列显式操作的生命周期，包括记住、遗忘、更新、反思及其组合。我们引入MemOps基准，将对话记忆重新表述为一系列生命周期操作，并用结构化追踪表示每个记忆事件，明确其触发条件、目标、作用范围、状态转换及支持证据。一个可控生成管道将这些操作嵌入长对话中，生成黄金操作轨迹及六类操作级探针，在相邻证据和长上下文设置下进行评估。在长上下文、检索式、参数化及托管内存系统中，MemOps揭示了仅凭最终答案准确性所掩盖的失败模式，表明当前系统仍远未达到统一可靠性。例如，会话级检索优于回合级检索，长上下文模型在重建有序记忆状态轨迹方面仍显著薄弱。这些结果推动长期记忆评估从最终答案评分转向可解释的操作级诊断。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of current benchmarks that only evaluate memory through final-answer correctness, this paper introduces MemOps, a new benchmark that models conversational memory as a lifecycle of explicit operations—such as remembering, forgetting, updating, and reflecting—each captured in structured traces detailing triggers, targets, and state transitions. Using a controllable generation pipeline, it embeds these operations into long, task-oriented conversations and generates gold traces alongside six operation-level probes. Experiments across retrieval-based, parametric, and managed-memory systems reveal that final-answer accuracy masks critical failure modes: session-level retrieval outperforms turn-level, and long-context models struggle to reconstruct ordered memory trajectories, indicating that current systems lack uniform reliability in managing memory lifecycles.
鉴于现有基准仅通过最终答案正确性评估记忆能力，本文提出MemOps，将对话记忆建模为包含记住、遗忘、更新、反思等显式操作的生命周期，并通过结构化追踪记录触发条件、目标、状态转换及证据。借助可控生成管道，构建包含黄金操作轨迹与六类操作级探针的长对话，分别在相邻证据与长上下文设置下评估。实验表明，最终答案准确率掩盖了关键失败模式：会话级检索优于轮次级，长上下文模型难以重建有序记忆轨迹，揭示当前系统在记忆管理上仍缺乏统一可靠性。
UR-VC: Unsupervised Robotic Value Correction for Time-Derived Progress Proxies
Authors: Lirui Zhao, Modi Shi, Li Chen, Qi Liu, Ping Luo, Hongyang Li
First: 2026-07-14T15:33:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:33:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern robot learning systems increasingly rely on dense progress or value signals to evaluate intermediate states, guide policy learning, and detect task completion, making the quality of these signals critical. Since such dense labels are rarely available at scale, normalized time within a demonstration is often used as a scalable substitute: later frames are treated as higher progress. However, this time-derived label is only a noisy proxy for physical task progress. In contact-rich manipulation, a robot may make progress and then lose it through slips, failed grasps, or partial undoing, while the time-derived label continues to increase monotonically. We introduce Unsupervised Robotic Value Correction (UR-VC), an offline, training-free method for correcting time-derived progress labels. UR-VC exploits a simple regularity in demonstration data: similar states often recur across different episodes, but at different timestamps. Instead of trusting the timestamp from a single trajectory, UR-VC retrieves similar states from other episodes and aggregates their time-derived labels to obtain a corrected progress estimate. UR-VC requires no manual progress labels, reward annotations, or additional value model. We evaluate UR-VC on real bimanual cloth flatten-and-fold data, a long-horizon deformable-object manipulation task with visible intermediate progress. The corrected labels capture local regressions and non-uniform progress that normalized time cannot represent, while preserving the overall task trend. We further use the corrected signal to construct advantage labels for VLA training, following recent advantage-conditioned policy learning. UR-VC shows a positive trend in real-robot task success under matched data, model, and training settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UR-VC：无监督机器人价值校正用于时间衍生进度代理
现代机器人学习系统日益依赖密集的进度或价值信号来评估中间状态、指导策略学习和检测任务完成，因此这些信号的质量至关重要。由于此类密集标签难以大规模获取，通常使用演示中的归一化时间作为可扩展替代：后期帧被视为更高进度。然而，这种时间衍生标签仅是物理任务进度的噪声代理。在接触密集的操作中，机器人可能取得进展，但因滑动、抓取失败或部分撤销而失去进展，而时间衍生标签仍单调递增。我们提出无监督机器人价值校正（UR-VC），一种离线、无需训练的方法，用于校正时间衍生进度标签。UR-VC 利用演示数据中的简单规律：相似状态常在不同演示中重复出现，但时间戳不同。UR-VC 不依赖单条轨迹的时间戳，而是从其他演示中检索相似状态并聚合其时间衍生标签，以获得校正后的进度估计。UR-VC 不需要人工进度标签、奖励标注或额外价值模型。我们在真实双臂布料展平与折叠数据上评估 UR-VC，该任务为长时程可变形物体操作，具有可见的中间进度。校正后的标签捕捉了归一化时间无法表示的局部退化与非均匀进度，同时保留整体任务趋势。我们进一步使用校正信号构建 VLA 训练所需的优势标签，遵循近期优势条件策略学习方法。在匹配数据、模型和训练设置下，UR-VC 在真实机器人任务中表现出正向成功率趋势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable progress signals in robotic learning, this paper addresses the issue that normalized time within demonstrations is a noisy proxy for actual task progress, especially in contact-rich manipulation where progress can be lost despite time increasing. UR-VC, an offline and training-free method, corrects these time-derived labels by leveraging the observation that similar states recur across different episodes at varying timestamps. It retrieves and aggregates progress estimates from similar states across episodes to produce a more accurate local progress signal, without requiring manual labels or reward annotations. Experiments on real bimanual cloth manipulation show that UR-VC captures non-uniform and regressive progress patterns missed by time-based proxies, while preserving overall task trend, and improves task success when used to construct advantage labels for VLA training.
为了解决机器人学习中基于时间的进度标签噪声大、无法反映真实任务进展的问题，UR-VC提出一种无监督、无需训练的方法，通过跨演示中相似状态的时间标签聚合来修正进度估计。在真实双臂布料折叠任务中，该方法有效捕捉了时间标签无法表达的局部退化与非均匀进展，同时保留整体任务趋势，并在用于优势标签训练时提升了机器人任务成功率。
Energy-Based Physics-Informed Form Finding for Clustered Tensegrity Structures
Authors: Jing Qin, Muhao Chen
First: 2026-07-14T15:32:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:32:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Tensegrity form-finding and physical property prediction are fundamental inverse problems in structural mechanics, which aim to determine equilibrium configurations and internal force distributions. These problems are challenging due to strong nonlinearity arising from the coupling between geometry and forces, the need to ensure structural stability, and the enforcement of constraints such as boundary conditions and symmetry. Moreover, traditional methods often lack robustness to noise and outliers. This paper proposes an energy-based learning framework for clustered tensegrity form finding and physical property prediction. The proposed approach incorporates total potential energy minimization and constitutive relations into the training objective, enabling the simultaneous prediction of equilibrium nodal configurations and associated physical quantities, including member forces and force densities. By incorporating energy-based physical losses directly into the learning process, the framework improves physical consistency, robustness, and data efficiency. Numerical experiments on tensegrity structures, including prism and lander systems, show the great potential of the proposed approach and demonstrate its capability for scalable form finding and accurate prediction of structural properties.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于能量的物理信息驱动的集群张拉整体结构形态优化
张拉整体结构形态优化与物理特性预测是结构力学中的基本逆问题，旨在确定平衡构型及内力分布。这些问题因几何与力的耦合导致强非线性、需确保结构稳定性及满足边界条件与对称性约束而具有挑战性。此外，传统方法常缺乏对噪声和异常值的鲁棒性。本文提出一种基于能量的学习框架，用于集群张拉整体结构的形态优化与物理特性预测。该方法将总势能最小化与本构关系纳入训练目标，可同时预测平衡节点构型及相关物理量，包括杆件内力与力密度。通过将基于能量的物理损失直接融入学习过程，该框架提升了物理一致性、鲁棒性与数据效率。在棱柱体与登陆器等张拉整体结构上的数值实验表明，该方法具有巨大潜力，可实现可扩展的形态优化与结构性能的精确预测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of finding equilibrium configurations and predicting physical properties in tensegrity structures due to nonlinear geometry-force coupling and sensitivity to noise, this paper introduces an energy-based learning framework that integrates total potential energy minimization and constitutive relations into the training objective. The method enables simultaneous prediction of nodal positions, member forces, and force densities while enforcing physical consistency. Experimental results on prism and lander tensegrity systems demonstrate that the approach achieves accurate form-finding and property prediction with improved robustness and data efficiency compared to traditional methods.
为解决张拉整体结构因几何与力强耦合导致的非线性、易受噪声干扰的形态求解与物理属性预测难题，本文提出一种基于能量的学习框架，将总势能极小化与本构关系融入训练目标，实现节点位置、杆件内力与力密度的同步预测。该方法通过直接引入物理能量损失提升物理一致性与鲁棒性。在棱柱与lander类张拉整体结构上的实验表明，该框架在形态求解与结构属性预测方面具有高精度与可扩展性。
A Multi-Agent System for Autonomous, Fine-Tuning-Free Clinical Symptom Detection: Development and Validation Study
Authors: Cameron Cagan, Pedram Fard, Jiazi Tian, Jingya Cheng, Shawn N. Murphy, Hossein Estiri
First: 2026-07-14T15:32:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:32:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Clinical notes contain many of the signs and symptoms that bring patients to care, yet this information rarely reaches structured fields. Existing extraction approaches either rely on context-insensitive rules that generate false positives or on supervised models that require substantial fine-tuning. We present Pythia, a multi-agent system that autonomously writes and optimizes extraction prompts for clinical concepts without manual prompt engineering or fine-tuning. Running on a locally hosted open-weights model, Pythia keeps clinical notes on local infrastructure and selects prompts using development-set sensitivity and specificity. We compared Pythia with a curated lexicon across 72 signs and symptoms from 400 clinical notes representing 387 patients. Development (n=300) and validation (n=100) sets were partitioned independently for each concept. Pythia achieved mean sensitivity of 0.76 and specificity of 0.95, compared with 0.82 and 0.76 for the lexicon, and matched or exceeded the lexicon on both metrics for 20 of 62 directly comparable concepts. For 14 concepts where the lexicon labeled every note positive, Pythia recovered mean specificity of 0.97 by requiring a present-tense, patient-attributed finding rather than any textual mention of a term. Specificity transferred from development to validation with minimal degradation across prevalences, whereas sensitivity transfer weakened below 5% prevalence, reaching a mean gap of 0.25 below 2% prevalence. A BERT classifier fine-tuned per concept on the same development set achieved mean sensitivity of 0.23 and collapsed to zero sensitivity for concepts below roughly 5% prevalence. These findings suggest that autonomous, fine-tuning-free prompt optimization can produce symptom extraction prompts that generalize effectively from development to validation while remaining deployable on local infrastructure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种无需微调的多智能体系统用于自主临床症状检测：开发与验证研究
临床记录包含大量导致患者就诊的体征和症状，但这些信息很少被结构化。现有提取方法要么依赖上下文无关的规则导致假阳性，要么依赖需要大量微调的监督模型。我们提出了Pythia，一种无需人工提示工程或微调即可自主编写和优化临床概念提取提示的多智能体系统。Pythia在本地部署的开源权重模型上运行，保持临床记录在本地基础设施中，并使用开发集敏感性和特异性选择提示。我们对比了Pythia与一个精选词典，在400份临床记录（代表387名患者）中的72种体征和症状上进行评估。开发集（n=300）和验证集（n=100）对每个概念独立划分。Pythia的平均敏感性为0.76，特异性为0.95，而词典分别为0.82和0.76；在62个可直接比较的概念中，Pythia在20个概念上达到或超过词典表现。对于14个词典标记所有记录为阳性概念，Pythia通过要求现时态、患者归因的发现而非任何术语文本提及，恢复了平均特异性0.97。特异性从开发集到验证集转移时衰减极小，而敏感性转移在低于5%患病率时显著减弱，在2%以下患病率时平均差距达0.25。在相同开发集上针对每个概念微调的BERT分类器，平均敏感性为0.23，低于约5%患病率时敏感性归零。这些发现表明，自主、无需微调的提示优化可生成在开发到验证集上有效泛化且可在本地基础设施部署的症状提取提示。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to extract clinical symptoms from unstructured notes without manual prompt engineering or model fine-tuning, the study introduces Pythia, a multi-agent system that autonomously generates and optimizes extraction prompts using a locally hosted open-weight model. Pythia selects prompts based on sensitivity and specificity on a development set, then validates performance on unseen data. Across 72 symptoms from 400 clinical notes, Pythia achieved mean sensitivity of 0.76 and specificity of 0.95, outperforming a curated lexicon (0.82 sensitivity, 0.76 specificity) for 20 of 62 comparable concepts. It notably recovered high specificity (0.97) for concepts where the lexicon had poor specificity by requiring patient-attributed, present-tense findings. Specificity generalized well from development to validation, while sensitivity degraded below 5% prevalence, with a mean gap of 0.25 at 2% prevalence. A BERT classifier fine-tuned per concept showed poor generalization, with sensitivity dropping to zero below 5% prevalence.
为解决从非结构化临床笔记中自动提取症状且无需人工提示工程或模型微调的问题，研究提出了Pythia多智能体系统，该系统基于本地部署的开源模型自主生成并优化提取提示，依据开发集上的敏感性和特异性选择提示。在400份临床笔记中的72种症状上，Pythia平均敏感性达0.76，特异性达0.95，优于人工词典（敏感性0.82，特异性0.76）的20种症状。对14种词典全为阳性标注的症状，Pythia通过强制要求患者主语、现在时态的明确描述，将特异性提升至0.97。特异性在开发集与验证集间泛化良好，而敏感性在低于5%流行率时显著下降，2%流行率时平均差距达0.25。相比之下，基于开发集微调的BERT分类器在低流行率下敏感性骤降至零。
LLM Judges Can Be Too Generous When There Is No Reference Answer
Authors: Chalamalasetti Kranti, Sowmya Vajjala
First: 2026-07-14T15:31:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:31:33+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM judges are increasingly being used to evaluate open-ended model responses, often in no-reference settings where a ground-truth answer is unavailable. However, can they reliably assess in such evaluation setups? We explore this question in this paper through a two stage pipeline with a) calibration experiments that assess the judge model's knowledge of the task it is evaluating, and b) sensitivity experiments that assess how the judge model's performance is impacted by the presence and positioning of the reference answer in the prompt. Across experiments covering three languages, we show that the judge models we evaluated tend to over-credit incorrect answers in the absence of a reference answer, and adding reference answer information to the prompt flips the judge model's correct/incorrect decisions by as much as 85% in some experimental settings. Comparison with a subset of human annotations shows that these reference-driven changes generally align with human judgments. Our results emphasize the need for calibrating the LLM judges with a sample with reference-aware evaluation before using them in reference-free setups reliably, and our methodology provides a blueprint for researchers and practitioners in doing such calibration of LLM judges for other tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当没有参考答案时，LLM裁判可能过于宽容
LLM裁判正越来越多地用于评估开放式模型响应，常在无参考答案的设置中进行，此时缺乏真实答案。然而，在这种评估设置下，它们能否可靠地评估？本文通过一个两阶段流程探讨此问题：a) 校准实验评估裁判模型对所评估任务的知识；b) 敏感性实验评估裁判模型性能如何受提示中参考答案存在与否及位置的影响。在覆盖三种语言的实验中，我们发现所评估的裁判模型在无参考答案时倾向于过度认可错误答案，而在提示中加入参考答案信息后，某些实验设置下裁判模型的正确/错误判断可翻转高达85%。与部分人工标注对比显示，这些参考驱动的变化通常与人类判断一致。我们的结果强调，在无参考设置中可靠使用LLM裁判前，需先以具备参考意识的样本对其进行校准，本文方法为研究人员和实践者提供了校准LLM裁判的蓝图。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether large language model (LLM) judges can reliably evaluate open-ended responses in no-reference settings, where ground-truth answers are unavailable. The authors propose a two-stage methodology: first, calibration experiments to measure the judge’s task knowledge, and second, sensitivity experiments to examine how the presence and placement of reference answers affect judging accuracy. Across three languages, results show that LLM judges tend to over-credit incorrect responses when no reference is provided, and introducing reference information can flip their correctness judgments by up to 85% in some cases. Human annotations confirm that these reference-driven shifts align with human judgment patterns, highlighting the need to calibrate LLM judges using reference-aware data before deploying them in reference-free evaluations.
本文探讨了大语言模型（LLM）裁判在无参考答案环境下评估开放性回答的可靠性。作者提出两阶段评估流程：首先通过校准实验评估裁判对任务的理解，其次通过敏感性实验检验参考答案在提示中的存在与否如何影响裁判判断。在三种语言的实验中发现，当无参考答案时，LLM裁判倾向于高估错误回答，而加入参考答案信息可使裁判判断翻转高达85%。这些变化与人类标注结果高度一致，表明在无参考环境下使用LLM裁判前，必须先用含参考答案的数据进行校准。
Evaluating Large Language Models on Misconceptions in Multi-Turn Medical Conversations
Authors: Monica Munnangi, Saiph Savage
First: 2026-07-14T15:30:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:30:37+00:00
Comments: Accepted to MLHC 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Patients seeking medical information often ask questions that embed incorrect assumptions or misconceptions. In such cases, safe medical communication requires not only answering the question, but identifying and correcting the underlying false belief. These interactions naturally unfold over multiple turns, a pattern now mirrored in interactions with LLMs. Yet current evaluation frameworks do not capture model behavior in these settings, where misconceptions can emerge, persist, or evolve over the course of a conversation. Whether LLMs can reliably correct such misconceptions over time remains largely unexamined. To study this, we introduce ThReadMed-QA, a multi-turn medical dialogue dataset of 2,437 patient-physician conversation threads comprising 8,204 question-answer pairs, derived from real patient interactions on AskDocs. This dataset enables systematic evaluation of whether models can detect and correct misconceptions under a multi-turn context. We evaluate five LLMs using a rubric-based LLM-as-a-Judge framework that scores responses based on their ability to identify and correct misconceptions. Our experiments reveal a consistent pattern: even frontier models that can address misconceptions in a single interaction degrade substantially over subsequent turns. GPT-5 and Claude-Haiku correct these false presuppositions around 85% on initial questions but drop to roughly 50% within two follow-ups. An oracle analysis replacing prior model outputs with physician responses shows that much of the degradation is driven by error propagation, while performance remains imperfect even under correct context. Even when models tend to correct misconceptions initially, their performance degrades substantially over later turns, leading to inconsistent and potentially unsafe guidance in patient-facing settings and highlighting the need for evaluation frameworks that capture multi-turn behavior.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在多轮医疗对话中评估大语言模型对误解的处理能力
患者在寻求医疗信息时，常提出包含错误假设或误解的问题。在这种情况下，安全的医疗沟通不仅需要回答问题，还需识别并纠正其背后的错误信念。此类互动天然呈多轮展开，当前与大语言模型（LLM）的互动也呈现类似模式。然而，现有评估框架未能捕捉模型在这些场景中的行为，即误解可能在对话过程中产生、持续或演变。LLM能否可靠地随时间纠正此类误解，仍鲜有研究。为此，我们引入ThReadMed-QA数据集，该数据集包含2,437个患者-医生对话线程，共8,204个问答对，源自AskDocs平台的真实患者互动。该数据集支持系统评估模型在多轮语境下检测和纠正误解的能力。我们采用基于评分标准的“LLM作为裁判”框架，对五种LLM进行评估，依据其识别和纠正误解的能力打分。实验结果揭示一致模式：即使能单轮处理误解的前沿模型，在后续轮次中表现显著下降。GPT-5和Claude-Haiku在初始问题中约85%能纠正错误预设，但在两轮后续中降至约50%。通过将先前模型输出替换为医生真实回答的“神谕分析”显示，性能下降主要源于错误传播，即使在正确上下文条件下，表现仍不完美。即使模型初始倾向于纠正误解，其后续轮次表现显著下降，导致面向患者的指导不一致且可能不安全，凸显亟需能捕捉多轮行为的评估框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate how large language models handle misconceptions in real-world multi-turn medical conversations, this study introduces ThReadMed-QA, a dataset of 2,437 patient-physician dialogue threads derived from real interactions. Using a rubric-based LLM-as-a-Judge framework, five models were assessed on their ability to detect and correct false assumptions across multiple conversational turns. Results show that even top-tier models like GPT-5 and Claude-Haiku, which correct misconceptions at around 85% accuracy on initial questions, drop to roughly 50% accuracy after two follow-up turns, indicating significant degradation due to error propagation. The study underscores the critical need for evaluation methods that account for multi-turn dynamics to ensure safe and consistent medical guidance.
为评估大语言模型在多轮医疗对话中识别并纠正患者误解的能力，研究构建了ThReadMed-QA数据集（2437条真实患者-医生对话线），并采用基于评分标准的LLM裁判框架对五种模型进行评估。实验发现，即使如GPT-5和Claude-Haiku这类顶尖模型，在首轮问题中纠正误解的准确率约85%，但在后续两轮对话中骤降至约50%，表明性能显著衰退主要源于错误传播。即便在上下文完全正确的情况下，模型仍无法保持稳定表现，凸显了在真实医疗场景中可能产生不安全引导的风险，亟需支持多轮交互评估的框架。
Inhibited Self-Attention: Sharpening Focus in Vision Transformers
Authors: Peter R. D. van der Wal, Nicola Strisciuglio, George Azzopardi
First: 2026-07-14T15:29:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:29:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision Transformers (ViTs) have demonstrated remarkable performance in computer vision tasks. However, their self-attention mechanism often diffuses focus across background regions, relying on spurious correlations rather than object-relevant cues. Inspired by inhibitory mechanisms observed in biological vision systems, we propose the Inhibited Self-Attention (ISA), a novel self-attention that integrates inhibitory signals to enhance feature selectivity and suppress spurious responses. In contrast to conventional self-attention, which relies solely on positive attention values due to softmax normalization, our approach retains and utilizes negative attention scores to suppress irrelevant features and sharpen focus on objects of interest. Experiments across multiple datasets, including ImageNet-1k and COCO, and several robustness benchmarks demonstrate that ISA enhances object-centric selectivity, reduces shortcut reliance, and improves out-of-distribution generalization. Our analysis of relevance maps confirms that ViTs with ISA exhibit sharper, more localized focus on object-relevant regions while reducing distractions from non-relevant (background) features, enabling more reliable models. We release our code at https://github.com/prdvanderwal/inhibited-self-attention
中文标题/摘要
标题：抑制自注意力：提升视觉Transformer的聚焦能力
视觉Transformer（ViTs）在计算机视觉任务中表现出色，但其自注意力机制常将注意力分散至背景区域，依赖虚假相关性而非物体相关线索。受生物视觉系统中抑制机制启发，我们提出抑制自注意力（ISA），一种整合抑制信号的新型自注意力机制，以增强特征选择性并抑制虚假响应。与传统自注意力仅依赖softmax归一化后的正值不同，我们的方法保留并利用负值注意力分数，以抑制无关特征并聚焦于目标物体。在ImageNet-1k和COCO等多个数据集及多个鲁棒性基准测试中，ISA提升了以物体为中心的选择性，减少了捷径依赖，并改善了分布外泛化能力。相关性图分析证实，采用ISA的ViT在物体相关区域表现出更锐利、更局部的聚焦，同时减少非相关（背景）特征的干扰，从而构建更可靠的模型。代码已开源：https://github.com/prdvanderwal/inhibited-self-attention
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the tendency of Vision Transformers to spread attention across irrelevant background regions, this work introduces Inhibited Self-Attention, a mechanism that leverages inhibitory signals to suppress spurious correlations and enhance object-focused feature selection. Unlike standard self-attention that discards negative attention scores due to softmax normalization, ISA retains and utilizes them to sharpen focus on relevant objects. Experiments on ImageNet-1k and COCO show ISA improves object-centric selectivity, reduces reliance on shortcuts, and enhances out-of-distribution generalization, with relevance maps confirming more localized and accurate attention patterns.
为了解决视觉Transformer在注意力机制中过度关注背景区域、依赖虚假关联的问题，本文提出抑制自注意力（ISA）机制，通过引入抑制信号增强特征选择性并抑制无关响应。与传统自注意力仅使用softmax归一化后的正注意力值不同，ISA保留并利用负注意力分数以抑制非目标特征，聚焦于目标物体。在ImageNet-1k、COCO及多个鲁棒性基准测试中，ISA显著提升目标导向选择性、减少捷径依赖，并增强分布外泛化能力，相关性热图验证其注意力更集中于物体区域、减少背景干扰。
Metric-Guided Synthetic Image Data Rendering for Deep Learning compatible with Agentic AI
Authors: Martina Radoynova, Samuel Pantze, Trina De, Ulrik Günther, Artur Yakimovich
First: 2026-07-14T15:22:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:22:26+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 3 figures, 4 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep learning computer vision for scientific applications requires collecting and annotating large datasets in a laborious, expensive and error-prone process. Synthetic data generation through 3D modelling and rendering may simplify this process and increase the accuracy of annotations by generating them programmatically. However, minimising the domain gap between real and synthetic images visually is subjective and lacks systematic quantitative guidance. We present GraNatPy, a Python package with metrics to guide improvement of the rendered scene. We show that quantifiable increase in realism, diversity and size of rendered dataset correlates with improved visual perception of the scene and higher zero-shot performance of an object detection model. Furthermore, we demonstrated using photographs of virological plaque assays that gradient similarity affects performance on small object detection, which can be improved by mixing real and synthetic data. Finally, we turn procedural data rendering into an agentic skill (SynthClaw) to automate the procedural parameter optimisation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于度量引导的合成图像数据渲染，适用于代理式人工智能的深度学习
科学应用中的深度学习计算机视觉需要收集和标注大量数据，这一过程费时、昂贵且易出错。通过3D建模和渲染生成合成数据可能简化该过程，并通过程序化生成提高标注准确性。然而，缩小真实图像与合成图像之间的视觉域差距是主观的，缺乏系统性的定量指导。我们提出了GraNatPy，一个带有度量指标的Python工具包，用于指导渲染场景的改进。我们表明，渲染数据集在真实感、多样性和规模上的可量化提升，与场景视觉感知的改善及目标检测模型的零样本性能提升相关。此外，我们通过病毒斑点试验照片证明，梯度相似性影响小目标检测性能，可通过混合真实与合成数据加以改善。最后，我们将程序化数据渲染转化为代理技能（SynthClaw），以自动化程序化参数优化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and error-proneness of annotating real-world scientific datasets, this work proposes GraNatPy, a Python toolkit that uses quantitative metrics to guide the generation of synthetic images via 3D rendering, aiming to reduce the domain gap between real and synthetic data. The method evaluates rendered scenes by measuring realism, diversity, and dataset size, and correlates these metrics with human visual perception and zero-shot object detection performance. Experiments using virological plaque assay images show that gradient similarity between real and synthetic data significantly impacts small-object detection accuracy, which improves when real and synthetic data are mixed. The study further introduces SynthClaw, an agentic system that automates procedural parameter tuning for synthetic data generation.
为解决科学视觉任务中真实数据标注成本高、易出错的问题，作者提出GraNatPy工具包，通过量化指标指导合成图像的渲染，以缩小真实与合成图像间的视觉与领域差距。实验表明，提升图像真实感、多样性与数据集规模的量化指标，与视觉感知质量及零样本目标检测性能正相关。在病毒斑点试验图像上，研究发现梯度相似性对小目标检测性能影响显著，混合真实与合成数据可改善效果。作者进一步通过SynthClaw系统，将程序化渲染参数优化自动化，实现代理智能体自主调参。
Deep4ge: DNN Training Trajectories for Fault Detection and Diagnosis
Authors: Sigma Jahan
First: 2026-07-14T15:20:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:20:16+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICSME Data & Tool Track, 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep learning systems often fail due to subtle implementation faults that alter training behavior. Recent work has studied how to detect and diagnose such failures from changes observed across training epochs. However, the software engineering community still lacks a public dataset of per-epoch training runs with documented fault history, feature extraction details, and clear reuse support for fault detection and diagnosis tasks. We present Deep4ge, a controlled benchmark of 14,227 training runs generated from 59 adapted TensorFlow/Keras deep neural network (DNN) programs collected from Stack Overflow. We generated faulty variants using 27 source-code transformations that introduce known faults across seven categories. The dataset contains 9,845 faulty runs and 4,382 correct baseline runs. For each run, we record 4 evaluation metrics and 26 features that measure training behavior at every epoch. These features capture weights, gradients, activations, accuracy and loss trends, learning rate, and hardware use. Deep4ge supports binary fault detection, multi-class fault diagnosis, and early fault prediction from partial training runs. We release the dataset and fault-injection framework at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20337241.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Deep4ge：用于故障检测与诊断的深度神经网络训练轨迹
深度学习系统常因细微的实现错误而失效，这些错误会改变训练行为。近期研究探讨了如何从训练各轮次的观测变化中检测和诊断此类故障。然而，软件工程领域仍缺乏一个公开的、包含每轮训练运行记录、故障历史文档、特征提取细节，并支持故障检测与诊断任务复用的数据集。我们提出了Deep4ge，这是一个由59个从Stack Overflow收集并修改的TensorFlow/Keras深度神经网络（DNN）程序生成的14,227次训练运行的受控基准数据集。我们通过27种源代码变换引入已知故障，覆盖七类故障。数据集包含9,845次故障运行和4,382次正确基线运行。对每次运行，我们记录4个评估指标和26个特征，用于衡量每轮训练行为，包括权重、梯度、激活值、准确率与损失趋势、学习率及硬件使用情况。Deep4ge支持二分类故障检测、多分类故障诊断及基于部分训练运行的早期故障预测。我们已在https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20337241发布该数据集和故障注入框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of standardized data for detecting and diagnosing faults in deep learning training, the authors introduce Deep4ge, a controlled benchmark comprising 14,227 training runs from 59 adapted DNN programs. Faulty variants were generated via 27 source-code transformations across seven fault categories, yielding 9,845 faulty runs and 4,382 correct baselines. Each run is annotated with 4 evaluation metrics and 26 behavioral features capturing weights, gradients, activations, loss trends, learning rate, and hardware usage. Experiments demonstrate Deep4ge’s effectiveness in binary fault detection, multi-class fault diagnosis, and early fault prediction using partial training data.
为解决深度学习系统中因细微实现错误导致训练行为异常而缺乏标准化评估基准的问题，作者构建了Deep4ge数据集，包含从59个修改后的TensorFlow/Keras模型生成的14,227次训练运行，其中9,845次为通过27种源码变换引入七类已知错误的故障版本。每轮训练记录4个评估指标和26个行为特征，涵盖权重、梯度、激活值、损失趋势、学习率及硬件使用情况。实验表明，该数据集可有效支持二分类故障检测、多分类故障诊断及基于部分训练过程的早期故障预测，为自动化故障检测工具的开发与评估提供可复用资源。
Statistical Non-linear Reconstruction Loss for Image Anomaly Detection
Authors: Nguyen Minh Tri, Hoang Khuong Duy, Huynh Cong Viet Ngu
First: 2026-07-14T15:18:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:18:24+00:00
Comments: Accepted at KES 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reconstruction-based methods are a cornerstone of unsupervised image anomaly detection, but they remain vulnerable to \emph{outlier leakage}, where standard mean squared error (MSE) loss drives the model to faithfully reconstruct anomalous patterns. We propose a Non-linear Reconstruction Loss that applies a sigmoid-based squashing function to suppress high-magnitude features, preventing outliers from dominating optimization while preserving sensitivity to normal patterns. In addition, we introduce a statistical calibration scheme that selects the scaling factor $k$ from the confidence interval (CI) of the normal feature distribution, enabling data-driven control of the suppression strength. Our approach achieves competitive or superior anomaly detection performance compared to state-of-the-art methods, reaching 99.0\% Image-AUROC and 97.3\% Pixel-AUROC on MVTec-AD, and 95.3\% Image-AUROC and 99.0\% Pixel-AUROC on VisA. These results indicate that non-linear gradient suppression is an effective mechanism for mitigating outlier leakage and improving anomaly localization in unified industrial inspection settings. The implementation is available at https://github.com/mintii13/Statistical-Non-linear-Reconstruction-Loss.git.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于图像异常检测的统计非线性重建损失
基于重建的方法是无监督图像异常检测的基石，但它们仍易受\emph{异常值泄漏}影响，即标准均方误差（MSE）损失会促使模型忠实地重建异常模式。我们提出一种非线性重建损失，通过基于sigmoid的挤压函数抑制高幅值特征，防止异常值主导优化过程，同时保留对正常模式的敏感性。此外，我们引入一种统计校准方案，从正常特征分布的置信区间（CI）中选择缩放因子$k$，实现数据驱动的抑制强度控制。我们的方法在性能上达到或优于当前最先进方法，在MVTec-AD上实现99.0\%图像AUROC和97.3\%像素AUROC，在VisA上实现95.3\%图像AUROC和99.0\%像素AUROC。这些结果表明，非线性梯度抑制是缓解异常值泄漏、提升统一工业检测场景下异常定位的有效机制。代码实现见https://github.com/mintii13/Statistical-Non-linear-Reconstruction-Loss.git。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of reconstruction-based anomaly detection to outlier leakage, where MSE loss overfits to anomalous patterns, this work introduces a Non-linear Reconstruction Loss using a sigmoid-based squashing function to suppress high-magnitude features. It further calibrates the suppression strength via a statistical scaling factor derived from the confidence interval of normal feature distributions. Experiments on MVTec-AD and VisA show the method achieves competitive or superior performance, reaching 99.0% Image-AUROC and 97.3% Pixel-AUROC on MVTec-AD, and 95.3% Image-AUROC and 99.0% Pixel-AUROC on VisA, demonstrating its effectiveness in mitigating outlier leakage and enhancing anomaly localization.
为解决重建式图像异常检测中因标准MSE损失导致的异常模式过拟合问题，本文提出一种非线性重建损失，通过Sigmoid挤压函数抑制高幅值特征，并结合正常特征分布的置信区间动态选择缩放因子以自适应控制抑制强度。在MVTec-AD和VisA数据集上的实验表明，该方法表现优异，分别达到99.0%图像AUROC和97.3%像素AUROC，以及95.3%图像AUROC和99.0%像素AUROC，验证了其在抑制异常泄漏和提升异常定位方面的有效性。
A Biomimetic Myoelectric Tentacle Prosthesis with Sensorless Object Detection and Vibrotactile Feedback
Authors: Gabrielle Marion, Olivier Lecompte, Amandine Gesta, Parsa Maghsoudloo, Abolfazl Mohebbi
First: 2026-07-09T20:26:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:17:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents the design and evaluation of a myoelectric tentacle-shaped prosthesis integrating electromyographic (EMG) control, sensorless object detection, and vibrotactile feedback. The objective was to develop a responsive and intuitive assistive device that adapts to various object shapes while providing sensory feedback to the user. The system relies on EMG signals to control the motion of a flexible, biomimetic structure whose curling geometry follows a logarithmic spiral, enabling it to coil around objects. To ensure stable control, the EMG signal is normalized and filtered, and a threshold-based method identifies user intention. Object contact is detected through a slope-based analysis of motor current, eliminating the need for external sensors, and a haptic feedback strategy based on cumulative vibrotactile stimulation conveys spatial information about the tentacle's configuration. The system was evaluated through quantitative and qualitative tests. The results demonstrate a low response time (77 ms on average), enabling smooth real-time interaction; an object-detection success rate above 90%, confirming robustness despite EMG variability; and an effective haptic feedback strategy that allowed users to reliably identify the folding zone of the tentacle. The proposed biomimetic design promotes further investigation of expressive artificial limbs by prioritizing expressive functionality over adherence to a predefined, anthropomorphic form factor.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种无传感器物体检测与振动触觉反馈的仿生肌电触手假肢
本文提出并评估了一种集成肌电（EMG）控制、无传感器物体检测和振动触觉反馈的肌电触手状假肢。目标是开发一种响应灵敏、直观的辅助设备，能适应不同物体形状并为用户提供触觉反馈。系统通过EMG信号控制柔性仿生结构的运动，其卷曲几何形状遵循对数螺旋，使其能缠绕物体。为确保稳定控制，EMG信号经归一化和滤波处理，采用阈值法识别用户意图。物体接触通过电机电流斜率分析检测，无需外部传感器；基于累积振动刺激的触觉反馈策略传递触手构型的空间信息。系统通过定量与定性测试评估，结果显示平均响应时间仅77毫秒，支持流畅实时交互；物体检测成功率超90%，即使在EMG信号波动下仍具鲁棒性；触觉反馈策略有效，使用户可可靠识别触手折叠区域。该仿生设计优先考虑表达性功能而非固定的人形外形，推动表达性仿生假肢的进一步研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for intuitive, adaptive prosthetic devices that mimic biological limb behavior, this study designed a myoelectric tentacle prosthesis using EMG control, sensorless object detection, and vibrotactile feedback. The tentacle’s flexible structure follows a logarithmic spiral to coil around objects, with EMG signals normalized and thresholded to interpret user intent. Object contact is inferred from motor current slope without external sensors, and vibrotactile feedback conveys spatial configuration through cumulative stimulation. Experimental results show a 77 ms average response time, over 90% object detection accuracy despite EMG variability, and successful user identification of the tentacle’s folding zone via haptic cues.
