Imputation-free transformer learning enables robust Alzheimer's disease prediction and calibrated uncertainty quantification across heterogeneous clinical cohorts
Authors: Christelle Schneuwly Diaz, Narmina Baghirova, Duy-Thanh Vu, Duy-Cat Can, Gilles Allali, Philippe Ryvlin, Oliver Y. Chén
First: 2026-07-13T15:05:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:16:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate diagnostic classification and disease-severity prediction for Alzheimer's disease are hampered by the incompleteness and heterogeneity of real-world clinical data. Left unaddressed, these barriers prevent reliable disease modelling and hinder effective clinical evaluation. Conventional imputation strategies introduce systematic bias, distort inter-feature relationships, and yield overconfident predictions, limitations especially consequential in diagnostic settings. Here, we propose NITROGEN, an imputation-free transformer that jointly models within-patient feature dependencies and between-patient relational structure through masked and intersample attention, enabling robust multimodal learning directly from partially observed records. We trained NITROGEN on ADNI (N=7858 scans), and evaluated it on two independent cohorts: OASIS-3 (N=2675 scans) and AIBL (N=1286 scans). Across cohorts and diagnostic and cognitive score prediction tasks, NITROGEN showed robust calibration and uncertainty quantification advantages over tree-based ensemble methods, while maintaining competitive discriminative performance. Cross-cohort and cross-method analyses identified cortical thickness in the temporal pole, age, and APOE genotype as important, though not individually sufficient, features for AD classification. We further introduced a modality-aware uncertainty adjustment that augments predictive uncertainty proportionally to the importance of absent modalities, enabling calibrated confidence when diagnostic information is unavailable. Together, our results show that imputation-free attention learning preserved meaningful discrimination under cohort shift, revealing expected degradation on more distributionally different cohorts, and demonstrate that evaluating models along calibration, interpretability, and cross-cohort reliability, not accuracy alone, is essential for clinical deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需填补的变压器学习实现阿尔茨海默病稳健预测及校准不确定性量化，适用于异质临床队列
阿尔茨海默病的准确诊断分类和疾病严重程度预测因现实临床数据的不完整性和异质性而受阻。若不解决这些问题，将阻碍可靠疾病建模和有效临床评估。传统填补策略会引入系统性偏差、扭曲特征间关系并导致过度自信的预测，尤其在诊断场景中后果严重。本文提出NITROGEN，一种无需填补的变压器，通过掩码和跨样本注意力机制联合建模患者内特征依赖关系和患者间关系结构，直接从部分观测记录中实现稳健的多模态学习。我们在ADNI队列（N=7858次扫描）上训练NITROGEN，并在两个独立队列OASIS-3（N=2675次扫描）和AIBL（N=1286次扫描）上评估。在多个队列和诊断与认知评分预测任务中，NITROGEN在校准和不确定性量化方面优于基于树的集成方法，同时保持竞争力的判别性能。跨队列和跨方法分析发现，颞极皮层厚度、年龄和APOE基因型是重要的AD分类特征，但单个特征不足以充分分类。我们进一步引入模态感知的不确定性调整机制，根据缺失模态的重要性成比例增强预测不确定性，从而在诊断信息缺失时提供校准的置信度。综合结果表明，无需填补的注意力学习在队列偏移下仍能保持有意义的判别能力，预期在分布差异更大的队列中性能下降，且证明在临床部署中，除准确率外，校准、可解释性和跨队列可靠性评估至关重要。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenges of incomplete and heterogeneous clinical data in Alzheimer’s disease prediction, the authors developed NITROGEN, an imputation-free transformer that models both within-patient feature dependencies and between-patient relational structures using masked and intersample attention. Trained on the ADNI cohort and evaluated across three independent datasets, NITROGEN demonstrated superior calibration and uncertainty quantification compared to tree-based ensembles while maintaining strong discriminative performance. Key findings identified cortical thickness in the temporal pole, age, and APOE genotype as critical features, though not individually sufficient for diagnosis. The model also introduced a modality-aware uncertainty adjustment that scales confidence based on missing data importance, enhancing reliability when diagnostic information is incomplete.
为解决阿尔茨海默病临床数据不完整和异质性导致的诊断困难，作者提出NITROGEN模型，一种无需填补缺失值的Transformer架构，通过掩码和跨样本注意力机制联合建模患者内特征依赖与患者间关系结构。在ADNI、OASIS-3和AIBL三个队列中评估，NITROGEN在不确定性量化和校准方面优于树集成方法，同时保持相当的判别性能。关键发现指出颞极皮层厚度、年龄和APOE基因型是重要特征，但单独不足以完成分类。引入的模态感知不确定性调整机制可按缺失模态的重要性调整预测不确定性，提升诊断信息缺失时的置信度校准，强调临床部署中校准、可解释性与跨队列可靠性的重要性。
Toward Localizing and Repairing Bias in Transformer Attention Heads
Authors: Sigma Jahan
First: 2026-07-14T15:15:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:15:16+00:00
Comments: Accepted in ICSME NIER track, 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformer language models are increasingly used as software components, yet biased outputs remain difficult to localize and repair inside the model. Existing fairness testing and repair methods largely operate at the input-output or retraining level, while recent work suggests that bias-related behavior can concentrate in a small set of attention heads. This paper studies whether attention heads can be localized and repaired through a targeted inference-time intervention. We introduce ROBIN, a white-box head-level fairness debugging method that ranks attention heads using sensitivity to fairness probes and removes a small bias subspace from selected head outputs. In a four-model pilot study, ROBIN reduces the measured WinoBias gap across all models while preserving language-modeling quality better than whole-head zeroing. These preliminary results suggest that head-level bias repair should consider not only which heads are selected, but also how selected heads are modified.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向Transformer注意力头的偏见定位与修复
Transformer语言模型日益被用作软件组件，但其偏见输出仍难以在模型内部定位和修复。现有公平性测试与修复方法主要在输入-输出或再训练层面操作，而近期研究表明，与偏见相关的行为可能集中在少量注意力头中。本文研究是否可通过针对性的推理时干预定位并修复注意力头。我们提出了ROBIN，一种白盒级注意力头公平性调试方法，通过公平性探针的敏感度对注意力头进行排序，并从选定头的输出中移除一小部分偏见子空间。在四个模型的试点研究中，ROBIN在所有模型中均缩小了WinoBias差距，同时比全头置零更好地保持语言建模质量。初步结果表明，头级偏见修复不仅应考虑选择哪些头，还应关注如何修改选定的头。
Summary / 总结
As transformer models increasingly serve as software components, their biased outputs remain hard to pinpoint and fix within the model. This paper proposes ROBIN, a white-box method that targets individual attention heads to localize and repair bias by ranking them based on sensitivity to fairness probes and selectively removing a bias subspace from selected head outputs. In a pilot study across four models, ROBIN successfully reduced the WinoBias gap while preserving language modeling performance better than simply zeroing entire heads, indicating that head-level bias repair must consider both selection and modification strategies.
为解决作为软件组件的Transformer模型中偏见难以定位和修复的问题，本文提出ROBIN方法，通过白盒方式对注意力头进行针对性调试。ROBIN依据各头对公平性探针的敏感度进行排序，并仅从选定头中移除偏见子空间，而非整头置零。在四个模型上的实验表明，ROBIN能有效缩小WinoBias差距，同时比整头置零更好地保持语言建模能力，说明头级偏见修复需兼顾头的选择与修改方式。
RESOURCE2SKILL: Distilling Executable Agent Skills from Human-Created Multimodal Resources
Authors: Yijia Fan, Zonglin Di, Zimo Wen, Yifan Yang, Mingxi Cheng, Qi Dai, Bei Liu, Kai Qiu, Yue Dong, Ji Li, Chong Luo
First: 2026-06-28T17:59:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:15:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Skills are a useful abstraction for software agents, turning human and agent experience into reusable procedural knowledge. Yet existing skill libraries are mostly hand-written, text-centric, or derived from agent traces, leaving tutorial videos and other multimodal human resources largely underused. We present RESOURCE2SKILL, a framework that distills multimodal resources, including tutorial videos, repositories, articles, and reference artifacts, into executable skills for software agents. RESOURCE2SKILL organizes these skills as a hierarchical multimodal Skill Wiki, where each entry combines structured text, code, visual examples, metadata, and provenance. This design preserves complementary signals from different resources: videos capture temporal operations and visual effects, code captures executable tool patterns, and articles or artifacts provide conceptual and stylistic grounding. At inference time, agents retrieve and compose relevant skills from the wiki; when coverage is insufficient, the same construction operator can acquire new skills online. Across seven practical authoring domains, RESOURCE2SKILL improves average overall score by +11.9 percentage points over no-skill agents and outperforms strong harness baselines in 26 of 28 main-aggregate model-domain cells. Ablations confirm the value of multimodal skill format, hierarchical organization, source diversity, selection strategy, and online acquisition.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RESOURCE2SKILL：从人类创建的多模态资源中提炼可执行的代理技能
技能是对软件代理有用的抽象，将人类和代理的经验转化为可复用的程序性知识。然而，现有技能库大多由人工编写、以文本为中心，或源自代理轨迹，导致教程视频等多模态人类资源被大量忽视。我们提出了RESOURCE2SKILL框架，将教程视频、仓库、文章和参考制品等多模态资源提炼为软件代理的可执行技能。RESOURCE2SKILL将这些技能组织为一个分层的多模态技能维基，每个条目结合结构化文本、代码、视觉示例、元数据和溯源信息。该设计保留了不同资源的互补信号：视频捕捉时序操作和视觉效果，代码捕捉可执行工具模式，文章或制品提供概念和风格基础。推理时，代理从维基中检索并组合相关技能；当覆盖不足时，同一构建操作符可在线获取新技能。在七个实际创作领域中，RESOURCE2SKILL相比无技能代理平均整体得分提升11.9个百分点，并在28个主聚合模型-领域单元中的26个中优于强基线方法。消融实验验证了多模态技能格式、分层组织、源多样性、选择策略和在线获取的价值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underutilization of multimodal human resources like tutorial videos and code repositories in agent skill development, RESOURCE2SKILL introduces a framework that distills these diverse sources into executable agent skills. It structures skills as a hierarchical multimodal Skill Wiki, integrating text, code, visuals, metadata, and provenance to preserve complementary signals from each resource type. Experiments across seven authoring domains show the framework improves average performance by +11.9 percentage points over no-skill agents and beats strong baselines in 26 of 28 model-domain combinations, with ablations confirming the importance of multimodal format, hierarchy, source diversity, selection, and online skill acquisition.
为解决教程视频和代码仓库等多模态人类资源在智能体技能构建中被忽视的问题，RESOURCE2SKILL提出将这些资源提炼为可执行技能，构建一个分层多模态技能维基，整合结构化文本、代码、视觉示例、元数据与溯源信息，以保留不同模态的互补信号。在七个实际创作领域中，该框架相较无技能智能体平均性能提升11.9个百分点，并在28个模型-领域组合中的26个上优于强基线方法，消融实验验证了多模态格式、分层结构、资源多样性、选择策略及在线获取能力的重要性。
How Inference Compute Shapes Frontier LLM Evaluation
Authors: Jessica McFadyen, Ole Jorgensen, Harry Coppock, Kevin Wei, Cozmin Ududec
First: 2026-06-16T13:40:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:12:22+00:00
Comments: 34 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI evaluations are shifting toward harder tasks that benefit from longer trajectories involving tool use and iterative problem solving. As a result, performance is increasingly sensitive to the amount and allocation of compute available at test time ("inference compute"). Yet many evaluations still report performance at a single restrictive budget, meaning that low scores may reflect the evaluation setup rather than the model's underlying capability. To test this, we evaluate up to 12 frontier language models on seven challenging benchmarks spanning software engineering, mathematics, medicine, and cybersecurity. We use a controlled setup combining three simple inference-scaling interventions: larger token budgets, context compaction, and repeated submission attempts, guided either by the model itself or by minimal correctness feedback. We find three main results. First, larger token budgets substantially improve performance on benchmarks across multiple domains, including cybersecurity, FrontierMath, Humanity's Last Exam, and TerminalBench. Second, fixed-budget evaluations can increasingly understate frontier capability as models advance. Newer models reach higher performance at large budgets, where they unlock harder tasks and solve them more reliably. Third, benchmarks differ in which inference-scaling methods help most: repeated submission broadly improves performance, but the value of larger token budgets, external feedback, and parallel attempts varies by benchmark. Overall, our results show that benchmark scores are protocol-dependent. We therefore argue that evaluations should report capability as a function of inference-time compute, specify protocol choices explicitly, and compare model generations over a large shared compute range at matched budgets, especially in safety- or policy-relevant settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：推理计算如何塑造前沿大语言模型的评估
AI评估正转向更困难的任务，这些任务受益于涉及工具使用和迭代问题解决的更长轨迹。因此，性能越来越依赖于测试时可用的计算量（“推理计算”）的多少和分配方式。然而，许多评估仍仅在单一受限预算下报告性能，这意味着低分可能反映的是评估设置而非模型的底层能力。为检验这一点，我们对12个前沿语言模型在涵盖软件工程、数学、医学和网络安全的七个挑战性基准上进行了评估。我们采用受控设置，结合三种简单的推理扩展干预措施：更大的token预算、上下文压缩和重复提交尝试，由模型自身或极简正确性反馈引导。我们发现三个主要结果：第一，更大的token预算显著提升了多个领域基准（包括网络安全、FrontierMath、Humanity's Last Exam和TerminalBench）的性能；第二，随着模型进步，固定预算评估越来越低估前沿能力，新模型在大预算下能实现更高性能，解锁更难任务并更可靠地解决；第三，不同基准对推理扩展方法的依赖不同：重复提交普遍提升性能，但更大token预算、外部反馈和并行尝试的价值因基准而异。总体而言，我们的结果表明基准分数具有协议依赖性。因此，我们主张评估应将能力表示为推理时计算量的函数，明确说明协议选择，并在安全或政策相关场景中，于匹配预算下，通过共享的大规模计算范围比较模型代际性能。
Summary / 总结
As AI evaluations shift toward harder tasks requiring tool use and iterative problem solving, performance becomes highly sensitive to inference compute allocation. To assess whether low scores reflect model limitations or restrictive evaluation protocols, the study evaluates 12 frontier language models across seven challenging benchmarks using three controlled inference-scaling interventions: larger token budgets, context compaction, and repeated submission attempts guided by model or minimal feedback. Results show that larger token budgets significantly boost performance across domains including cybersecurity and mathematics; fixed-budget evaluations increasingly fail to capture true model capability as newer models excel at high compute; and benchmark responsiveness to scaling methods varies, with repeated submissions universally helpful but token budget effectiveness differing by task. The study concludes that benchmark scores are protocol-dependent and recommends reporting capability as a function of inference compute, explicitly specifying protocols, and comparing models across matched compute budgets, especially in safety-critical contexts.
鉴于推理计算在评估前沿语言模型中的关键作用，本研究探讨了模型性能如何随测试时计算资源变化。作者在七个跨领域挑战性基准上评估了12个大模型，采用三种控制性推理扩展方法：增加token预算、上下文压缩和由模型或最小反馈引导的多次提交。结果表明，更大的token预算在多个领域（如网络安全和数学）显著提升性能。固定预算评估越来越无法反映模型真实能力，新模型在高计算资源下能解锁更难任务并更可靠地解决。不同基准对扩展方法的敏感度不同：多次提交普遍有效，而token预算和外部反馈的价值因任务而异。研究结论指出，基准分数依赖于评估协议，建议以推理计算为变量报告能力，并明确说明协议选择，尤其在安全或政策相关场景中应进行匹配预算比较。
Unveiling Complex Collective Behaviors from Simple Rewards
Authors: Yize Mi, Jianan Li, Liang Li, Shiyu Zhao
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-07-14T15:11:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:11:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted by IROS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) holds great potential for robot swarms, but the black-box nature of neural policies complicates strategic analysis, limiting multi-robot applications. Furthermore, complex swarm behaviors can surprisingly emerge from simple rewards without explicit aggregation incentives. Unveiling the mechanisms behind this emergence is critical, but the disconnection between simple rewards and collective behaviors exacerbates interpretability challenges. This paper aims to reveal the hidden mechanisms in this process. We propose a two-stage EEC (\LinkIII) explanatory framework. This includes a novel analytical tool called the Agent Response Map (ARM), which reveals agents' decision-making patterns across space and identifies regions of aggregation and avoidance. ARM reveals that the robots implicitly learn the geometric fields of the environment and utilize these structures as desired targets for coordinated movement. We validate this finding across two distinct tasks: a cooperative multi-robot shape assembly and a competitive predator-prey pursuit-evasion. 1) In the cooperative task, ARM identifies the unoccupied target interior as the desired destination for robot navigation. As the center becomes occupied, this target region automatically shifts toward the boundary, demonstrating the robots' capacity to autonomously explore unoccupied areas. 2) In the competitive task, ARM surprisingly identifies the boundary of the predators' Voronoi diagram as the convergence destination for prey agents. Together, these two tasks demonstrate the capability of ARM to discover the hidden geometric structures underlying MARL policies in robot swarms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从简单奖励揭示复杂群体行为
多智能体强化学习（MARL）在机器人集群中具有巨大潜力，但神经策略的黑箱特性使战略分析复杂化，限制了多机器人应用。此外，简单奖励下可意外涌现出复杂群体行为，而无需显式聚合激励。揭示其涌现机制至关重要，但简单奖励与群体行为之间的脱节加剧了可解释性挑战。本文旨在揭示该过程中的隐藏机制。我们提出一种两阶段EEC（\LinkIII）解释框架，包含一种名为“智能体响应图”（ARM）的新型分析工具，可揭示智能体在空间中的决策模式，并识别聚集与回避区域。ARM显示机器人隐式学习环境几何场，并利用这些结构作为协调运动的期望目标。我们在两个不同任务中验证了该发现：协作多机器人形状组装与竞争性捕食者-猎物追逃。1）在协作任务中，ARM识别出未被占据的目标内部区域为机器人导航的期望目的地；当中心被占据时，该目标区域自动移向边界，表明机器人能自主探索未被占据区域。2）在竞争任务中，ARM意外发现猎物智能体的汇聚目标为捕食者Voronoi图的边界。这两个任务共同证明ARM能够发现MARL策略在机器人集群中所隐含的几何结构。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of interpreting complex collective behaviors in multi-agent reinforcement learning systems, where simple rewards can lead to emergent coordination without explicit design. The authors introduce a two-stage EEC framework featuring the Agent Response Map (ARM), a novel analytical tool that visualizes how agents respond across spatial regions, revealing implicit geometric structures guiding their behavior. Experimental results across two tasks—cooperative shape assembly and competitive pursuit-evasion—show that ARM identifies unoccupied interior regions as navigation targets in the cooperative task, which dynamically shift as space fills, and the Voronoi boundary of predators as the convergence point for prey in the competitive task, demonstrating ARM’s ability to uncover hidden spatial patterns underlying MARL policies.
该论文旨在揭示多智能体强化学习系统中，简单奖励如何自发引导复杂群体行为。作者提出EEC框架中的Agent Response Map（ARM）工具，通过可视化智能体在空间中的响应模式，发现其隐含地利用环境几何结构作为协同运动目标。实验在协作形状组装与竞争捕食者-猎物追逃任务中验证：协作任务中，ARM识别出未被占据的内部区域为目标，随中心被占自动移向边界；竞争任务中，猎物收敛于捕食者Voronoi图边界。结果表明ARM能有效揭示MARL策略背后隐藏的空间结构。
Evolution Strategies at Scale: LLM Fine-Tuning Beyond Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Xin Qiu, Yulu Gan, Conor F. Hayes, Qiyao Liang, Yinggan Xu, Roberto Dailey, Elliot Meyerson, Babak Hodjat, Risto Miikkulainen
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-09-29T07:19:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:11:12+00:00
Comments: Published at ICML 2026 main conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) for downstream tasks is an essential stage of modern AI deployment. Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as the dominant fine-tuning paradigm, underpinning many state-of-the-art LLMs. In contrast, evolution strategies (ES) has largely been overlooked due to the widespread belief that it does not scale to modern model sizes. This paper overturns this assumption by demonstrating the first successful application of ES to full-parameter fine-tuning of LLMs at the billion-parameter scale, without dimensionality reduction. ES can indeed search over extremely high-dimensional parameter spaces and outperform established RL implementations across multiple axes, including improved tolerance to long-horizon and delayed rewards, robustness across diverse base LLMs, reduced susceptibility to reward hacking, and improved training stability. These findings suggest that ES is not merely a viable alternative to RL, but a fundamentally different and powerful backpropagation-free post-training paradigm that opens a new direction for LLM fine-tuning beyond current RL-based approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大规模进化策略：超越强化学习的LLM微调
为下游任务微调大语言模型（LLM）是现代AI部署的关键阶段。强化学习（RL）已成为主导的微调范式，支撑着许多最先进的LLM。相比之下，进化策略（ES）因普遍认为其无法扩展到现代模型规模而被忽视。本文推翻这一假设，首次成功将ES应用于十亿参数规模的LLM全参数微调，无需降维。ES确实能在极高维参数空间中搜索，并在多个维度上超越现有RL实现，包括对长时程和延迟奖励的更强容忍度、对多种基础LLM的鲁棒性、更少的奖励作弊倾向以及更稳定的训练表现。这些发现表明，ES不仅是一种可行的RL替代方案，更是一种根本不同且强大的无反向传播后训练范式，为超越当前RL方法的LLM微调开辟了新方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the belief that evolution strategies (ES) cannot scale to modern large language models (LLMs), this paper challenges that assumption by successfully applying full-parameter ES fine-tuning to billion-parameter LLMs without dimensionality reduction. The method leverages evolutionary optimization to search high-dimensional parameter spaces, bypassing gradient-based backpropagation. Experimental results show ES outperforms reinforcement learning across multiple dimensions: it handles long-horizon and delayed rewards more effectively, demonstrates robustness across diverse base models, resists reward hacking, and offers greater training stability, suggesting ES is a viable and distinct post-training paradigm beyond RL.
针对进化策略（ES）被认为无法扩展到现代大语言模型（LLM）的普遍观点，本文通过在不进行维度压缩的前提下，成功将ES应用于十亿参数级别的LLM全参数微调，推翻了这一假设。该方法利用进化优化在高维参数空间中搜索，无需梯度反向传播。实验结果表明，ES在多个维度上优于强化学习（RL）：对长时程与延迟奖励更具鲁棒性，对不同基础模型适应性强，抗奖励作弊能力更强，训练更稳定，表明ES不仅是RL的可行替代方案，更是一种本质不同且强大的后训练范式。
LARAD: Layout-Aware Road Anomaly Detection via Spatial-Logic Reasoning
Authors: Shiyi Mu, Xujie Chen, Shugong Xu
First: 2026-07-14T15:09:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:09:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate open-world obstacle detection is critical for autonomous driving. Current anomaly segmentation methods suffer from a fundamental blind spot: they over-rely on texture novelty to identify out-of-distribution (OoD) objects while ignoring contextual spatial logic. Furthermore, mitigating the resulting false positives often requires cascading massive vision models, introducing unacceptable inference latency. To address these issues, we propose Layout-Aware Road Anomaly Detection (LARAD), shifting the paradigm from appearance matching to spatial-logic reasoning. First, we introduce the Spatial-Logic Violation Synthesis (SLVS) pipeline, which generates training samples that are texture-consistent yet spatially invalid, forcing the model to learn contextual violations. Second, we augment a standard closed-set segmentation network with a lightweight, OoD-guided attention branch. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LARAD significantly enhances robustness against logical anomalies and establishes a new state-of-the-art, all while retaining the high efficiency of a single-model architecture.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LARAD：通过空间逻辑推理实现布局感知的道路异常检测
准确的开放世界障碍物检测对自动驾驶至关重要。当前的异常分割方法存在根本性盲点：过度依赖纹理新颖性来识别分布外（OoD）物体，而忽视了上下文空间逻辑。此外，缓解由此产生的误报通常需要级联大量视觉模型，引入不可接受的推理延迟。为解决这些问题，我们提出布局感知道路异常检测（LARAD），将范式从外观匹配转向空间逻辑推理。首先，我们引入空间逻辑违反合成（SLVS）流程，生成纹理一致但空间无效的训练样本，迫使模型学习上下文违规。其次，我们在标准闭集分割网络基础上增加一个轻量级、OoD引导的注意力分支。大量实验表明，LARAD显著增强了对逻辑异常的鲁棒性，并建立了新的最先进水平，同时保持单模型架构的高效率。
