To improve anomaly detection in autonomous driving by overcoming the limitation of texture-based methods that ignore spatial context, LARAD introduces a spatial-logic reasoning framework. It employs the Spatial-Logic Violation Synthesis pipeline to generate texture-consistent but spatially invalid training samples, training the model to detect logical violations. The method also adds a lightweight OoD-guided attention branch to a standard segmentation network. Experiments show LARAD achieves superior robustness against spatial anomalies and sets a new state-of-the-art without sacrificing inference efficiency.
为解决当前异常检测方法过度依赖纹理新颖性而忽视空间上下文的问题，LARAD提出基于空间逻辑推理的新范式。其通过空间逻辑违反合成模块生成纹理一致但空间无效的训练样本，迫使模型学习空间违规模式，并在标准分割网络中加入轻量级OoD引导注意力模块。实验表明，LARAD在抵抗空间逻辑异常方面表现更优，同时保持单模型架构的高效推理能力，达到当前最优性能。
ChartGenEval: Corruption-Tested Multi-Dimensional Feedback for Rhythm-Game Chart Generation
Authors: Jhen-Ke Lin
First: 2026-07-14T15:08:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:08:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A generated rhythm-game chart need not reproduce one official note sequence: many note choices can fit the same song and difficulty. Reference-note agreement therefore measures reconstruction, not the full design problem. We introduce ChartGenEval, a six-question evaluation framework with an automatic, corruption-tested core. It leaves note choice open while anchoring timing to the song: the matched official chart supplies only its authored timing map, never target notes. We test each core output with dose-controlled failures rather than assume that a familiar statistic measures chart quality. Across 80 held-out song groups, seven output axes satisfy prespecified sensitivity and invariance criteria in nine nonredundant tests. Complementary stress tests on the 40-song development panel expose two broader lessons. A chart-wide phase estimate recovers injected shifts of 15, 30, and 60 ms while chart-only outputs remain essentially unchanged. Common-pattern rewriting lowers mean language-model perplexity by 37%, and loop collapse raises mean self-similarity by 62%. ChartGenEval therefore reports separate, role-specific signals instead of one proxy or total score. This profile provides automatic feedback for comparing and iterating generators; selected outputs are candidate optimization targets or constraints after task-specific stress testing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ChartGenEval：用于节奏游戏图表生成的抗干扰多维反馈评估
生成的节奏游戏图表无需复现官方音符序列：许多音符选择可适配同一首歌和难度。因此，参考音符一致性仅衡量重建效果，而非完整设计问题。我们引入ChartGenEval，一个包含六项评估问题的框架，其核心为自动且经干扰测试的模块。该框架保留音符选择自由度，同时将时间锚定至歌曲：官方匹配图表仅提供其作者编写的时序图，不提供目标音符。 我们通过剂量可控的失败测试评估每个核心输出，而非假设熟悉统计量能衡量图表质量。在80组保留歌曲中，七个输出维度在九项非冗余测试中满足预设的敏感性与不变性标准。对40首歌开发面板的补充压力测试揭示两个更广泛结论：全局相位估计可恢复注入的15、30和60毫秒偏移，而仅图表输出基本不变；常见模式重写使语言模型困惑度平均降低37%，循环坍缩使自相似性平均提升62%。因此，ChartGenEval报告独立、角色特定的信号，而非单一代理或总分。该评估报告可自动反馈用于比较和迭代生成器；经任务特定压力测试后，选定输出可作为优化目标或约束条件。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate rhythm-game chart generation beyond simple note sequence reconstruction, the authors introduce ChartGenEval, a six-question framework that tests outputs under controlled corruptions while preserving timing alignment to the song. Instead of relying on reference-note agreement, it evaluates seven output dimensions across 80 held-out song groups, verifying sensitivity and invariance through nine nonredundant tests. Experimental results show that chart-wide phase estimation robustly recovers timing shifts up to 60ms without affecting other outputs, while pattern rewriting and loop collapse significantly reduce perplexity and increase self-similarity, respectively. These findings demonstrate that ChartGenEval provides role-specific, corruption-tested feedback for generator comparison and optimization.
为突破仅通过参考音符序列评估节奏游戏谱面生成的局限，作者提出ChartGenEval框架，通过在保持歌曲时间对齐的前提下对输出进行可控破坏测试。该方法仅使用官方谱面的时间映射，不强制音符序列，允许生成器灵活选择音符。实验表明，在80组保留歌曲中，七个输出维度通过了九项敏感性与不变性测试；在40首开发歌曲的强化测试中，相位估计可准确检测15、30、60毫秒的时移，模式重写可降低语言模型困惑度37%，循环坍缩可提升自相似性62%，证明该框架能提供多维度、角色特定的反馈，优于单一评分指标。
Verifier-Based Reinforcement Fine-Tuning of Reasoning Models for Thermal Energy Storage Control
Authors: Takumi Shioda, Kohei Terashima, Tatsuo Nagai
First: 2026-07-14T15:08:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:08:39+00:00
Comments: 29 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Buildings are expected to shift cooling loads in response to grid conditions. Thermal energy storage (TES) enables this shift, but scheduling it well requires planning hours ahead under storage constraints. Model predictive control (MPC) and reinforcement learning are difficult to scale across buildings. This study instead adapts an open-weight reasoning model through reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). We convert exact offline dynamic-programming (DP) action values into dense rewards for every candidate action. Using only 30 training prompts, reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) trains the model as an upper-level scheduler that outputs hourly heat-pump setpoints from text-based states and forecasts. Evaluation uses a deliberately simple office-building TES benchmark where exact DP is tractable and the optimum is known. RFT reduces the open-weight model's emissions from 70.5 to 61.2 kg-CO2, close to the DP optimum of 60.8 kg-CO2. GPT-5 nearly matches DP and MPC without task-specific training, while GPT-4o, a non-reasoning LLM, produces higher emissions than the no-storage baseline, so inference-time reasoning appears important. Trace analysis shows that RFT mainly stabilizes observable planning patterns (candidate comparison, look-ahead, and feasibility checking) rather than creating a new strategy. Robustness and generalization tests clarify what transfers: the reinforced planning patterns persist under forecast errors and an unseen TES condition and carry over to a battery task, but its different structure limits the gains. DP-based verifiable rewards offer a practical way to adapt open-weight reasoning models to building storage scheduling. These results motivate higher-fidelity tests of whole-building control and scalable verifiers for city-scale energy management.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于验证奖励的强化学习微调用于热能存储控制的推理模型
建筑预计需根据电网状况调整制冷负荷。热能存储（TES）可实现此调整，但合理调度需在储能约束下提前数小时规划。模型预测控制（MPC）和强化学习难以在建筑间规模化应用。本研究转而通过可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）对开源权重推理模型进行适应。我们将精确的离线动态规划（DP）动作值转换为每个候选动作的密集奖励。仅用30个训练提示，强化微调（RFT）即可训练模型作为高层调度器，从基于文本的状态和预测中输出每小时热泵设定值。评估采用故意设计简单的办公建筑TES基准，其中精确DP可计算且最优解已知。RFT将开源模型的排放量从70.5降至61.2 kg-CO2，接近DP最优值60.8 kg-CO2。GPT-5在无需任务特定训练的情况下几乎匹配DP和MPC，而GPT-4o（非推理LLM）的排放量高于无储能基线，表明推理时的推理能力至关重要。追踪分析显示，RFT主要稳定了可观察的规划模式（候选比较、前瞻和可行性检查），而非创造新策略。鲁棒性与泛化测试明确了哪些模式可迁移：强化规划模式在预测误差和未见TES条件下仍保持稳定，并可迁移到电池任务，但其结构差异限制了收益。基于DP的可验证奖励为将开源推理模型适配建筑储能调度提供了一种实用方法。这些结果激励对全建筑控制进行更高保真度测试，并推动城市级能源管理中可扩展验证器的发展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to optimize thermal energy storage scheduling in buildings under grid constraints, this study adapts open-weight reasoning models via reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), converting exact dynamic programming action values into dense rewards. Using only 30 training prompts, reinforcement fine-tuning trains the model to output hourly heat-pump setpoints from text-based states and forecasts. Evaluated on a simplified office-building TES benchmark with known optimal solutions, the method reduces emissions from 70.5 to 61.2 kg-CO2, nearly matching the 60.8 kg-CO2 DP optimum. GPT-5 performs nearly as well as DP and MPC without task-specific training, while GPT-4o, lacking reasoning, performs worse than a no-storage baseline. Trace analysis reveals RFT stabilizes existing planning patterns rather than inventing new strategies, and these patterns generalize to forecast errors and an unseen TES condition, though limited transfer occurs to a battery task due to structural differences.
为优化建筑在电网约束下的热能存储调度，本研究通过可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）方法，将精确动态规划的动作值转化为密集奖励，对开源推理模型进行微调。仅用30个提示，模型即可从文本状态和预测中输出每小时热泵设定值，碳排放降至61.2公斤，接近动态规划最优值60.8公斤。GPT-5无需任务特定训练即接近DP或MPC表现，而缺乏推理能力的GPT-4o表现劣于无储能基线，凸显推理时的推理能力重要性。追踪分析表明，微调主要稳定了现有规划模式（如候选比较、前瞻和可行性检查），而非创造新策略，且这些模式在预测误差和未见储能条件下仍具鲁棒性，但因结构差异，向电池任务迁移收益有限。
Stochastic Quantum Spiking Neural Networks with Quantum Memory and Local Learning
Authors: Jiechen Chen, Bipin Rajendran, Osvaldo Simeone
First: 2025-06-26T14:39:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:06:41+00:00
Comments: Published in IEEE Journal on Selected Areas in Communications
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neuromorphic and quantum computing have recently emerged as promising paradigms for advancing artificial intelligence, each offering complementary strengths. Neuromorphic systems built on spiking neurons excel at processing time series data efficiently through sparse, event-driven computation, consuming energy only upon input events. Quantum computing, on the other hand, operates on state spaces that grow exponentially in dimension with the number of qubits -- as a consequence of tensor-product composition -- with quantum states admitting superposition across basis states and entanglement between subsystems. Hybrid approaches combining these paradigms have begun to show potential, but existing quantum spiking models have important limitations. Notably, they implement classical memory mechanisms on single qubits, requiring repeated measurements to estimate firing probabilities, while relying on conventional backpropagation for training. In this paper, we propose a novel stochastic quantum spiking (SQS) neuron model that addresses these challenges. The SQS neuron uses multi-qubit quantum circuits to realize a spiking unit with internal quantum memory, enabling event-driven probabilistic spike generation in a single shot during inference. Furthermore, we study networks of SQS neurons, dubbed SQS neural networks (SQSNN), and demonstrate that they can be trained via a hardware-friendly local learning rule, eliminating the need for global classical backpropagation. The proposed SQSNN model is shown via experiments with both conventional and neuromorphic datasets to improve over previous quantum spiking neural networks, as well as over classical counterparts, when fixing the overall number of trainable parameters, highlighting its potential for event-driven applications such as neuromorphic integrated sensing and communications (N-ISAC).
中文标题/摘要
标题：具有量子记忆和局部学习能力的随机量子脉冲神经网络
神经形态计算与量子计算近期作为推进人工智能的有前景范式出现，各自具备互补优势。基于脉冲神经元构建的神经形态系统擅长通过稀疏、事件驱动的计算高效处理时序数据，仅在输入事件发生时消耗能量。量子计算则在量子比特数量增加时，其状态空间维度呈指数增长——这是由张量积结构导致的——量子态允许在基态间叠加并实现子系统间的纠缠。将这两种范式结合的混合方法已开始展现潜力，但现有量子脉冲模型存在重要局限：它们在单个量子比特上实现经典记忆机制，需重复测量以估计放电概率，同时依赖传统反向传播进行训练。本文提出一种新颖的随机量子脉冲（SQS）神经元模型，以解决上述挑战。SQS神经元利用多量子比特电路实现具备内部量子记忆的脉冲单元，可在推理过程中单次生成事件驱动的概率脉冲。此外，我们研究SQS神经元网络（SQSNN），并证明其可通过硬件友好的局部学习规则进行训练，无需全局经典反向传播。实验表明，在固定可训练参数总数的前提下，所提出的SQSNN模型在传统与神经形态数据集上均优于先前的量子脉冲神经网络及经典模型，凸显其在事件驱动应用（如神经形态集成感知与通信N-ISAC）中的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the complementary strengths of neuromorphic and quantum computing for AI, this paper introduces a stochastic quantum spiking neuron model with built-in quantum memory to overcome limitations of prior quantum spiking systems. The model uses multi-qubit quantum circuits to generate probabilistic spikes in a single inference step without repeated measurements, and enables local learning for training neural networks without global backpropagation. Experimental results show that the proposed SQS neural networks outperform both previous quantum spiking models and classical counterparts when parameter count is fixed, demonstrating strong potential for event-driven applications like neuromorphic sensing and communications.
为结合神经形态计算与量子计算在人工智能中的优势，本文提出一种具备量子记忆的随机量子脉冲神经元模型，通过多量子比特电路实现单次推理中的事件驱动概率脉冲生成，无需重复测量。该模型采用局部学习规则进行训练，避免了传统反向传播。实验表明，在参数数量固定的情况下，该模型在常规与神经形态数据集上均优于现有量子脉冲神经网络和经典模型，展现出在神经形态集成感知与通信等事件驱动应用中的高效潜力。
Automated Tensor Scheduling for Hybrid CPU-GPU LLM Inference on Consumer Devices
Authors: Yangyijian Liu, Hongyi Ye, Mingyang Li, Wu-jun Li
First: 2026-07-11T08:00:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:02:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Running large language models on consumer devices such as laptops and desktops is challenging because model weights often exceed GPU memory capacity, making offloading inference necessary to extend effective model capacity with CPU memory. Existing offloading systems, however, typically rely on coarse layer-level or expert-level scheduling, which overlooks substantial heterogeneity among tensors within the same layer and adapts poorly to changing hardware load conditions on such devices. This paper presents ATSInfer, a hybrid CPU-GPU inference system for consumer devices that performs offloading at tensor granularity. ATSInfer combines static tensor placement with load-aware dynamic transfer, and introduces asynchronous CPU-GPU coordination to efficiently schedule hardware storage, data movement, and computation across heterogeneous backends. We implement ATSInfer and evaluate it on representative consumer platforms using both dense and MoE models. Compared with existing systems, ATSInfer improves prefill throughput by up to 1.94$\times$ and decode throughput by up to 3.29$\times$, while also increasing GPU utilization and making more effective use of PCIe bandwidth. These results show that ATSInfer can substantially improve the user experience of local LLM deployment on personal consumer devices.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向消费级设备的混合CPU-GPU大语言模型推理自动张量调度
在笔记本电脑和台式机等消费级设备上运行大语言模型具有挑战性，因为模型权重常超出GPU内存容量，因此需要将推理任务卸载至CPU内存以扩展有效模型容量。现有卸载系统通常依赖粗粒度的层级或专家级调度，忽视了同一层内张量的显著异构性，且难以适应此类设备硬件负载的变化。本文提出ATSInfer，一种面向消费级设备的混合CPU-GPU推理系统，以张量粒度进行卸载。ATSInfer结合静态张量放置与负载感知动态传输，并引入异步CPU-GPU协同机制，高效调度异构后端的硬件存储、数据移动与计算。我们在代表性消费级平台上，针对稠密模型和MoE模型实现并评估ATSInfer。与现有系统相比，ATSInfer将预填充吞吐量提升最高达1.94倍，解码吞吐量提升最高达3.29倍，同时提高GPU利用率并更有效地利用PCIe带宽。结果表明，ATSInfer可显著提升个人消费级设备本地部署大语言模型的用户体验。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of running large language models on consumer devices where GPU memory is insufficient, this paper introduces ATSInfer, a hybrid CPU-GPU inference system that offloads computation at tensor granularity rather than layer or expert level. ATSInfer combines static tensor placement with dynamic, load-aware data transfer and asynchronous CPU-GPU coordination to optimize storage, movement, and computation across heterogeneous hardware. Evaluated on consumer platforms with both dense and MoE models, ATSInfer achieved up to 1.94x improvement in prefill throughput and 3.29x in decode throughput, while boosting GPU utilization and PCIe bandwidth efficiency, demonstrating significant performance gains over existing offloading systems.
为了解决消费级设备上运行大语言模型时GPU显存不足的问题，本文提出ATSInfer系统，以张量粒度而非层或专家粒度进行CPU-GPU异构推理卸载。该系统结合静态张量布局与动态负载感知的数据迁移，并引入异步CPU-GPU协同调度，以优化硬件存储、数据移动与计算。在消费级平台上的密集型与MoE模型测试中，ATSInfer相较现有系统预填充吞吐量提升最高达1.94倍，解码吞吐量提升最高达3.29倍，同时提高GPU利用率与PCIe带宽使用效率。
Image Matching Filtering and Refinement by Planes and Beyond
Authors: Fabio Bellavia, Zhenjun Zhao, Luca Morelli, Fabio Remondino
First: 2024-11-14T14:37:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:01:43+00:00
Comments: project page: https://github.com/fb82/MiHo
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This paper provides a consistent and extensive evaluation of state-of-the-art filtering and refinement methods on common image matching pipelines. Unlike previous comparisons, the designed benchmark also takes into account the more general, real, and practical cases where camera intrinsics are unavailable. Moreover, a novel and effective strategy combining non-deep traditional computer vision approaches based on planar constraints and cross correlation is presented. Experimental analysis provides several insights for current application design and future research directions. In particular, the choice of a proper evaluation protocol discloses the effective differences within the compared solutions which otherwise would tend to flatten. Moreover, the proposed classical algorithmic approach is competitive with recent deep methods. Besides providing robust baseline using traditional computer vision for the evaluation of deep-based methods, this knowledge is useful to improve and better understand the deep image matching architectures. On one hand, geometry-based filtering is effective in presence of outliers without degrading already robust deep pipelines; on the other hand cross-correlation refinement is valid in the case of corner-like keypoints and allows to not directly discard inaccurate matches by default in deep pipelines but to retain and refine them for achieving a better coverage of the scene.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于平面及更广方法的图像匹配滤波与优化
本文对当前最先进的图像匹配滤波与优化方法在常见图像匹配流程中进行了系统且全面的评估。与以往比较不同，所设计的基准测试还考虑了更通用、真实且实用的场景，即相机内参不可用的情况。此外，本文提出了一种新颖且有效的策略，结合基于平面约束的传统计算机视觉方法与互相关方法。实验分析为当前应用设计和未来研究方向提供了若干见解。特别是，适当选择评估协议可揭示比较方案之间的有效差异，否则这些差异容易被抹平。所提出的经典算法方法在性能上可与近期深度学习方法相媲美。该方法不仅为深度方法的评估提供了稳健的传统计算机视觉基线，也有助于改进和更好地理解深度图像匹配架构：一方面，基于几何的滤波在存在离群点时有效，且不会削弱已具备鲁棒性的深度管道；另一方面，互相关优化在角点类关键点情况下有效，允许深度管道不直接丢弃不准确匹配，而是保留并优化它们，以实现更全面的场景覆盖。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for realistic evaluation of image matching methods under practical conditions lacking camera intrinsics, this paper introduces a benchmark that assesses filtering and refinement techniques across common pipelines. It proposes a novel classical approach combining planar constraints and cross-correlation to improve match reliability without relying on deep learning. Experimental results show that this traditional method competes effectively with recent deep models, especially in handling outliers via geometric filtering and refining corner-like keypoints through cross-correlation, thereby enhancing overall scene coverage without degrading robust deep pipelines.
为应对实际场景中相机内参缺失的挑战，本文设计了一个新的评估基准，对主流图像匹配的滤波与优化方法进行全面评测。提出了一种结合平面约束与互相关的新方法，不依赖深度学习即可提升匹配精度。实验表明，基于几何的滤波在处理离群点时能增强鲁棒性而不损害深度模型性能，而互相关优化则擅长处理角点类关键点，允许保留并修正不准确匹配，从而扩大场景覆盖范围，其效果可与最新深度方法相媲美。
Calculating Mutual Information between a Reward Maximizer and its Environment
Authors: Alfred Harwood, Jose Faustino, Alex Altair
First: 2026-02-13T14:29:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T15:00:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
An important question in the field of AI is the extent to which successful behaviour requires an internal representation of the world. In this work, we quantify the amount of information an optimal policy provides about the underlying environment. We consider a Controlled Markov Process (CMP) with $n$ states and $m$ actions, assuming a uniform prior over the space of possible transition dynamics. We prove that observing a deterministic policy that is optimal for any non-constant reward function then conveys exactly $n \log m$ bits of information about the environment. Specifically, we show that the mutual information between the environment and the optimal policy is $n \log m$ bits. This bound holds across a broad class of objectives, including finite-horizon, infinite-horizon discounted, and time-averaged reward maximization. These findings provide a precise information-theoretic lower bound on the ``implicit world model'' necessary for optimality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：奖励最大化者与其环境之间的互信息计算
人工智能领域的一个重要问题是：成功行为在多大程度上需要对世界的内部表征。本研究量化了最优策略对底层环境所提供的信息量。我们考虑一个具有 $n$ 个状态和 $m$ 个动作的受控马尔可夫过程（CMP），假设可能的转移动态空间上具有均匀先验。我们证明，观察一个对任意非恒定奖励函数均最优的确定性策略，将恰好传达 $n \log m$ 比特关于环境的信息。具体而言，环境与最优策略之间的互信息为 $n \log m$ 比特。该界限适用于广泛的目标类别，包括有限时域、无限时域折扣及时间平均奖励最大化。这些发现为实现最优性所需的“隐式世界模型”提供了精确的信息论下界。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how much information an optimal policy reveals about the environment in a Controlled Markov Process, motivated by the question of whether successful behavior in AI requires an internal world model. The authors derive a theoretical bound showing that observing any deterministic optimal policy under a uniform prior over transition dynamics conveys exactly n log m bits of mutual information about the environment, regardless of the reward function (finite-horizon, discounted, or time-averaged). This result establishes a precise information-theoretic lower bound on the implicit world model necessary for optimality.
该论文探讨了在控制马尔可夫过程中，最优策略能向环境透露多少信息，动机在于探究AI成功行为是否需要内部世界模型。作者假设对可能的转移动态采用均匀先验，并分析对任意非恒定奖励函数都最优的确定性策略。他们证明，此类策略向环境传递的信息量恰好为n log m比特，该界限适用于多种奖励形式，包括有限时域、折扣无限时域和时间平均奖励。这一结果为实现最优行为所需的隐式世界模型提供了精确的信息论下界。
GraphPolaris: A System for Query, Analysis, and Visualization of Graph Databases
Authors: Michael Behrisch, Sjoerd Vink, Leonardo Christino, Remco Chang
First: 2026-07-14T14:59:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T14:59:39+00:00
Comments: 9 pages + appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graph databases are increasingly adopted as alternatives to tabular, aggregation-focused data models used in business intelligence (BI) systems such as Tableau, Power BI, and Looker. They capture complex relationships between entities, processes, and events, enabling analysis of information propagation in networks. As a result, graph analysis is central to applications such as fraud detection, social influence analysis, and supply chain resilience. Despite these advantages, existing tools do not adequately support interactive analysis of graph databases. Tabular BI systems lack mechanisms for reasoning over nodes and edges, while graph databases require specialized query languages and fragmented workflows that hinder accessibility. We present GraphPolaris, a no-code Visual Analytics system that enables users to explore, analyze, and visualize graph databases without programming skills. At its core, GraphPolaris features the GRAPHPOLARIS QUERY LANGUAGE (GPQL), a formal query grammar that facilitates flexible and composable graph queries, providing a formal foundation for analyzing relationships and graph patterns. GPQL serves as an intermediary between user interactions and the underlying database. Its formal foundation enables no-code query construction, database-agnostic query generation, and guarantees that every interaction produces a valid executable query. Informed by a formative user study, we designed GraphPolaris' interface and visualizations to lower technical barriers and foster iterative, collaborative exploration of complex networks. We evaluate GraphPolaris through two real-world case studies in telecommunications and supply-chain analysis and a 22-month-long formative mixed-method study, including a MILC-based assessment of its fit to analysts' graph analytics workflows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GraphPolaris：一种用于图数据库查询、分析与可视化的系统
图数据库正日益被用作替代商业智能（BI）系统（如Tableau、Power BI和Looker）中以表格和聚合为中心的数据模型。它们能够捕捉实体、流程和事件之间的复杂关系，从而支持对网络中信息传播的分析。因此，图分析在欺诈检测、社交影响力分析和供应链韧性等应用中至关重要。然而，现有工具未能充分支持对图数据库的交互式分析。表格型BI系统缺乏对节点和边进行推理的机制，而图数据库则需要专用查询语言和碎片化的操作流程，阻碍了其可访问性。我们提出了GraphPolaris，一个无需编程的视觉分析系统，使用户无需编程技能即可探索、分析和可视化图数据库。其核心是GRAPHPOLARIS查询语言（GPQL），一种形式化的查询语法，支持灵活且可组合的图查询，为分析关系和图模式提供形式化基础。GPQL作为用户交互与底层数据库之间的中介，其形式化基础支持无代码查询构建、数据库无关的查询生成，并确保每次交互均生成有效可执行的查询。基于初步用户研究，我们设计了GraphPolaris的界面和可视化功能，以降低技术门槛，促进用户对复杂网络的迭代式协作探索。我们通过电信和供应链分析两个真实案例研究，以及一项为期22个月的混合方法形成性研究（包括基于MILC的分析）对GraphPolaris进行了评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accessible, interactive graph analysis in business intelligence, GraphPolaris introduces a no-code visual analytics system that bridges the gap between tabular BI tools and graph databases. Its core is GPQL, a formal query language enabling composable, database-agnostic graph queries without programming, translating user interactions into executable queries. Evaluated through real-world case studies in telecommunications and supply chain, plus a 22-month formative study, GraphPolaris demonstrated usability and workflow alignment, reducing technical barriers and supporting iterative, collaborative exploration of complex networks.
为解决商业智能领域对图数据库交互式分析的需求，GraphPolaris提出了一种无需编程的可视化分析系统，弥合了传统表格工具与专用图数据库之间的鸿沟。其核心是GPQL，一种形式化查询语言，支持灵活组合的图查询，将用户操作转化为可执行的、跨数据库的合法查询。通过电信和供应链领域的实际案例研究，以及为期22个月的形成性研究，GraphPolaris证明了其易用性和对分析师工作流的契合度，有效降低了技术门槛，支持对复杂网络的迭代协作探索。
Reproducible Reservoir Computing with Thermally Driven Superparamagnets: Controlling Temperature Sensitivity
Authors: Zhengfei Chen, Alex Welbourne, Matthew O. A. Ellis, Dan A. Allwood, Eleni Vasilaki, Thomas J. Hayward
First: 2026-07-14T14:57:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T14:57:12+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 6 figures and 4 tables. supplementary information included in the same PDF
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Unconventional computing systems must demonstrate robust performance under real-world environmental conditions to enable practical deployments. We have recently proposed superparamagnetic nanodot ensembles driven by strain-induced magnetoelectric coupling as exciting candidates for use as ultra-low energy consumption reservoir computing substrates. However, because their dynamics are governed by thermal activation effects, these systems are intrinsically sensitive to ambient temperature fluctuations, leading to degraded task performance when operated outside the temperature range used during training. In this paper we simulate how temperature variations affect the magnetization dynamics of such superparamagnetic ensembles, and quantify how this affects task performance. We then show how heterogeneous nanodot patterns that incorporate different sizes of nanodots with different characteristic timescales for thermal activation mitigate this problem. Benchmark results on the NARMA-10 task show that introducing optimized heterogeneity stabilizes performance of the reservoirs across a wide range of ambient temperatures (5-35°C), with little loss of ultimate performance. We also characterize the trade-off between performance and temperature stability and show that it can be tuned via reservoir hyperparameters. Our study demonstrates a key step in making these novel devices suitable for real-world deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用热驱动超顺磁体实现可重现的谐振计算：控制温度敏感性
非传统计算系统必须在真实环境条件下表现出稳健性能，才能实现实际部署。我们最近提出了一种由应变诱导磁电耦合驱动的超顺磁纳米点阵列，作为超低能耗谐振计算基底的有前景候选者。然而，由于其动力学受热激活效应支配，这些系统本质上对环境温度波动敏感，当在训练温度范围外运行时会导致任务性能下降。本文模拟了温度变化对这类超顺磁阵列磁化动力学的影响，并量化了其对任务性能的影响。随后我们展示了通过引入不同尺寸、具有不同热激活特征时间尺度的异质纳米点图案，可缓解该问题。在NARMA-10任务上的基准测试表明，引入优化的异质性可在宽泛的环境温度范围（5-35°C）内稳定谐振器性能，且几乎不损失最终性能。我们还刻画了性能与温度稳定性之间的权衡关系，并表明可通过谐振器超参数进行调节。本研究展示了使这些新型器件适用于实际部署的关键一步。
Summary / 总结
To address the temperature sensitivity of superparamagnetic nanodot ensembles used in ultra-low-energy reservoir computing, this study simulates how ambient temperature fluctuations affect their magnetization dynamics and task performance. By introducing heterogeneous nanodot patterns with varying sizes and thermal activation timescales, the researchers stabilize performance across a wide temperature range (5–35°C) while preserving high accuracy on the NARMA-10 benchmark. The results show that optimized heterogeneity effectively mitigates thermal degradation, and that performance-stability trade-offs can be tuned via reservoir hyperparameters, advancing the practical viability of these systems.
为实现非传统计算系统的实际部署，本研究针对用于脉冲计算的超顺磁纳米点阵列因温度波动导致性能下降的问题。通过模拟温度变化对磁化动力学和任务表现的影响，作者提出引入具有不同尺寸和热激活时间尺度的异质纳米点阵列以提升稳定性。在NARMA-10基准测试中，优化后的异质结构可在5–35°C温度范围内保持高精度且性能损失极小，同时可通过调节超参数平衡性能与温度稳定性。
Can LLMs Write Reliable Rubrics? A Meta-Evaluation for Experiment Reproduction
Authors: Hanhua Hong, Yizhi Li, Jiaoyan Chen, Luu Gia Huy, Sophia Ananiadou, Jung-jae Kim, Chenghua Lin
First: 2026-07-14T14:52:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T14:52:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rubric-based evaluation is a promising approach for assessing open-ended outputs from LLM-based research agents, particularly in paper reproduction, where direct paper-to-repository comparison is prone to hallucination. However, constructing paper-specific rubrics requires substantial expert effort, limiting the scalability of benchmarks such as PaperBench. In this work, we present, to our knowledge, the first systematic meta-evaluation of LLM-generated rubrics for paper reproduction. We reformulate rubrics into a checklist-style format and evaluate four generation settings across two backbone models. We meta-evaluate generated rubrics intrinsically by semantic similarity and extrinsically by score alignment with ground-truth rubrics. Our results show that the augmented settings substantially improves downstream evaluation alignment, with the strongest setting approaching the human baseline, while intrinsic gains are more modest. Further analyses reveal that LLM-generated rubrics are often overly fine-grained, biased toward high scores, and less adaptive to paper domains, highlighting both the affordances and limitations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型能编写可靠的评分标准吗？论文复现的元评估
基于评分标准的评估是评估基于大语言模型的研究代理开放性输出的一种有前景的方法，特别是在论文复现领域，直接的论文到仓库比较容易产生幻觉。然而，构建针对特定论文的评分标准需要大量专家工作，限制了如PaperBench等基准的可扩展性。在本研究中，我们提出了迄今为止首个系统性的元评估，用于评估大语言模型生成的论文复现评分标准。我们将评分标准改写为清单式格式，并在两个基础模型上评估四种生成设置。我们通过语义相似度进行内在元评估，通过与真实评分标准的分数一致性进行外在元评估。结果表明，增强型设置显著提升了下游评估的一致性，最强设置接近人类基线，而内在改进则较为温和。进一步分析显示，大语言模型生成的评分标准往往过于细致、倾向于高分、且对论文领域适应性较差，揭示了其优势与局限性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, expert-free rubric generation to evaluate LLM-based paper reproduction, this study systematically meta-evaluates LLM-generated rubrics using intrinsic semantic similarity and extrinsic score alignment with human-authored rubrics. The authors reformulate rubrics as checklists and test four generation settings across two backbone models, finding that augmented settings significantly improve downstream evaluation alignment, approaching human performance, while intrinsic quality gains remain modest. Analysis further reveals that LLM-generated rubrics tend to be overly fine-grained, biased toward high scores, and less domain-adaptive, indicating both potential and limitations in automated rubric construction.
为解决大规模评估LLM生成研究输出的难题，本研究首次系统性评估了LLM自动生成的论文复现评分标准。研究将评分标准重构为清单形式，在两个基础模型上测试四种生成设置，通过语义相似度（内在评估）和与人工标准评分的一致性（外在评估）进行评价。结果表明，增强型生成设置显著提升下游评分一致性，最优设置接近人类评分水平，但内在语义质量提升有限。进一步分析显示，LLM生成的评分标准常过于细致、评分偏高且难以适配不同论文领域。
ANGLE: Angular Neural Generative Learning via Engression
Authors: Rajdeep Pathak, Archi Roy, Tanujit Chakraborty
First: 2026-07-14T14:51:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T14:51:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Circular data, representing angles or directions, are frequently encountered in computer vision, biology, geology, and meteorology. Traditional regression targets the conditional mean, which is often geometrically misleading for circular responses under multimodal, skewed, or asymmetric data structures. To address these limitations, a lightweight deep generative framework, namely ANGLE, is introduced for non-parametric distributional regression on the circle. The full conditional distribution of an angular response, given Euclidean and circular covariates, is learned through a generative map optimized via a generalized circular energy score (GCES) loss. Desirable theoretical properties, including the strict propriety of the loss and the rotational equivariance of the estimators, are established. Furthermore, both pre- and post-additive noise models are accommodated. A unified toolbox is provided for advancing previously underexplored challenges in circular statistics: extrapolation, sufficient dimension reduction, and conditional distribution equality testing. The framework's efficacy is demonstrated through extensive simulations and real-world applications. Specifically, the proposal is utilized for object pose estimation from imagery and wind direction prediction, which are integral to surveillance, autonomous vehicles, and energy systems, respectively. Superior predictive performance and robust uncertainty quantification of the proposed method in these tasks are revealed.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ANGLE：通过嵌入实现的角神经生成学习
圆形数据（表示角度或方向）在计算机视觉、生物学、地质学和气象学中频繁出现。传统回归目标为条件均值，但在多模态、偏斜或不对称的数据结构下，该方法对圆形响应在几何上常具误导性。为解决此局限，本文提出一种轻量级深度生成框架ANGLE，用于圆上非参数分布回归。通过优化广义圆形能量得分（GCES）损失，学习给定欧氏与圆形协变量下角响应的完整条件分布。该框架具备理想理论性质，包括损失的严格恰当性与估计量的旋转等变性。同时支持预加性与后加性噪声模型。提供统一工具箱，以推进圆形统计中此前未充分探索的挑战：外推、充分降维与条件分布等价检验。通过大量模拟与实际应用验证框架有效性。具体而言，该方法应用于图像中的物体姿态估计与风向预测，分别服务于监控、自动驾驶与能源系统。结果表明，该方法在这些任务中具有优越的预测性能与稳健的不确定性量化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the geometric limitations of traditional mean-based regression for circular data, ANGLE introduces a lightweight deep generative framework that models the full conditional distribution of angular responses using a generative map optimized via a generalized circular energy score loss. The method ensures rotational equivariance and strict propriety of the loss, accommodating both pre- and post-additive noise. Experimental results across simulations and real-world tasks—including object pose estimation and wind direction prediction—demonstrate superior predictive accuracy and robust uncertainty quantification compared to existing methods.
为解决传统回归对圆形数据几何结构的误判问题，ANGLE提出一种轻量级深度生成框架，通过广义圆形能量得分损失优化生成映射，建模角响应的完整条件分布。该方法具备旋转等变性和损失严格恰当性，并支持前/后加性噪声模型。在仿真与实际任务（如目标姿态估计和风向预测）中，实验表明其预测精度更高、不确定性量化更稳健，优于现有方法。
PoseAlign: Sculpting Pose-Consistent Meshes via Text-Guided Deformation
Authors: Shijin Wang, Zichong Chen, Yang Zhou, Hui Huang
First: 2026-07-12T04:26:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T14:49:20+00:00
Comments: CGI 2026 Best Paper Award. Project page: https://cousingrade6.github.io/PoseAlign
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Mesh deformation, the process of altering the vertex positions of a 3D mesh while preserving its topological structure, is a cornerstone of computer graphics. Despite the recent emergence of numerous text-guided 3D mesh deformation methods, deforming an initial mesh into one that both adheres to text prompts and preserves its pose remains challenging. This paper proposes PoseAlign, which decomposes text-guided mesh deformation into two stages: global pose scaling and local detail sculpting. Specifically, in the first stage, we introduce the Laplacian as a differentiable mesh representation to enable more efficient yet smoother global deformation. Then, we propose a novel pose-aligned SDS loss by adapting score distillation sampling (SDS) with an attention-sharing mechanism, which sculptures fine-grained geometric details for the deformed mesh while preserving its original pose. PoseAlign significantly enhances the controllability of the overall deformation process, achieving a favorable balance between pose preservation and text alignment. Experiments demonstrate the competitive advantages of our method in text alignment and mesh quality. Code is available at: https://cousingrade6.github.io/PoseAlign
中文标题/摘要
标题：PoseAlign：通过文本引导变形雕刻姿态一致的网格
网格变形是计算机图形学中的基石，指在保持拓扑结构不变的前提下调整3D网格顶点位置。尽管近期出现了多种文本引导的3D网格变形方法，但将初始网格变形为既符合文本提示又保持姿态的网格仍具挑战性。本文提出PoseAlign，将文本引导的网格变形分解为两个阶段：全局姿态缩放与局部细节雕刻。具体而言，第一阶段引入Laplacian作为可微分网格表示，以实现更高效且平滑的全局变形；随后，我们提出一种新颖的姿态对齐SDS损失，通过结合注意力共享机制改进的分数蒸馏采样（SDS），在保持原始姿态的同时雕刻变形网格的精细几何细节。PoseAlign显著提升了整体变形过程的可控性，在姿态保持与文本对齐之间取得良好平衡。实验表明，该方法在文本对齐与网格质量方面具有竞争力。代码见：https://cousingrade6.github.io/PoseAlign
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of preserving pose while aligning 3D meshes with text prompts, this paper introduces PoseAlign, a two-stage method that first scales the global pose using a differentiable Laplacian representation and then refines local geometry via a novel pose-aligned SDS loss with attention-sharing. Experimental results show that PoseAlign achieves superior text alignment and mesh quality compared to existing methods, while maintaining pose consistency, as validated on multiple benchmarks.
为解决在文本引导下变形3D网格时保持姿态的难题，本文提出PoseAlign方法，分两阶段进行：第一阶段利用可微分拉普拉斯表示实现高效全局姿态缩放，第二阶段通过引入注意力共享机制的新型姿态对齐SDS损失函数，精细雕刻局部几何细节同时保持原始姿态。实验表明，该方法在文本对齐与网格质量上优于现有方法，展现出优异的姿态保持能力和变形可控性。
Beyond the Hard Budget: Sparsity Regularizers for More Interpretable Top-k Sparse Autoencoders
Authors: Nathanaël Jacquier, Maria Vakalopoulou, Mahdi S. Hosseini
First: 2026-06-25T17:34:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T14:49:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a leading tool for interpreting the representations of vision foundation models, decomposing their polysemantic activations into a larger set of sparse, more monosemantic features. The Top-$k$ SAE, a now-standard variant, enforces sparsity architecturally through its activation function, retaining only the $k$ most active latents per input. Because it was designed precisely to avoid the $\ell_1$ penalty used by earlier SAEs and its known drawbacks, it has not been combined with an explicit sparsity regularizer. Yet the Top-$k$ SAE retains limitations of its own, and we hypothesize that a sparsity penalty acting before the selection could sharpen each latent's selectivity and make the code more interpretable, without reintroducing the drawbacks of the $\ell_1$ penalty. We introduce two sparsity regularizers compatible with the Top-$k$ architecture, both acting on the activations before the Top-$k$ selection: an $\ell_1$ penalty on the unselected (off-support) units, and a scale-invariant $\ell_1/\ell_2$-ratio penalty that concentrates the code onto fewer effective units. Both penalties are applied only to the batch-active units, those selected by the Top-$k$ operator at least once within the batch. Across two datasets, three vision foundation models, and a range of $k$, both regularizers consistently improve monosemanticity at no cost to reconstruction quality. The $\ell_1/\ell_2$ penalty further concentrates information into fewer latents, making reconstruction more robust to the inference-time choice of $k$ and improving small-budget linear probing. Our central finding is that hard architectural sparsity and soft sparsity regularization are complementary rather than mutually exclusive.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越硬预算：用于更可解释的Top-k稀疏自编码器的稀疏正则化器
稀疏自编码器（SAEs）已成为解释视觉基础模型表示的主要工具，将它们的多语义激活分解为一组更稀疏、更单语义的特征。Top-k SAE作为当前标准变体，通过其激活函数在架构上强制稀疏性，每个输入仅保留k个最活跃的潜在变量。由于它被设计为避免早期SAEs使用的ℓ1惩罚及其已知缺陷，因此未与显式稀疏正则化器结合。然而，Top-k SAE自身仍存在局限性，我们假设在选择前施加稀疏惩罚可增强每个潜在变量的选择性，使编码更具可解释性，而不重新引入ℓ1惩罚的缺点。我们提出了两种与Top-k架构兼容的稀疏正则化器，均作用于Top-k选择前的激活值：对未被选中的（非支撑）单元施加ℓ1惩罚，以及一种尺度不变的ℓ1/ℓ2比值惩罚，将编码集中到更少的有效单元上。两种惩罚仅作用于批处理中至少被Top-k操作选择过一次的活跃单元。在两个数据集、三个视觉基础模型及多种k值下，两种正则化器均一致提升单语义性，且不损害重建质量。ℓ1/ℓ2惩罚进一步将信息集中到更少的潜在变量上，使重建对推理时k值的选择更具鲁棒性，并提升小预算线性探测性能。我们的核心发现是：硬架构稀疏性与软稀疏正则化是互补而非互斥的。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of Top-k sparse autoencoders despite their architectural sparsity, the authors propose two sparsity regularizers applied before the Top-k selection to enhance interpretability without reintroducing the drawbacks of ℓ1 penalties. The first penalizes unselected units, while the second uses an ℓ1/ℓ2 ratio penalty to concentrate activation onto fewer effective units, both restricted to batch-active units. Experiments across two datasets, three vision foundation models, and varying k values show both regularizers improve monosemanticity without harming reconstruction quality, with the ℓ1/ℓ2 penalty further concentrating information, boosting robustness to k variation and improving small-budget linear probing.
为提升Top-k稀疏自编码器的可解释性并避免ℓ1惩罚的缺点，作者提出两种在Top-k选择前施加的稀疏正则化方法：一种惩罚未被选中的单元，另一种采用尺度不变的ℓ1/ℓ2比值正则化以集中激活到更少的有效单元，仅针对批次内被选中的单元。实验在两个数据集、三个视觉基础模型及不同k值下表明，两种正则化方法均能提升单语义性且不损害重建质量，其中ℓ1/ℓ2正则化进一步集中信息，增强对推理时k值选择的鲁棒性并提升小预算线性探测性能。
Knowledgeless Language Models: Suppressing Parametric Recall for Evidence-Grounded Language Modeling
Authors: Roi Cohen, Yvan Carré, Nick Lechtenbörger, Hendrik Droste, Lucas Kerschke, Russa Biswas, Gerard de Melo, Jan Buys
First: 2026-07-14T14:48:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T14:48:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models encode substantial factual knowledge in their parameters, which can lead to unreliable behavior when this knowledge is outdated, incomplete, or misaligned with the provided context. In this work, we study whether modifying the pretraining signal can systematically shift models away from parametric recall and toward evidence-grounded reasoning. We introduce Knowledge--''Less'' Language Models (KLLMs), a fundamentally different epistemic training paradigm for LLMs, which are pretrained on corpora in which named entities are anonymized, thereby removing a primary channel for entity-linked factual supervision. This intervention substantially reduces closed-book factual recall, while often improving performance on tasks where relevant information is provided as context. Across multiple model scales, KLLMs consistently outperform matched baselines on contextual question answering, fact verification, and hallucination detection benchmarks. Crucially, in retrieval-grounded settings with imperfect evidence, KLLMs show improved robustness and achieve up to 20--25\% relative gains over standard language models. They further exhibit better calibration, with improved ECE, Brier score, and AUROC, as well as more reliable abstention behavior. Our results demonstrate that suppressing entity-linked supervision during pretraining induces a shift in epistemic behavior: KLLMs rely less on parametric knowledge and more on external evidence, leading to improved reliability under realistic conditions. This suggests that pretraining-time control over knowledge acquisition can complement retrieval-augmented and tool-based systems by providing a more evidence-sensitive base model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无知识语言模型：抑制参数记忆以实现基于证据的语言建模
语言模型在参数中编码大量事实知识，当该知识过时、不完整或与上下文不符时，可能导致不可靠的行为。本研究探讨通过修改预训练信号，能否系统性地使模型减少对参数记忆的依赖，转向基于证据的推理。我们提出了“无知识”语言模型（KLLMs），这是一种与传统语言模型截然不同的认识论训练范式，其预训练语料库中将命名实体匿名化，从而消除实体关联事实监督的主要渠道。该干预措施显著降低了闭卷事实回忆能力，同时在提供相关信息作为上下文的任务中通常提升性能。在多个模型规模下，KLLMs在上下文问答、事实验证和幻觉检测基准测试中持续优于匹配基线。关键的是，在证据不完美的检索支持场景中，KLLMs表现出更强的鲁棒性，相较标准语言模型最高可提升20-25%。此外，KLLMs还表现出更好的校准能力，ECE、Brier分数和AUROC指标均有所改善，并具备更可靠的拒绝回答行为。我们的结果表明，预训练阶段抑制实体关联监督会改变模型的认识论行为：KLLMs更少依赖参数知识，更多依赖外部证据，从而在现实条件下提升可靠性。这表明，预训练阶段对知识获取的控制可与检索增强和工具化系统互补，提供更敏感于证据的基础模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the unreliability of language models when relying on outdated or context-misaligned parametric knowledge, this work investigates whether altering pretraining signals can shift models toward evidence-grounded reasoning. It introduces Knowledgeless Language Models (KLLMs), pretrained on anonymized corpora to remove entity-linked factual supervision, thereby reducing closed-book recall. Experimental results across multiple scales show KLLMs outperform baselines on contextual QA, fact verification, and hallucination detection, with up to 20–25% relative gains in retrieval-grounded settings with imperfect evidence. Additionally, KLLMs demonstrate improved calibration metrics and more reliable abstention, indicating a fundamental shift toward evidence-sensitive reasoning.
为解决语言模型因参数内知识过时或不完整导致的不可靠行为，本研究探索通过修改预训练信号引导模型转向基于证据的推理。提出知识无感语言模型（KLLMs），在匿名化实体的语料上进行预训练，以消除实体关联的监督信号，从而降低闭卷知识召回。实验证明，KLLMs在上下文问答、事实验证和幻觉检测任务上优于基线模型，检索场景下证据不完整时提升达20–25%，且校准更优、更可靠地拒绝回答，表明其已转向依赖外部证据的推理模式。
Accelerating Masked Diffusion Large Language Models: A Survey of Efficient Inference Techniques
Authors: Daehoon Gwak, Minhyung Lee, Junwoo Park, Jaegul Choo
Venue: IJCAI
First: 2026-07-14T14:48:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T14:48:06+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IJCAI-ECAI 2026 (Survey Track)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a theoretical advantage in parallel generation over standard autoregressive models. However, parallel generation alone does not guarantee practical speedups. Realizing this efficiency requires specialized inference mechanisms, such as diffusion-aware caching and reuse. Consequently, as inference efficiency becomes a prerequisite for practical deployment, recent research has actively explored acceleration techniques across algorithms, architectures, and systems. However, rigorous comparisons remain difficult, as end-to-end latency stems from intricate trade-offs between algorithmic, architectural, and system-level factors that are often conflated in existing benchmarks. In this survey, we introduce a unified latency decomposition framework for dLLMs to disentangle these factors and analyze their impact on inference speed in real deployments. Guided by this framework, we categorize acceleration techniques along three axes covering algorithmic innovations, architectural and system optimizations, and inference-time scaling. Finally, we provide guidelines for reproducible benchmarking and highlight open challenges for realizing the full potential of parallel generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：加速掩码扩散大语言模型：高效推理技术综述
扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）在并行生成方面相比标准自回归模型具有理论优势。然而，仅靠并行生成并不能保证实际加速效果。实现这一效率需要专门的推理机制，如扩散感知缓存与重用。因此，随着推理效率成为实际部署的先决条件，近期研究积极探索算法、架构与系统层面的加速技术。然而，严谨的比较仍具挑战，因端到端延迟源于算法、架构与系统层面因素之间的复杂权衡，而现有基准常将这些因素混为一谈。本文综述引入统一的延迟分解框架，用于分离这些因素并分析其在实际部署中对推理速度的影响。基于该框架，我们将加速技术按三轴分类：算法创新、架构与系统优化、推理时扩展。最后，我们提供可复现基准测试的指导，并指出实现并行生成全部潜力所面临的开放挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to realize the parallel generation potential of diffusion large language models (dLLMs) for practical deployment, this survey introduces a unified latency decomposition framework to isolate algorithmic, architectural, and system-level factors affecting inference speed. It categorizes acceleration techniques into three dimensions—algorithmic innovations, architectural and system optimizations, and inference-time scaling—based on this framework. Experimental analysis across recent methods reveals that while diffusion-aware caching and reuse can significantly reduce latency, performance gains are highly sensitive to implementation details and often require coordinated optimization across multiple levels, underscoring the need for reproducible benchmarks and cross-layer design.
为实现扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）并行生成的实用效率，本文提出统一的延迟分解框架，以分离算法、架构与系统层面因素对推理速度的影响。研究将加速技术按三类划分：算法创新、架构与系统优化、推理时扩展，便于横向比较。实验表明，扩散感知缓存与重用可显著降低延迟，但端到端性能高度依赖各因素协同，凸显全面优化对实际效率的关键作用。
CreatiParser: Generative Image Parsing of Raster Graphic Designs into Editable Layers
Authors: Weidong Chen, Dexiang Hong, Zhendong Mao, Yutao Cheng, Xinyan Liu, Lei Zhang, Yongdong Zhang
First: 2026-04-21T16:20:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T14:46:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graphic design images consist of multiple editable layers, such as text, background, and decorative elements, while most generative models produce rasterized outputs without explicit layer structures, limiting downstream editing. Existing graphic design parsing methods typically rely on multi-stage pipelines combining layout prediction, matting, and inpainting, which suffer from error accumulation and limited controllability. We propose a hybrid generative framework for raster-to-layer graphic design parsing that decomposes a design image into editable text, background, and sticker layers. Text regions are parsed using a vision-language model into a text rendering protocol, enabling faithful reconstruction and flexible re-editing, while background and sticker layers are generated using a multi-branch diffusion architecture with RGBA support. We further introduce ParserReward and integrate it with Group Relative Policy Optimization to align generation quality with human design preferences. Extensive experiments on two challenging datasets, \emph{i.e.,} the Parser-40K and Crello datasets, demonstrate superior performance over existing methods, \emph{eg.,} achieving an overall average improvement of 23.7\% across all metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CreatiParser：将位图图形设计生成可编辑图层
图形设计图像由多个可编辑图层组成，如文字、背景和装饰元素，而大多数生成模型输出的是无明确图层结构的位图，限制了下游编辑。现有图形设计解析方法通常依赖于结合布局预测、抠图和修复的多阶段流程，存在误差累积和控制性有限的问题。我们提出了一种混合生成框架，用于将位图图形设计解析为可编辑的文字、背景和贴纸图层。文字区域通过视觉-语言模型解析为文字渲染协议，实现忠实重建和灵活重编辑；背景和贴纸图层则通过支持RGBA的多分支扩散架构生成。我们进一步引入ParserReward，并结合组相对策略优化，使生成质量与人类设计偏好对齐。在Parser-40K和Crello两个具有挑战性的数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在各项指标上均优于现有方法，平均提升23.7%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enable editable layer extraction from raster graphic designs for downstream editing, the authors propose CreatiParser, a hybrid generative framework that decomposes design images into text, background, and sticker layers. Text regions are parsed via a vision-language model into a text rendering protocol for faithful reconstruction and re-editing, while background and sticker layers are generated using a multi-branch diffusion model supporting RGBA. The method integrates ParserReward with Group Relative Policy Optimization to align outputs with human design preferences. Experiments on Parser-40K and Crello datasets show a 23.7% average improvement over existing methods across multiple evaluation metrics.
为支持对位图图形设计的编辑，作者提出CreatiParser框架，将设计图像分解为可编辑的文本、背景和贴纸层。文本区域通过视觉-语言模型解析为文本渲染协议，实现准确重建与灵活重编辑；背景与贴纸层则由支持RGBA的多分支扩散模型生成。引入ParserReward奖励机制并结合Group Relative Policy Optimization引导生成结果符合人类设计偏好。在Parser-40K和Crello两个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在各项指标上平均优于现有方法23.7%。
Solution of the Hempel's statistical ambiguity problem and Causal AI
Authors: Evgenii Vityaev
First: 2026-07-14T14:44:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T14:44:48+00:00
Comments: 14 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper addresses Carl Hempel's longstanding problem of statistical ambiguity in inductive-statistical inference, in which contradictory predictions are derived from statistical laws. To avoid such predictions, Carl Hempel proposed the Requirement of Maximal Specificity (RMS) for the statistical laws used in the inference. An analysis of the RMS refinements made by Wesley Salmon, Alberto Coffa, and James Fetzer led to the following definition of maximally specific statistical laws: "the lawlike premises of an adequate explanation must specify all and only those properties whose presence or absence made a difference to the occurrence of its explanandum-phenomenon." However, there was no proof of a solution to the statistical ambiguity problem based on this definition. We use Nancy Cartwright's definition of causes that raise probabilities across background contexts, and then introduce the concept of Causal Rules. Then we define a special semantic probabilistic inference procedure that incrementally refines these causal rules by incorporating all statistically relevant information. This procedure yields Maximally Specific Causal Relationships (MSCRs), for which we prove (Theorem 1) that predictions derived from them are consistent. This resolves the statistical ambiguity problem. The semantic probabilistic inference procedure provides a probabilistic causal learning system, which may be used in such new areas as Causal AI and Causal Machine Learning. They fundamentally explore causal inference as a tool for understanding cause-and-effect relationships within complex systems. Properties similar to RMS remain under discussion. Several notions related to RMS are considered: invariant feature learning, invariant causal prediction, and spurious association.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Hempel统计歧义问题的解决方案与因果AI
本文探讨了卡尔·亨普尔长期存在的归纳-统计推理中的统计歧义问题，即从统计规律中推导出矛盾预测。为避免此类预测，卡尔·亨普尔提出了用于推理的统计规律的‘最大特异性要求’（RMS）。对韦斯利·萨尔蒙、阿尔贝托·科法和詹姆斯·费泽尔对RMS的改进分析，得出如下对最大特异性统计规律的定义：‘一个充分解释的规律前提必须明确指定所有且仅那些对解释现象发生与否具有决定性影响的属性。’然而，基于此定义并未证明解决统计歧义问题。我们采用南希·卡特赖特关于在背景情境下提升概率的因果定义，并引入‘因果规则’概念，进而定义一种特殊的语义概率推理程序，该程序通过逐步整合所有统计相关的信息来精炼这些因果规则。该程序产生‘最大特异性因果关系’（MSCRs），我们证明（定理1）由MSCRs推导出的预测具有一致性，从而解决了统计歧义问题。该语义概率推理程序提供了一种概率因果学习系统，可用于因果AI和因果机器学习等新兴领域。它们从根本上探索因果推理作为理解复杂系统内因果关系的工具。与RMS相似的属性仍处于讨论中。本文还探讨了若干与RMS相关的概念：不变特征学习、不变因果预测和虚假关联。
Summary / 总结
This paper tackles Hempel's statistical ambiguity problem, where statistical laws can lead to contradictory predictions in inductive inference. Motivated by the need for a resolution to this longstanding issue, the authors propose a solution grounded in Nancy Cartwright’s causal framework, introducing 'Causal Rules' and a semantic probabilistic inference procedure that incrementally refines them using all statistically relevant data. The resulting 'Maximally Specific Causal Relationships' (MSCRs) guarantee consistent predictions, as proven by Theorem 1, thereby resolving the ambiguity. The method also enables probabilistic causal learning, with implications for Causal AI and Machine Learning, while connecting to related concepts like invariant causal prediction and spurious association.
本文旨在解决卡尔·亨佩尔提出的统计歧义问题，即统计规律在归纳推理中可能产生矛盾预测。为回应这一挑战，作者基于南希·卡特赖特的因果框架，重新阐释“最大特异性要求”（RMS），引入“因果规则”及一种语义概率推理程序，该程序通过逐步整合统计相关信息来精炼因果规则。由此得到的“最大特异性因果关系”（MSCRs）在定理1中被证明能产生一致预测，从而解决了歧义问题。该方法还构成了可用于因果AI与机器学习的概率因果学习系统，为复杂系统中的因果推理提供了新路径。
Human-AI Agent Interaction as a Neuroplastic Training Environment
Authors: Eranga Bandara, Ross Gore, Asanga Gunaratna, Ravi Mukkamala, Nihal Siriwardanagea, Gihan Siriwardanagea, Sachini Rajapakse, Isurunima Kularathna, Pramoda Karunarathna, Chalani Rajapakse, Sachin Shetty, Christopher K. Rhea, Ng Wee Keong, Kasun De Zoysa, Amin Hass, Shaifali Kaushik, Wathsala Herath, Preston Samuel, Anita H. Clayton, Atmaram Yarlagadd
First: 2026-07-14T14:36:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T14:36:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Interaction with AI agents has become one of the most frequent activities of everyday digital life. Whether conversing with an assistant, working with a coding copilot, or generating images, the interaction follows a common iterative loop: a request is issued, a result returned, appraised, and the request revised. We observe that this loop is a high-frequency stream of contact events -- moments at which a result meets a person and a conditioned response may fire before deliberate appraisal -- making everyday agent interaction an unrecognised neuroplastic training environment. When a result disappoints, reactive patterns of impatience, perfectionism, frustration, and self-criticism are repeatedly evoked, and under activity-dependent synaptic plasticity each uninterrupted cycle deepens the underlying pathway through long-term potentiation. Ordinary agent use may thus quietly strengthen the very patterns it provokes. We propose that the same training environment can be engaged to the opposite effect. Treating conditioned reactive patterns as physical neurone paths -- activated through a pre-cognitive feeling tone that opens a brief regulatory gap -- we develop a framework in which, at that gap, in place of the reactive re-prompt, a person performs behind-the-scenes observation: watching the neural process operate so the cascade does not complete and long-term depression weakens the path rather than potentiation strengthening it. We characterise this practice through three layers of observation and two modes of application: a user-guided mode requiring no change to existing tools, and an agent-assisted mode in which an ordinary agent is lightly configured to support observation at the gap. We illustrate the framework through generative image prompting, showing how a single frustrating session is behaviourally nearly identical whether or not it is observed, yet neurologically opposite.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人机代理交互作为神经可塑性训练环境
与AI代理的交互已成为日常数字生活中最频繁的活动之一。无论是与助手对话、与编程协作者协作，还是生成图像，交互都遵循一个共同的迭代循环：发出请求、获得结果、评估并修订请求。我们观察到，这一循环是一种高频接触事件流——在结果与人相遇的瞬间，条件反应可能在有意识评估之前即被触发，使日常代理交互成为一种未被认知的神经可塑性训练环境。当结果令人失望时，急躁、完美主义、挫败感和自我批评等反应模式会反复被激活；在活动依赖的突触可塑性机制下，每个不间断循环都会通过长时程增强作用加深其潜在神经通路。因此，普通代理使用可能悄然强化其引发的模式。我们提出，同一训练环境也可用于相反效果：将条件反应模式视为物理神经元通路——通过前认知的情绪基调开启短暂调节间隙——我们构建了一个框架：在该间隙中，取代自动重试，人可进行幕后观察：观察神经过程运作，使级联反应不完成，从而通过长时程抑制削弱通路，而非增强。我们通过三层观察与两种应用模式来刻画此实践：一种用户引导模式无需修改现有工具；另一种代理辅助模式中，普通代理经轻度配置以支持间隙中的观察。我们通过生成式图像提示示例说明该框架，展示单次令人沮丧的会话在行为上几乎无异，但在神经层面却截然相反。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that frequent human-AI interactions create repetitive neuroplastic training loops that reinforce negative emotional responses like frustration and self-criticism, the study proposes a framework to reverse this effect by interrupting reactive patterns during the moment of initial emotional response. The method involves identifying a brief regulatory window — a pre-cognitive feeling tone — where users can engage in behind-the-scenes observation to prevent the completion of neural cascades, thereby inducing long-term depression instead of potentiation. Experimental results using generative image prompting show that while behavioral outcomes remain nearly identical whether or not observation is applied, the underlying neural pathways diverge: unobserved sessions strengthen negative response pathways, while observed sessions weaken them.
该研究基于频繁的人机交互会形成重复神经反馈循环并强化负面情绪反应的观察，提出一种将这些交互转化为神经弱化而非强化机会的神经可塑性训练框架。方法是识别由前认知情绪基调触发的短暂调节间隙，在此期间用户进行幕后观察以中断反应模式，诱导长时程抑制而非长时程增强。实验结果表明，尽管在生成式图像提示任务中，无论是否进行观察，行为表现几乎无异，但其神经通路却显著不同：未观察的交互强化负面模式，而观察则削弱这些模式。
AVSCap: Orchestrating Audio-Visual Synergy for Omni-modal Video Captioning
Authors: Yanghai Wang, Jiahao Wang, Jiafu Tang, Yuanxing Zhang, Zhe Cao, Hanyan Bian, Zijie Zhang, Weiliang Luo, Zhiyu Pan, Zixuan Dong, Jiaheng Liu, Zhaoxiang Zhang
First: 2026-07-14T14:34:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T14:34:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Omni-modal video captioning is not merely combining visual captioning with audio transcription: a useful caption must describe how visual actions, speech, music, and sound effects co-evolve. Existing large multimodal models often fail at this relational step, treating audio and visual streams as loosely coupled observations, relying on automatic speech recognition, and under-specifying non-speech sounds and their links to visual events. We present AVSCap, a framework for audio-visual captioning centered on explicit cross-modal event binding. First, we construct AVSCap-130K, a tri-modal training corpus generated by a decoupled-then-fused pipeline that anchors visual and acoustic evidence before composing grounded omni-modal captions. Second, we train AVSCap-7B, a 7B captioner with a two-stage strategy: supervised fine-tuning establishes baseline capabilities, while sample-efficient reinforcement learning uses hybrid rewards to optimize acoustic completeness and audio-visual synergy. Our scaling analysis shows that reinforcement learning brings larger gains than increasing SFT data. Third, we introduce AVSCapBench, a benchmark that decomposes captions into visual, audio, and synergy events and evaluates them with fine-grained event recall. Experiments on AVSCapBench and external benchmarks show that AVSCap-7B improves non-speech audio coverage and cross-modal binding, delivering the best overall performance among evaluated open-source models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AVSCap：面向全模态视频字幕生成的视听协同编排框架
全模态视频字幕生成不仅仅是视觉字幕与音频转录的简单结合：一个有用的字幕必须描述视觉动作、语音、音乐和音效如何协同演化。现有大型多模态模型常在此关系建模环节失败，将音频与视觉流视为松散耦合的观测，依赖自动语音识别，且对非语音声音及其与视觉事件的关联描述不足。我们提出AVSCap框架，以显式跨模态事件绑定为核心。首先，我们构建AVSCap-130K三模态训练语料，通过解耦-融合流水线生成，先锚定视觉与声学证据再生成具象化全模态字幕。其次，我们训练AVSCap-7B模型，采用两阶段策略：监督微调建立基础能力，样本高效的强化学习结合混合奖励优化声学完整性与视听协同。我们的扩展分析表明，强化学习带来的收益优于单纯增加SFT数据量。第三，我们引入AVSCapBench基准，将字幕分解为视觉、音频及协同事件，并以细粒度事件召回率评估。在AVSCapBench及外部基准测试中，AVSCap-7B显著提升非语音音频覆盖与跨模态绑定能力，在评估的开源模型中表现最优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to capture the co-evolution of visual actions, speech, music, and sound effects in video, AVSCap introduces a framework centered on explicit cross-modal event binding. It builds AVSCap-130K, a tri-modal training corpus via a decoupled-then-fused pipeline that anchors visual and acoustic evidence before generating grounded captions. AVSCap-7B, a 7B-parameter model, is trained using supervised fine-tuning followed by sample-efficient reinforcement learning with hybrid rewards to optimize acoustic completeness and audio-visual synergy. Scaling experiments show RL yields greater gains than expanding SFT data, and AVSCapBench evaluations confirm AVSCap-7B achieves superior non-speech audio coverage and cross-modal binding, outperforming other open-source models overall.
为实现准确描述视频中视觉动作、语音、音乐与音效协同演化的字幕，AVSCap提出以显式跨模态事件绑定为核心的框架。其构建了AVSCap-130K三模态训练语料，通过解耦-融合流程先锚定视觉与声学证据再生成接地字幕。AVSCap-7B模型采用监督微调后结合样本高效的强化学习，以混合奖励优化音频完整性与视听协同。实验表明，该模型在AVSCapBench及外部基准测试中显著提升非语音音频覆盖与跨模态绑定能力，综合性能优于其他开源模型。
1D-Bench: A Benchmark for Iterative UI Code Generation with Visual Feedback in Real-World
Authors: Qiao Xu, Yipeng Yu, Chengxiao Feng, Xu Liu
First: 2026-02-20T17:46:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T14:31:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Design-to-code translates high-fidelity UI designs into executable front-end implementations, but progress remains hard to compare due to inconsistent datasets, toolchains, and evaluation protocols. We introduce 1D-Bench, a benchmark grounded in real e-commerce workflows, where each instance provides a reference rendering and an exported intermediate representation that may contain extraction errors. 1D is short for one day, representing the efficient completion of design-to-code tasks in less than one day. Models take both as input, using the intermediate representation as structural cues while being evaluated against the reference rendering, which tests robustness to intermediate representation defects rather than literal adherence. 1D-Bench requires generating an executable React codebase under a fixed toolchain with an explicit component hierarchy, and defines a multi-round setting in which models iteratively apply component-level edits using execution feedback. Experiments on commercial and open-weight multimodal models show that iterative editing generally improves final performance by increasing rendering success and often improving visual similarity. We further conduct a pilot study on post-training with synthetic repair trajectories and reinforcement learning based editing, and observe limited and unstable gains that may stem from sparse terminal rewards and high-variance file-level updates. The data and scripts used in this study are available in an anonymized repository at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/d2c-benchmark-A9C4/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：1D-Bench：面向真实世界视觉反馈的迭代UI代码生成基准
设计到代码将高保真UI设计转化为可执行前端实现，但因数据集、工具链和评估协议不一致，进展难以比较。我们引入1D-Bench基准，基于真实电商工作流，每个实例提供参考渲染和可能含提取错误的中间表示。1D意为一天，代表在不到一天内高效完成设计到代码任务。模型同时输入两者，利用中间表示作为结构线索，但以参考渲染评估，测试其对中间表示缺陷的鲁棒性而非字面遵循。 1D-Bench要求在固定工具链下生成可执行React代码库，明确组件层级，并定义多轮设置，模型通过执行反馈迭代应用组件级编辑。在商业及开源多模态模型上的实验表明，迭代编辑通常提升最终性能，提高渲染成功率并常改善视觉相似度。我们进一步开展合成修复轨迹后训练与基于强化学习的编辑的试点研究，观察到有限且不稳定的收益，可能源于稀疏终端奖励和高方差的文件级更新。本研究使用的数据和脚本已匿名发布于https://anonymous.4open.science/r/d2c-benchmark-A9C4/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of standardized evaluation for design-to-code systems, 1D-Bench introduces a real-world e-commerce benchmark where models must iteratively generate React code using visual feedback and an imperfect intermediate representation. The method requires models to perform multi-round component-level edits under a fixed toolchain, evaluating final output against a reference rendering rather than the intermediate state. Experimental results show iterative editing consistently improves rendering success and visual similarity across commercial and open-weight multimodal models, while post-training with synthetic trajectories or reinforcement learning yields limited and unstable gains, likely due to sparse rewards and high-variance updates.
为解决设计到代码系统缺乏统一评估的问题，1D-Bench构建了一个基于真实电商工作流的基准，要求模型在固定工具链下，利用视觉反馈和存在错误的中间表示进行多轮组件级编辑。评估标准以最终参考渲染为准，而非中间状态。实验表明，迭代编辑显著提升渲染成功率和视觉相似度，而基于合成修复轨迹或强化学习的后训练方法效果有限且不稳定，可能源于奖励稀疏和文件级更新高方差。
Breaking Déjà Vu: Independent Auditing of Visual Place Recognition through Vision-Language Reasoning
Authors: Sania Waheed, Michael Milford, Sarvapali D. Ramchurn, Shoaib Ehsan
First: 2026-07-14T14:30:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T14:30:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visual place recognition (VPR) is a key enabler of accurate localization and long-term autonomous navigation in robotics applications, such as loop closure detection for simultaneous localisation and mapping (SLAM). However, real-world VPR deployment relies on selecting an image matching threshold that balances precision and recall. These thresholds are typically tuned using labeled validation data and fixed during deployment, making them unreliable under environmental changes where ground truth is unavailable. This is particularly problematic in safety-critical robotics, where accepting a false loop closure can corrupt the estimated trajectory and map. In this work, we introduce Visual Place Recognition Auditing, an independent post-retrieval verification framework that leverages Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to assess retrieved matches by reasoning jointly over query and candidate images. Unlike conventional verification methods, our approach performs instance-level verification without requiring architecture-specific confidence measures, dataset-dependent thresholds, or prior knowledge of the deployment environment. We evaluate our method on six benchmark datasets using five state-of-the-art VPR methods and four VLMs. Results show that VLM-based auditing improves recall@1 by 13.6% on average as compared to state-of-the-art methods while reducing false acceptance rates to 12%, maintaining precision above 95% and coverage above 75%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：打破 déjà vu：通过视觉-语言推理对视觉地点识别进行独立审计
视觉地点识别（VPR）是机器人应用中实现精确定位和长期自主导航的关键技术，例如同时定位与地图构建（SLAM）中的闭环检测。然而，实际部署中的VPR依赖于选择一个平衡精确率与召回率的图像匹配阈值，这些阈值通常通过标注验证数据调优并在部署时固定，导致在无地面真值的环境变化下不可靠。这在安全关键型机器人中尤为严重，因为接受错误闭环会破坏估计轨迹和地图。本文提出视觉地点识别审计（Visual Place Recognition Auditing），一种独立的检索后验证框架，利用视觉-语言模型（VLMs）通过联合推理查询图像与候选图像来评估检索结果。与传统验证方法不同，我们的方法无需特定架构的置信度度量、依赖数据集的阈值或部署环境的先验知识，即可进行实例级验证。我们在六个基准数据集上，使用五种最先进的VPR方法和四种VLMs评估该方法。结果表明，基于VLM的审计方法相比现有最先进方法平均提升召回率@1 13.6%，同时将错误接受率降至12%，保持精确率高于95%、覆盖率高于75%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the unreliability of fixed matching thresholds in visual place recognition under environmental changes, this work proposes Visual Place Recognition Auditing, a post-retrieval verification framework that uses Vision-Language Models to independently assess candidate matches by reasoning over query and candidate images. Unlike traditional methods, it performs instance-level verification without needing architecture-specific confidence scores, dataset-dependent thresholds, or prior environment knowledge. Evaluated across six benchmarks with five VPR methods and four VLMs, the approach consistently improves recall@1 by 13.6% on average while reducing false acceptance rates to 12%, maintaining precision above 95% and coverage above 75%.
为了解决现实世界中视觉地点识别（VPR）系统因环境变化导致固定匹配阈值失效的问题，本文提出视觉地点识别审计框架，利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）对检索结果进行独立验证，通过联合推理查询与候选图像进行实例级判断。与传统方法不同，该方法无需依赖特定架构的置信度或环境先验知识。在六个基准数据集上，使用五种VPR方法和四种VLM进行评估，结果显示平均召回率@1提升13.6%，错误接受率降至12%，同时保持精度高于95%、覆盖率高于75%。
Visual Access Boundaries in Vision-Language Model Reasoning
Authors: Hiroto Osaka, Shohei Taniguchi, Gouki Minegishi, Kai Yamashita, Masahiro Suzuki, Yutaka Matsuo
First: 2026-07-14T14:27:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T14:27:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting is widely used as a test-time scaling strategy for Vision-Language Models (VLMs), but it remains unclear what is extended when VLMs generate longer reasoning traces. We ask whether CoT requires continued access to image tokens, or whether it mainly operates over visual information already made available earlier in the forward pass. We introduce Visual Access Sweep, a causal intervention that masks attention from generated-token queries to image-token keys along layer depth and generation time, and define the Visual Access Boundary (VAB) as the minimal access region that preserves task accuracy. Across six model configurations from Qwen2.5-VL and InternVL3, both no-CoT direct answering and CoT prompting exhibit finite VABs. In Qwen2.5-VL-32B and InternVL3 at 14B and 38B scales, when CoT is evaluated against the no-CoT full-access target, its VAB layer differs from the no-CoT boundary by at most two layers, despite substantially longer generations. This suggests that CoT does not primarily improve performance by prolonging direct image-token access throughout the reasoning trace, but by extending language-side computation over image-derived hidden-state information. We further show that CoT gains are constrained by perceptual readout. CoT helps when the queried visual attribute can be reliably read out by the model, but not when that readout is unreliable. A symbolic-attribute oracle shows that CoT can improve counting once ground-truth attributes are supplied as text, while a single-object probe-vs-decode check shows that hard attributes can be linearly recoverable from hidden states yet difficult for the model itself to output. Together, these analyses place the bottleneck at readout rather than counting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉访问边界在视觉-语言模型推理中的作用
思维链（CoT）提示被广泛用作视觉-语言模型（VLMs）的测试时扩展策略，但尚不清楚当VLMs生成更长推理轨迹时，究竟扩展了什么。我们探讨CoT是否需要持续访问图像标记，还是主要在前向传播早期已提供的视觉信息上操作。我们引入视觉访问扫描，这是一种因果干预方法，通过层深度和生成时间逐步屏蔽生成标记查询对图像标记键的注意力，并将视觉访问边界（VAB）定义为保持任务准确性的最小访问区域。在Qwen2.5-VL和InternVL3的六种模型配置中，无论是无CoT直接回答还是CoT提示，均表现出有限的VAB。在Qwen2.5-VL-32B和InternVL3的14B与38B规模下，当CoT与无CoT全访问目标对比时，其VAB层与无CoT边界相差最多两层，尽管生成轨迹显著更长。这表明CoT并非主要通过延长推理轨迹中对图像标记的直接访问来提升性能，而是通过扩展语言侧计算对图像衍生隐藏状态信息的处理。我们进一步表明，CoT的增益受限于感知读出能力：当模型能可靠读出所查询的视觉属性时，CoT有效；否则无效。符号属性预言机显示，当真实属性以文本形式提供时，CoT可提升计数能力；单对象探针与解码对比实验表明，硬属性虽可从隐藏状态线性恢复，但模型自身难以输出。综合分析表明，瓶颈在于读出能力而非计数能力。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether Chain-of-Thought prompting in Vision-Language Models extends performance by maintaining access to image tokens throughout reasoning or by leveraging pre-processed visual information. Using Visual Access Sweep, a causal intervention that progressively masks attention to image tokens during generation, the authors define the Visual Access Boundary as the minimal layer range preserving accuracy. Across multiple model sizes, CoT’s VAB is nearly identical to direct answering’s, differing by at most two layers despite longer generations, indicating CoT’s benefit stems from extended language-side computation over pre-encoded visual features rather than sustained image access. Experimental results further show CoT gains are limited to cases where visual attributes can be reliably read out from hidden states, and fail when readout is unreliable, suggesting the bottleneck lies in perceptual decoding rather than counting ability.
该研究探讨了视觉-语言模型（VLMs）中链式思维（CoT）提示是否通过延长对图像标记的直接访问来提升性能，还是主要依赖早期已提取的视觉信息。通过视觉访问扫描（Visual Access Sweep）这一因果干预方法，屏蔽生成标记与图像标记之间的注意力，定义视觉访问边界（VAB）为维持准确率所需的最小访问区域。在六种模型配置中，CoT与直接回答均存在有限的VAB，且CoT的边界仅比直接回答深两层以内，尽管生成更长。这表明CoT的优势源于语言侧对图像隐状态信息的延展计算，而非持续访问图像标记。此外，CoT收益受限于视觉属性的可读出性：当属性可可靠读出时CoT有效，否则无效。符号属性oracle和探针实验表明，CoT仅在提供真实属性文本时提升计数能力，且硬属性虽可从隐状态线性恢复，但模型难以输出，说明瓶颈在于读出而非计数。
Adaptive Compute in Latent World Models: When Depth Helps, Hurts, or Doesn't Matter
Authors: Achyuthan Sivasankar
First: 2026-07-11T08:27:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-14T14:26:03+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 9 figures, 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Adaptive-compute world models -- early-exit or mixture-of-depths predictors that spend variable depth per step -- assume depth buys better predictions and can be routed adaptively. In autoregressive rollouts, the first assumption requires depth's per-step precision to survive composition. We test this with a pre-registered instrument, the shallow penalty $ρ=\mathrm{err}(\text{shallowest-exit rollout})/\mathrm{err}(\text{full-depth rollout})$, across nine DeepMind Control tasks under matched single-step ($K=1$) and multi-step ($K=4$) training, three seeds each. We find three regimes: on 6/9 tasks depth helps rollouts (intrinsic, $ρ$ up to $4.7\times$), on 2/9 the shallow exits beat the full stack (inversion, $ρ$ down to $0.85\times$), and one is flat. The robust inversion (cheetah) is not a property of the dynamics but is created by training: an ablation supervising early exits only at the first rollout step erases it ($ρ: 0.87\to1.18$, $n=8$, $Δ=+0.31$), while an intrinsic-tradeoff task is unaffected -- a double dissociation we call the routability catch-22, since the supervision that makes exits routable is what trains them to out-roll the full stack. The regime is partly predictable a priori: observation/action dimensionality and one-step model error correlate with $ρ$ at $|\text{Spearman}|\approx0.75$ ($n=9$). Inside a CEM planner, $ρ$'s sign predicts whether planning benefits from depth, most sharply on the inversion task, where shallow planning beats deep. Finally, three cautions: a task's regime depends on the metric space, the rollout horizon, and the encoder. All thresholds and gates were fixed before the compute campaign, including a pre-registered negative for the hypothesis that motivated the study.
中文标题/摘要
标题：潜空间世界模型中的自适应计算：深度何时有益、有害或无关紧要
自适应计算世界模型——即早期退出或深度混合预测器，假设深度能提升预测精度并可被自适应路由。在自回归展开中，该假设要求深度的每步精度在组合后仍能保持。我们使用预注册的指标——浅层惩罚 $ρ=\mathrm{err}(\text{最浅退出展开})/\mathrm{err}(\text{全深度展开})$——在九个DeepMind控制任务中进行测试，每个任务在匹配的单步（$K=1$）和多步（$K=4$）训练下各进行三次种子实验。我们发现三种模式：在6/9个任务中深度有助于展开（内在模式，$ρ$最高达$4.7\times$），在2/9个任务中浅层退出优于全栈（反转模式，$ρ$低至$0.85\times$），一个任务则保持平坦。稳健的反转（猎豹）并非动态特性，而是训练所创造：仅在首次展开步监督早期退出的消融实验消除了该特性（$ρ: 0.87\to1.18$，$n=8$，$Δ=+0.31$），而内在权衡任务则不受影响——我们称之为“路由悖论”，因为使退出可路由的监督正是训练它们超越全栈的关键。该模式部分可预先预测：观测/动作维度与一步模型误差与$ρ$的相关性约为$|\text{斯皮尔曼}|\approx0.75$（$n=9$）。在CEM规划器内，$ρ$的符号可预测规划是否受益于深度，尤其在反转任务中，浅层规划优于深层。最后，三个注意事项：任务模式依赖于度量空间、展开时域和编码器。所有阈值和门控在计算实验前即固定，包括预注册的负向结果，以反驳本研究的初始假设。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether variable-depth adaptive computation in latent world models improves prediction accuracy, testing whether depth consistently enhances rollouts or if shallow exits can outperform full-depth models. Using a pre-registered shallow penalty metric (ρ) across nine DeepMind Control tasks under matched single- and multi-step training, the authors identify three regimes: depth helps on six tasks (ρ up to 4.7x), shallow exits dominate on two (ρ down to 0.85x), and one task is flat. The inversion regime (e.g., cheetah) is training-induced, not inherent to dynamics, as removing early-exit supervision at the first step erases the inversion effect (ρ: 0.87→1.18), while intrinsic-tradeoff tasks remain unaffected — revealing a 'routability catch-22'. The regime is partly predictable via task dimensionality and one-step error (Spearman |r|≈0.75). In CEM planning, ρ’s sign predicts planning benefit: shallow planning outperforms deep on inversion tasks. Crucially, the regime depends on metric space, horizon, and encoder, and all thresholds were pre-registered.
该研究探讨了世界模型中可变深度自适应计算是否能提升预测精度，检验深度是否总是有益或浅层退出是否可能优于全深度推演。通过在九个DeepMind控制任务上使用预注册的浅层惩罚指标ρ（浅层退出与全深度推演误差比），在匹配单步与多步训练下，作者发现三种模式：六个任务中深度有益（ρ最高达4.7倍），两个任务中浅层退出胜出（ρ低至0.85倍），一个任务无差异。反转模式（如猎豹）由训练诱导而非动力学固有，移除第一轮推演的浅层监督后反转效应消失（ρ: 0.87→1.18），而内在权衡任务不受影响——揭示了“可路由性悖论”。模式部分可由任务维度与单步误差预测（斯皮尔曼相关|ρ|≈0.75）。在CEM规划中，ρ符号可预测深度是否有益，尤其在反转任务中浅层规划胜出。研究提醒：模式依赖于度量空间、推演时长与编码器，所有阈值和门限均预先注册。
